+++ b/app/src/main/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/cache/CacheContents.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,99 @@
+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache
+
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time.AccountTime
+import java.time.Instant
+
+/** Every cached row. */
+data class CacheContents(
+    val timeEntries: List<CachedTimeEntry> = emptyList(),
+    val projects: List<CachedProject> = emptyList(),
+    val clients: List<CachedClient> = emptyList(),
+    val tags: List<CachedTag> = emptyList(),
+    val tasks: List<CachedTask> = emptyList(),
+    val favorites: List<CachedFavorite> = emptyList(),
+)
+
+/**
+ * What one refresh read from the server. A null list was not read, because its request failed or
+ * the server does not offer it, and the cache keeps those rows as they are.
+ *
+ * @property history the days of history the refresh covers, or null when the phone does not know
+ *   the account's timezone. Then the refresh reads no entries and prunes none.
+ */
+class ServerSnapshot(
+    val history: HistoryWindow? = null,
+    val timeEntries: List<CachedTimeEntry>? = null,
+    val projects: List<CachedProject>? = null,
+    val clients: List<CachedClient>? = null,
+    val tags: List<CachedTag>? = null,
+    val tasks: List<CachedTask>? = null,
+    val favorites: List<CachedFavorite>? = null,
+)
+
+/**
+ * The cached history: entries that start from [start] up to [end], which is the end of today.
+ * [start] is the start of the day [DAYS] - 1 days before today, so the cache holds today and the
+ * 13 days before it, counted in the account's timezone.
+ */
+data class HistoryWindow(val start: Instant, val end: Instant) {
+
+    companion object {
+        const val DAYS = 14L
+
+        /** The window that ends with today in [time]'s zone. */
+        fun endingToday(time: AccountTime): HistoryWindow {
+            val today = time.today()
+            return HistoryWindow(
+                start = today.minusDays(DAYS - 1).atStartOfDay(time.zone).toInstant(),
+                end = today.plusDays(1).atStartOfDay(time.zone).toInstant(),
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The cache after [server]'s refresh. The server's rows replace the cached ones, and a cached row
+ * the server no longer sends goes, with these exceptions:
+ *
+ * - An entry that starts before the history window is pruned, also when the entries were not read.
+ * - An archived project or a done task stays while a cached entry or favorite uses it, so the
+ *   entry still shows it. The server's values replace it as for any other row.
+ * - A project, task or tag the server no longer sends stays, with the values it had, while a
+ *   cached entry or favorite uses it.
+ * - A list the refresh did not read stays as it was.
+ */
+fun CacheContents.refreshedWith(server: ServerSnapshot): CacheContents {
+    val historyStart = server.history?.start
+    val entries = (server.timeEntries ?: timeEntries)
+        .filter { historyStart == null || it.start >= historyStart }
+        .distinctBy { it.id }
+    val favorites = (server.favorites ?: favorites).distinctBy { it.position }
+    val usedProjects = (entries.mapNotNull { it.projectId } + favorites.mapNotNull { it.projectId }).toSet()
+    val usedTasks = (entries.mapNotNull { it.taskId } + favorites.mapNotNull { it.taskId }).toSet()
+    val usedTags = entries.flatMap { it.tagIds }.toSet()
+    return CacheContents(
+        timeEntries = entries,
+        projects = replaced(projects, server.projects, CachedProject::id, { it.id in usedProjects }) { it.active },
+        clients = server.clients?.distinctBy { it.id } ?: clients,
+        tags = replaced(tags, server.tags, CachedTag::id, { it.id in usedTags }) { true },
+        tasks = replaced(tasks, server.tasks, CachedTask::id, { it.id in usedTasks }) { it.active },
+        favorites = favorites,
+    )
+}
+
+/**
+ * The [server] rows that [keep] accepts or that are [used], and the [cached] rows the server no
+ * longer sends that are [used]. [cached] as it is when the server's list was not read.
+ */
+private fun <T> replaced(
+    cached: List<T>,
+    server: List<T>?,
+    id: (T) -> Long,
+    used: (T) -> Boolean,
+    keep: (T) -> Boolean,
+): List<T> {
+    if (server == null) return cached
+    val fresh = server.distinctBy(id)
+    val sent = fresh.mapTo(HashSet(), id)
+    return fresh.filter { keep(it) || used(it) } + cached.filter { id(it) !in sent && used(it) }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache
+
+import androidx.room.TypeConverter
+import java.time.Instant
+import kotlinx.serialization.builtins.ListSerializer
+import kotlinx.serialization.builtins.serializer
+import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
+
+/** Stores instants as epoch milliseconds and lists as JSON arrays. */
+internal class CacheConverters {
+
+    @TypeConverter
+    fun toInstant(epochMillis: Long?): Instant? = epochMillis?.let(Instant::ofEpochMilli)
+
+    @TypeConverter
+    fun fromInstant(instant: Instant?): Long? = instant?.toEpochMilli()
+
+    @TypeConverter
+    fun toNames(json: String): List<String> = Json.decodeFromString(NAMES, json)
+
+    @TypeConverter
+    fun fromNames(names: List<String>): String = Json.encodeToString(NAMES, names)
+
+    @TypeConverter
+    fun toIds(json: String): List<Long> = Json.decodeFromString(IDS, json)
+
+    @TypeConverter
+    fun fromIds(ids: List<Long>): String = Json.encodeToString(IDS, ids)
+
+    private companion object {
+        val NAMES = ListSerializer(String.serializer())
+        val IDS = ListSerializer(Long.serializer())
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache
+
+import androidx.room.Dao
+import androidx.room.Insert
+import androidx.room.Query
+import androidx.room.Transaction
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
+
+/**
+ * Reads and replaces the cached rows. The flows emit the current rows and again after every
+ * change. [refresh] blocks on disk I/O, so call it off the main thread.
+ */
+@Dao
+abstract class CacheDao {
+
+    /** The newest entry first. */
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM time_entries ORDER BY start DESC, id DESC")
+    abstract fun timeEntries(): Flow<List<CachedTimeEntry>>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM projects ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id")
+    abstract fun projects(): Flow<List<CachedProject>>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM clients ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id")
+    abstract fun clients(): Flow<List<CachedClient>>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM tags ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id")
+    abstract fun tags(): Flow<List<CachedTag>>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE, id")
+    abstract fun tasks(): Flow<List<CachedTask>>
+
+    /** In the user's order. */
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM favorites ORDER BY position")
+    abstract fun favorites(): Flow<List<CachedFavorite>>
+
+    /**
+     * Replaces every row with what [change] makes of the current rows, in one transaction, so a
+     * reader sees the cache before or after the refresh and never part way.
+     */
+    @Transaction
+    open fun refresh(change: (CacheContents) -> CacheContents) {
+        val current = CacheContents(
+            timeEntries = allTimeEntries(),
+            projects = allProjects(),
+            clients = allClients(),
+            tags = allTags(),
+            tasks = allTasks(),
+            favorites = allFavorites(),
+        )
+        val next = change(current)
+        deleteTimeEntries()
+        insertTimeEntries(next.timeEntries)
+        deleteProjects()
+        insertProjects(next.projects)
+        deleteClients()
+        insertClients(next.clients)
+        deleteTags()
+        insertTags(next.tags)
+        deleteTasks()
+        insertTasks(next.tasks)
+        deleteFavorites()
+        insertFavorites(next.favorites)
+    }
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM time_entries")
+    protected abstract fun allTimeEntries(): List<CachedTimeEntry>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM projects")
+    protected abstract fun allProjects(): List<CachedProject>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM clients")
+    protected abstract fun allClients(): List<CachedClient>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM tags")
+    protected abstract fun allTags(): List<CachedTag>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks")
+    protected abstract fun allTasks(): List<CachedTask>
+
+    @Query("SELECT * FROM favorites")
+    protected abstract fun allFavorites(): List<CachedFavorite>
+
+    @Query("DELETE FROM time_entries")
+    protected abstract fun deleteTimeEntries()
+
+    @Query("DELETE FROM projects")
+    protected abstract fun deleteProjects()
+
+    @Query("DELETE FROM clients")
+    protected abstract fun deleteClients()
+
+    @Query("DELETE FROM tags")
+    protected abstract fun deleteTags()
+
+    @Query("DELETE FROM tasks")
+    protected abstract fun deleteTasks()
+
+    @Query("DELETE FROM favorites")
+    protected abstract fun deleteFavorites()
+
+    @Insert
+    protected abstract fun insertTimeEntries(rows: List<CachedTimeEntry>)
+
+    @Insert
+    protected abstract fun insertProjects(rows: List<CachedProject>)
+
+    @Insert
+    protected abstract fun insertClients(rows: List<CachedClient>)
+
+    @Insert
+    protected abstract fun insertTags(rows: List<CachedTag>)
+
+    @Insert
+    protected abstract fun insertTasks(rows: List<CachedTask>)
+
+    @Insert
+    protected abstract fun insertFavorites(rows: List<CachedFavorite>)
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache
+
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.room.Database
+import androidx.room.Room
+import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
+import androidx.room.TypeConverters
+
+/**
+ * The offline cache in the private database file [FILE_NAME]. It stays on the phone, because the
+ * backup and data extraction rules leave out every domain. Room exports each version's schema to
+ * app/schemas, which git tracks, so a later version can migrate from it.
+ */
+@Database(
+    entities = [
+        CachedTimeEntry::class,
+        CachedProject::class,
+        CachedClient::class,
+        CachedTag::class,
+        CachedTask::class,
+        CachedFavorite::class,
+    ],
+    version = 1,
+    exportSchema = true,
+)
+@TypeConverters(CacheConverters::class)
+abstract class CacheDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
+
+    abstract fun cache(): CacheDao
+
+    companion object {
+        const val FILE_NAME = "account_cache.db"
+
+        /** Opens the file on the first query, so call this from any thread. */
+        fun open(context: Context): CacheDatabase =
+            Room.databaseBuilder(context.applicationContext, CacheDatabase::class.java, FILE_NAME).build()
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache
+
+import androidx.room.Entity
+import androidx.room.Index
+import androidx.room.PrimaryKey
+import java.time.Instant
+
+// The rows of the offline cache (docs/PRD.md O2): what the app last loaded from the server. The
+// server is the source of truth, and a refresh replaces these rows with its values. Ids are the
+// server's. The account settings are not here: AccountSettingsStore keeps them, and
+// AccountSettingsSource is the one place to read them.
+

+data class CachedTimeEntry(
+    @PrimaryKey val id: Long,

+    val start: Instant,
+    val stop: Instant?,
+    val billable: Boolean,
+    val tags: List<String>,
+    val tagIds: List<Long>,
+)
+

+    @PrimaryKey val id: Long,

+    val color: String?,

+    val billable: Boolean,
+)
+

+    val workspaceId: Long,
+    val name: String,

+)

+@Entity(tableName = "tags")
+data class CachedTag(
+    @PrimaryKey val id: Long,
+    val workspaceId: Long,
+    val name: String,
+)
+
+/** A task. A done one ([active] false) stays only while a cached entry or favorite uses it. */
+@Entity(tableName = "tasks")
+data class CachedTask(
+    @PrimaryKey val id: Long,
+    val workspaceId: Long,
+    val projectId: Long?,
+    val name: String,
+    val active: Boolean,
+)
+
+/** One of Tokkl's favorites, at [position] in the user's list. Favorites have no server id. */
+@Entity(tableName = "favorites")
+data class CachedFavorite(
+    @PrimaryKey val position: Int,
+    val description: String,
+    val projectId: Long?,
+    val taskId: Long?,

+    val billable: Boolean,

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache

+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiJson
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiResult

+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ServerProject
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ServerTag
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ServerTask
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ServerTimeEntry

+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.Me

+import java.time.Clock
+import java.time.Instant
+import java.time.OffsetDateTime
+import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException

+/**
+ * Reads what a refresh puts in the cache. [me] is the refresh's `GET /api/v9/me` with related
+ * data, which carries the clients, projects, tags and tasks. [preferences] is its
+ * `GET /api/v9/me/preferences`, which carries the favorites, or null when that request failed. On
+ * top of them comes one request, `GET /api/v9/me/time_entries` for the history window, counted in
+ * the account's timezone. A failed request leaves its list null. Without [favorites], as on Toggl
+ * Track, the favorites are an empty list.
+ *
+ * Blocks on the network, so call it off the main thread.
+ */
+fun readServerSnapshot(
+    api: TogglApiClient,
+    me: Me,
+    preferences: MePreferences?,
+    clock: Clock,
+    favorites: Boolean,
+): ServerSnapshot {
+    val history = me.accountSettings()?.time(clock)?.let(HistoryWindow::endingToday)
+    return ServerSnapshot(
+        history = history,
+        timeEntries = history?.let { readTimeEntries(api, it) },
+        projects = me.projects?.map { it.toCached() },
+        clients = me.clients?.map { it.toCached() },
+        tags = me.tags?.map { it.toCached() },
+        tasks = me.tasks?.map { it.toCached() },
+        favorites = if (favorites) favoritesFrom(preferences) else emptyList(),
+    )
+}
+
+/**
+ * Entries that start within [history]. Sends both ends as instants, which Tokkl and Toggl read the
+ * same way, so neither server's idea of the day matters.
+ */
+private fun readTimeEntries(api: TogglApiClient, history: HistoryWindow): List<CachedTimeEntry>? {
+    val url = api.server.api("me", "time_entries").newBuilder()
+        .addQueryParameter("start_date", history.start.toString())
+        .addQueryParameter("end_date", history.end.toString())
+        .build()
+    val entries = api.get(url, ListSerializer(ServerTimeEntry.serializer()))
+    return (entries as? ApiResult.Success)?.value?.mapNotNull { it.toCached() }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Tokkl leaves `favorites` out until the user saves one, which means none. Null when the request
+ * failed or the favorites do not decode.
+ */
+private fun favoritesFrom(preferences: MePreferences?): List<CachedFavorite>? {
+    val body = preferences?.body ?: return null
+    val decoded = try {
+        ApiJson.decodeFromJsonElement(ServerPreferences.serializer(), body)
+    } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
+        // kotlinx.serialization's SerializationException is an IllegalArgumentException.
+        return null
+    }
+    return decoded.favorites.orEmpty().mapIndexed { position, favorite -> favorite.toCached(position) }
+}
+
+/** Null when [ServerTimeEntry.start] is not an RFC 3339 timestamp. */
+private fun ServerTimeEntry.toCached(): CachedTimeEntry? {
+    val start = instant(start) ?: return null
+    return CachedTimeEntry(
+        id = id,
+        workspaceId = workspaceId,
+        projectId = projectId,
+        taskId = taskId,
+        description = description.orEmpty(),
+        start = start,
+        stop = stop?.let(::instant),
+        billable = billable ?: false,
+        tags = tags.orEmpty(),
+        tagIds = tagIds.orEmpty(),
+    )
+}
+
+private fun ServerProject.toCached() = CachedProject(
+    id = id,
+    workspaceId = workspaceId,
+    clientId = clientId,
+    name = name.orEmpty(),
+    color = color,
+    active = active ?: true,
+    billable = billable ?: false,
+)
+
+private fun ServerClient.toCached() = CachedClient(id, workspaceId, name.orEmpty(), archived ?: false)
+
+private fun ServerTag.toCached() = CachedTag(id, workspaceId, name.orEmpty())
+
+private fun ServerTask.toCached() = CachedTask(id, workspaceId, projectId, name.orEmpty(), active ?: true)
+
+private fun ServerFavorite.toCached(position: Int) = CachedFavorite(
+    position = position,
+    description = description.orEmpty(),
+    projectId = projectId,
+    taskId = taskId,
+    tags = tags.orEmpty(),
+    billable = billable ?: false,
+)
+
+/** Tokkl sends `+00:00` and Toggl `Z`; both carry the offset, so no zone is assumed. */
+private fun instant(text: String): Instant? =
+    try {
+        OffsetDateTime.parse(text).toInstant()
+    } catch (e: DateTimeParseException) {
+        null
+    }

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.capabilities
+
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiError
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiResult
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.TogglApiClient
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.Me
+import kotlinx.serialization.json.JsonArray
+
+/**
+ * The Tokkl-only features the server offers (docs/PRD.md section 8.3). Features read these flags
+ * and hide what the server lacks, as on Toggl Track or an older Tokkl. The app never compares
+ * server versions.
+ *
+ * @property trash `GET /api/app/trash` answers with a list.
+ * @property restore undo of a delete, `POST /api/app/time_entries/{id}/restore`. It lives in the
+ *   same router as the trash, so the trash probe decides it too, and nothing probes it.
+ * @property favorites `GET /api/v9/me` carries Tokkl's `role`. On Tokkl a missing `favorites` key
+ *   in `/me/preferences` means no favorites yet, not missing support.
+ * @property timeResolution `GET /api/v9/me` carries `time_resolution_minutes`.
+ */
+data class Capabilities(
+    val trash: Boolean,
+    val restore: Boolean,
+    val favorites: Boolean,
+    val timeResolution: Boolean,
+) {
+
+    /**
+     * The flags from [me] and the [trash] probe. A probe that got no clear answer keeps the
+     * trash and restore flags as they were.
+     */
+    fun updatedWith(me: Me, trash: TrashProbe): Capabilities {
+        val hasTrash = when (trash) {
+            TrashProbe.PRESENT -> true
+            TrashProbe.ABSENT -> false
+            TrashProbe.UNKNOWN -> this.trash
+        }
+        return Capabilities(
+            trash = hasTrash,
+            restore = if (trash == TrashProbe.UNKNOWN) restore else hasTrash,
+            favorites = me.role != null,
+            timeResolution = me.timeResolutionMinutes != null,
+        )
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** Before the first refresh: every Tokkl-only feature hidden. */
+        val NONE = Capabilities(trash = false, restore = false, favorites = false, timeResolution = false)
+    }
+}
+
+/** What `GET /api/app/trash` said about the trash. */
+enum class TrashProbe {
+    /** It answered with a list. */
+    PRESENT,
+
+    /** 404, or a 2xx that is not a list, so the server has no trash the app can read. */
+    ABSENT,
+
+    /** No clear answer: no network, a refused trust check, 403, 429 or another status. */
+    UNKNOWN,
+}
+
+/** Sends `GET /api/app/trash` once. Blocks, so call it off the main thread. */
+fun probeTrash(api: TogglApiClient): TrashProbe =
+    when (val result = api.get(api.server.app("trash"), JsonArray.serializer())) {
+        is ApiResult.Success -> TrashProbe.PRESENT
+        is ApiResult.Failure -> when (val error = result.error) {
+            is ApiError.UnexpectedStatus -> if (error.status == 404) TrashProbe.ABSENT else TrashProbe.UNKNOWN
+            is ApiError.InvalidResponse -> TrashProbe.ABSENT
+            else -> TrashProbe.UNKNOWN
+        }
+    }

new file mode 100644

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.capabilities
+
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.TogglApiClient
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.Me
+import java.io.IOException
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
+
+/**
+ * The server's capabilities for the whole process, and the one place features read them from.
+ * They refresh with each `GET /api/v9/me` of the account settings refresh, and persist.
+ *
+ * @param initial the stored capabilities, or null when none are stored.
+ * @param save writes the capabilities, throwing [IOException] when it cannot.
+ */
+class CapabilitiesSource(initial: Capabilities?, private val save: (Capabilities) -> Unit) {
+
+    private val mutableState = MutableStateFlow(initial ?: Capabilities.NONE)
+
+    /** What the disk holds, so a refresh after a failed write writes again. */
+    private var stored: Capabilities? = initial
+
+    /** The latest capabilities: the stored ones after a restart, [Capabilities.NONE] before any refresh. */
+    val state: StateFlow<Capabilities> = mutableState.asStateFlow()
+
+    /**
+     * Sets the flags from [me], the refresh's `GET /api/v9/me`, and one `GET /api/app/trash`,
+     * and stores them. A failed write keeps the new flags for this process. Blocks on the network
+     * and the disk, so call it off the main thread. Returns the new flags.
+     */
+    @Synchronized
+    fun refresh(api: TogglApiClient, me: Me): Capabilities {
+        val next = mutableState.value.updatedWith(me, probeTrash(api))
+        if (next != stored) {
+            try {
+                save(next)
+                stored = next
+            } catch (e: IOException) {
+                // The next refresh writes them again.
+            }
+        }
+        mutableState.value = next
+        return next
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.capabilities
+
+import android.content.Context
+import android.content.SharedPreferences
+import java.io.IOException
+
+/**
+ * The server's capabilities in their own private preferences file, so features know what to show
+ * before the first refresh after a restart, and offline. The file holds no credential. It stays on
+ * the phone, because the backup and data extraction rules leave out every domain.
+ *
+ * The first call loads the preferences file, so make calls off the main thread.
+ */
+class CapabilitiesStore(context: Context) {
+
+    private val preferences by lazy { context.getSharedPreferences(FILE_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE) }
+
+    /** The stored capabilities, or null when none are stored. */
+    fun load(): Capabilities? {
+        if (!preferences.contains(KEY_TRASH)) return null
+        return Capabilities(
+            trash = preferences.getBoolean(KEY_TRASH, false),
+            restore = preferences.getBoolean(KEY_RESTORE, false),
+            favorites = preferences.getBoolean(KEY_FAVORITES, false),
+            timeResolution = preferences.getBoolean(KEY_TIME_RESOLUTION, false),
+        )
+    }
+
+    /** Replaces the stored capabilities in one write. */
+    @Throws(IOException::class)
+    fun save(capabilities: Capabilities) {
+        commit {
+            putBoolean(KEY_TRASH, capabilities.trash)
+            putBoolean(KEY_RESTORE, capabilities.restore)
+            putBoolean(KEY_FAVORITES, capabilities.favorites)
+            putBoolean(KEY_TIME_RESOLUTION, capabilities.timeResolution)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Throws(IOException::class)
+    fun clear() {
+        commit { clear() }
+    }
+
+    private fun commit(change: SharedPreferences.Editor.() -> Unit) {
+        if (!preferences.edit().apply(change).commit()) throw IOException("Could not write $FILE_NAME")
+    }
+
+    internal companion object {
+        const val FILE_NAME = "capabilities"
+        private const val KEY_TRASH = "trash"
+        private const val KEY_RESTORE = "restore"
+        private const val KEY_FAVORITES = "favorites"
+        private const val KEY_TIME_RESOLUTION = "time_resolution"
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.licences
+
+/**
+ * [text], a licence hard-wrapped at about 75 characters as OFL.txt ships, as the blocks between
+ * its rules of dashes, with each paragraph's lines joined so that it wraps at the screen's width.
+ * The rules themselves are left out; the screen draws them as dividers.
+ *
+ * A line still starts a new line after a blank line, after a heading such as PREAMBLE and before
+ * a numbered condition such as `1)`. Blank lines stay, so paragraphs keep their gaps.
+ */
+internal fun licenceBlocks(text: String): List<String> =
+    splitAtRules(text.replace("\r\n", "\n")).map(::reflow)
+
+/** The blocks of [text] between the lines made only of dashes, without those lines. */
+private fun splitAtRules(text: String): List<String> {
+    val blocks = mutableListOf(mutableListOf<String>())
+    for (line in text.split('\n')) {
+        if (line.isNotEmpty() && line.all { it == '-' }) blocks += mutableListOf<String>() else blocks.last() += line
+    }
+    return blocks.map { it.joinToString("\n") }
+}
+
+/** [block] with each line that continues a paragraph joined to the line before it with a space. */
+private fun reflow(block: String): String {
+    val lines = block.split('\n').map { it.trimEnd() }
+    return buildString {
+        for ((i, line) in lines.withIndex()) {
+            if (i > 0) append(if (continuesParagraph(lines, i)) ' ' else '\n')
+            append(line)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** Whether `lines[i]` goes on from the line before it, in the same paragraph. */
+private fun continuesParagraph(lines: List<String>, i: Int): Boolean {
+    val line = lines[i]
+    val previous = lines[i - 1]
+    if (line.isEmpty() || previous.isEmpty() || NUMBERED_CONDITION.containsMatchIn(line)) return false
+    val previousStartsParagraph = i == 1 || lines[i - 2].isEmpty()
+    return !(previousStartsParagraph && isHeading(previous))
+}
+
+/**
+ * Whether [line], the first line of a paragraph, is a heading such as PREAMBLE or PERMISSION &
+ * CONDITIONS: in capitals, and ending in a letter or a digit. The disclaimer under DISCLAIMER is
+ * in capitals too, but it starts no paragraph of its own, and its first line ends in a comma.
+ */
+private fun isHeading(line: String): Boolean =
+    line.any(Char::isLetter) && line.none(Char::isLowerCase) && line.last().isLetterOrDigit()
+
+/** The start of a numbered condition, such as `1)`. */
+private val NUMBERED_CONDITION = Regex("""^\d+\)""")

+/**
+ * [text] in the body size, with each line made only of dashes drawn as a rule. A line of dashes
+ * set as text wraps onto a second line wherever the screen is narrower than it. Each paragraph's
+ * hard-wrapped lines are joined, so it wraps at the screen's width instead of breaking early
+ * ([licenceBlocks]). Semantics keeps the whole text as it ships, dashes included, as one node,
+ * as the single Text did before.
+ */
+@Composable
+private fun LicenceText(text: String) {

+            Text(text = block, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
         }
     }

+/**
+ * The range of the report: buttons that step it by its own length on either side of a button
+ * that names it and opens the presets and the custom range. As the web app's RangeControl, the
+ * button names the preset the range matches today, when it matches one.
+ *
+ * [selection] is null while there is no range, and then every control is off. [time] gives
+ * today and the week start in the account's timezone.
+ */
+@Composable
+internal fun RangeControl(
+    selection: RangeSelection?,
+    time: AccountTime?,
+    onShift: (Int) -> Unit,
+    onPreset: (RangePreset) -> Unit,
+    onCustom: (ReportRange) -> Unit,
+    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
+) {
+    var presetsOpen by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
+    var customOpen by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
+    val enabled = selection != null && time != null
+    val today = time?.today()
+    val active = selection?.preset
+        ?: if (selection != null && time != null) RangePreset.matching(selection.range, time.today(), time.firstDayOfWeek) else null
+    val words = DateWords(LocalResources.current)
+    val rangeText = if (selection != null && today != null) words.range(selection.range, today) else ""
+    val changeLabel = stringResource(R.string.reports_range_change)
+
+    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
+        IconButton(onClick = { onShift(-1) }, enabled = enabled) {
+            Icon(painterResource(R.drawable.ic_chevron_left), contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.reports_previous_period))
+        }
+        OutlinedButton(
+            onClick = { presetsOpen = true },
+            enabled = enabled,
+            shape = MaterialTheme.shapes.small,
+            modifier = Modifier
+                .weight(1f)
+                .heightIn(min = LocalMinimumInteractiveComponentSize.current)
+                .semantics { onClick(label = changeLabel) { presetsOpen = true; true } },
+        ) {
+            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
+                if (active != null) {
+                    Text(
+                        text = stringResource(PRESET_LABELS.getValue(active)),
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
+                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
+                    )
+                }
+                Text(
+                    text = rangeText,
+                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = if (active == null) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal),
+                    color = if (active == null) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
+                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
+                )
+            }
+        }
+        IconButton(onClick = { onShift(1) }, enabled = enabled) {
+            Icon(painterResource(R.drawable.ic_chevron_right), contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.reports_next_period))
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (presetsOpen && enabled) {
+        PresetDialog(
+            active = active,
+            onPreset = { presetsOpen = false; onPreset(it) },
+            onCustom = { presetsOpen = false; customOpen = true },
+            onDismiss = { presetsOpen = false },
+        )
+    }
+    if (customOpen && selection != null && today != null) {
+        CustomRangeDialog(
+            initial = selection.range,
+            today = today,
+            onApply = { customOpen = false; onCustom(it) },
+            onDismiss = { customOpen = false },
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+/** The presets as radio buttons, which apply on a tap, and a button that opens the custom range. */
+@Composable
+private fun PresetDialog(active: RangePreset?, onPreset: (RangePreset) -> Unit, onCustom: () -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
+    AlertDialog(
+        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
+        title = { Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_range_title), modifier = Modifier.semantics { heading() }) },
+        text = {
+            Column(Modifier.verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())) {
+                // Radio buttons: the selected one differs in shape, not only in colour.
+                Column(Modifier.selectableGroup()) {
+                    for (preset in RangePreset.entries) {
+                        RadioOptionRow(
+                            label = stringResource(PRESET_LABELS.getValue(preset)),
+                            selected = preset == active,
+                            onSelect = { onPreset(preset) },
+                            textStyle = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
+                        )
+                    }
+                }
+                TextButton(onClick = onCustom, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = TokklTheme.spacing.inGroup)) {
+                    Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_custom_range))
+                }
+            }
+        },
+        // One button in the slot, so nothing can overlap at any font scale.
+        confirmButton = { DialogButton(R.string.reports_cancel, onDismiss) },
+    )
+}
+
+/**
+ * Material's date range picker, full screen as Material lays it out, since a dialog leaves its
+ * month grid no room at large font scales. The headline names the range in the screen's words.
+ * Dates go in and out as UTC midnights, the picker's own convention, so no zone of the phone
+ * touches them. Apply is off until the range has both ends and spans at most [MAX_RANGE_DAYS]
+ * days.
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
+@Composable
+private fun CustomRangeDialog(initial: ReportRange, today: LocalDate, onApply: (ReportRange) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
+    val state = rememberDateRangePickerState(
+        initialSelectedStartDateMillis = initial.from.utcMillis(),
+        initialSelectedEndDateMillis = initial.to.utcMillis(),
+    )
+    val start = state.selectedStartDateMillis?.let(::utcDate)
+    val end = state.selectedEndDateMillis?.let(::utcDate)
+    val picked = if (start != null && end != null && !end.isBefore(start)) ReportRange(start, end) else null
+    val tooLong = picked != null && picked.days > MAX_RANGE_DAYS
+    val words = DateWords(LocalResources.current)
+    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss, properties = DialogProperties(usePlatformDefaultWidth = false)) {
+        Surface(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface) {
+            Column {
+                Row(
+                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
+                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = TokklTheme.spacing.space1),
+                ) {
+                    IconButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
+                        Icon(painterResource(R.drawable.ic_close), contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.reports_cancel))
+                    }
+                    Text(
+                        text = stringResource(R.string.reports_custom_range_title),
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
+                        modifier = Modifier
+                            .weight(1f)
+                            .semantics { heading() },
+                    )
+                    TextButton(onClick = { picked?.let(onApply) }, enabled = picked != null && !tooLong) {
+                        Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_apply))
+                    }
+                }
+                DateRangePicker(
+                    state = state,
+                    title = null,
+                    headline = {
+                        Column(
+                            modifier = Modifier.padding(
+                                start = TokklTheme.spacing.space6,
+                                end = TokklTheme.spacing.space3,
+                                bottom = TokklTheme.spacing.space3,
+                            ),
+                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space1),
+                        ) {
+                            Text(
+                                text = when {
+                                    start == null -> stringResource(R.string.reports_custom_range_pick_start)
+                                    picked == null -> stringResource(R.string.reports_custom_range_pick_end, words.shortWithYear(start))
+                                    else -> words.range(picked, today)
+                                },
+                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
+                                modifier = Modifier.semantics { liveRegion = LiveRegionMode.Polite },
+                            )
+                            if (tooLong) {
+                                Text(
+                                    text = pluralStringResource(R.plurals.reports_custom_range_too_long, MAX_RANGE_DAYS, MAX_RANGE_DAYS),
+                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
+                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
+                                )
+                            }
+                        }
+                    },
+                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
+                )
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun DialogButton(@StringRes label: Int, onClick: () -> Unit) {
+    TextButton(onClick = onClick, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
+        Text(stringResource(label), textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
+    }
+}

+private fun LocalDate.utcMillis(): Long = atStartOfDay(ZoneOffset.UTC).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
+
+private fun utcDate(millis: Long): LocalDate = Instant.ofEpochMilli(millis).atOffset(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate()

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports
+
+import android.content.res.Resources
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.R
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time.jsToFixed
+import java.math.BigDecimal
+import java.math.RoundingMode
+import java.text.NumberFormat
+import java.time.LocalDate
+import java.util.Currency
+import java.util.Locale
+
+// How the reports screen writes numbers and dates, after the web app's helpers in
+// ../tokkl/packages/web/src/pages/reports/format.ts, range.ts and lib/settings.ts. Words and
+// date patterns come from string resources.
+
+/**
+ * [part] as a percentage of [whole] with one decimal, as formatPercent(percent(part, whole)):
+ * halves rounded up on the double's exact value, as Intl.NumberFormat does. Zero when [whole] is.
+ */
+internal fun sharePercent(part: Long, whole: Long): String =
+    jsToFixed(if (whole > 0) part.toDouble() / whole * 100 else 0.0, 1)
+
+/**
+ * [cents] of [currency] in [locale]'s currency format with the currency's own decimals, as
+ * formatMoney does with Intl.NumberFormat. A code the phone does not know gives `12.50 XYZ`.
+ */
+internal fun formatMoney(cents: Long, currency: String, locale: Locale): String {
+    val amount = BigDecimal.valueOf(cents, 2)
+    val known = try {
+        Currency.getInstance(currency)
+    } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
+        null
+    }
+    if (known == null) return "${amount.toPlainString()} $currency"
+    val format = NumberFormat.getCurrencyInstance(locale)
+    format.currency = known
+    if (known.defaultFractionDigits >= 0) {
+        format.minimumFractionDigits = known.defaultFractionDigits
+        format.maximumFractionDigits = known.defaultFractionDigits
+    }
+    // Intl rounds halves away from zero; NumberFormat's default rounds them to even.
+    format.roundingMode = RoundingMode.HALF_UP
+    return format.format(amount)
+}
+
+/** Dates in the words of the string resources, such as `13 Oct` and `Mon 13 Oct`. */
+internal class DateWords(private val resources: Resources) {
+    private val weekdays = resources.getStringArray(R.array.reports_weekdays_short)
+    private val months = resources.getStringArray(R.array.reports_months_short)
+
+    fun weekday(date: LocalDate): String = weekdays[date.dayOfWeek.value - 1]
+
+    fun month(date: LocalDate): String = months[date.monthValue - 1]
+
+    /** `13 Oct`, as shortDate. */
+    fun short(date: LocalDate): String = resources.getString(R.string.reports_date, date.dayOfMonth, month(date))
+
+    /** `13 Oct 2026`. */
+    fun shortWithYear(date: LocalDate): String =
+        resources.getString(R.string.reports_date_year, date.dayOfMonth, month(date), date.year)
+
+    /**
+     * The range as formatRange writes it, such as `13 Oct to 19 Oct`. A year shows where the
+     * range spans two years, and on both ends when it lies in another year than [today].
+     */
+    fun range(range: ReportRange, today: LocalDate): String {
+        val sameYear = range.from.year == range.to.year
+        val showYear = !sameYear || range.from.year != today.year
+        fun label(date: LocalDate, withYear: Boolean) = if (withYear) shortWithYear(date) else short(date)
+        if (range.from == range.to) return label(range.from, showYear)
+        return resources.getString(R.string.reports_range, label(range.from, !sameYear), label(range.to, showYear))
+    }
+
+    /** A bar's label: `Mon 13 Oct` for a day, `Week of 13 Oct` for a week, `Oct 2026` for a month. */
+    fun bucket(start: LocalDate, resolution: Resolution): String = when (resolution) {
+        Resolution.DAY -> resources.getString(R.string.reports_bucket_day, weekday(start), short(start))
+        Resolution.WEEK -> resources.getString(R.string.reports_bucket_week, short(start))
+        Resolution.MONTH -> resources.getString(R.string.reports_bucket_month, month(start), start.year)
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * [totalSeconds] in words for TalkBack, such as `2 hours, 30 minutes`, since a screen reader
+ * reads `2:30:00` as a time of day. Zero reads as `no time`.
+ */
+internal fun spokenDuration(resources: Resources, totalSeconds: Long): String {
+    val seconds = totalSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0)
+    val parts = listOf(
+        R.plurals.reports_spoken_hours to seconds / 3600,
+        R.plurals.reports_spoken_minutes to seconds % 3600 / 60,
+        R.plurals.reports_spoken_seconds to seconds % 60,
+    ).filter { (_, count) -> count > 0 }
+        .map { (id, count) -> resources.getQuantityString(id, count.toQuantity(), count) }
+    return if (parts.isEmpty()) resources.getString(R.string.reports_spoken_no_time) else parts.joinToString(", ")
+}
+
+/** Plurals take an Int. A count past Int.MAX_VALUE only picks the plural form; the number is printed in full. */
+private fun Long.toQuantity(): Int = coerceAtMost(Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt()

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports
+
+import java.time.DayOfWeek
+import java.time.LocalDate
+import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit
+import java.time.temporal.TemporalAdjusters
+
+// Ports of the range helpers in ../tokkl/packages/web/src/pages/reports/range.ts and of
+// resolutionFor and timeSeries's buckets in ../tokkl/packages/api/src/services/reports.ts, so a
+// range and its chart cover the same days as in the web app.
+
+/** The longest range the Reports API v3 accepts, in days: Tokkl's TOGGL_MAX_DAYS, as in Toggl. */
+const val MAX_RANGE_DAYS = 366
+
+/**
+ * An inclusive range of calendar days in the account's timezone, as range.ts's DateRange. The
+ * server reads both days in the account's timezone too.
+ */
+data class ReportRange(val from: LocalDate, val to: LocalDate) {
+
+    init {
+        require(!to.isBefore(from)) { "the range ends before it starts" }
+    }
+
+    /** How many days the range covers, both ends included. */
+    val days: Long get() = ChronoUnit.DAYS.between(from, to) + 1
+
+    /** The chart's buckets for this range, as resolutionFor on the server. */
+    val resolution: Resolution
+        get() = when {
+            days <= 62 -> Resolution.DAY
+            days <= 366 -> Resolution.WEEK
+            else -> Resolution.MONTH
+        }
+
+    /**
+     * The range moved by its own length, earlier for a negative [direction]. A range of whole
+     * months moves by calendar months, so last month steps to the month before it; any other
+     * range moves by its number of days. Port of shiftRange.
+     */
+    fun shift(direction: Int): ReportRange {
+        val step = direction.toLong()
+        val months = wholeMonths()
+        if (months > 0) {
+            val start = from.plusMonths(step * months)
+            return ReportRange(start, start.plusMonths(months).minusDays(1))
+        }
+        return ReportRange(from.plusDays(days * step), to.plusDays(days * step))
+    }
+
+    /** The number of calendar months the range covers, or 0 when it does not start and end on month edges. */
+    private fun wholeMonths(): Long {
+        if (from.dayOfMonth != 1 || to != to.with(TemporalAdjusters.lastDayOfMonth())) return 0
+        return (to.year - from.year) * 12L + (to.monthValue - from.monthValue) + 1
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The web app's presets, in its order, without All time: that one needs Tokkl's own
+ * `/api/app/entries/bounds` and spans up to ten years, more than the Reports API v3 accepts.
+ */
+enum class RangePreset {
+    TODAY,
+    YESTERDAY,
+    THIS_WEEK,
+    LAST_WEEK,
+    THIS_MONTH,
+    LAST_MONTH,
+    LAST_30_DAYS,
+    THIS_YEAR,
+    LAST_YEAR,
+    ;
+
+    /** The preset's days around [today], with weeks that start on [firstDayOfWeek]. Port of presetRange. */
+    fun range(today: LocalDate, firstDayOfWeek: DayOfWeek): ReportRange {
+        val weekStart = today.with(TemporalAdjusters.previousOrSame(firstDayOfWeek))
+        val monthStart = today.withDayOfMonth(1)
+        return when (this) {
+            TODAY -> ReportRange(today, today)
+            YESTERDAY -> today.minusDays(1).let { ReportRange(it, it) }
+            THIS_WEEK -> ReportRange(weekStart, weekStart.plusDays(6))
+            LAST_WEEK -> ReportRange(weekStart.minusDays(7), weekStart.minusDays(1))
+            THIS_MONTH -> ReportRange(monthStart, monthStart.plusMonths(1).minusDays(1))
+            LAST_MONTH -> monthStart.minusMonths(1).let { ReportRange(it, monthStart.minusDays(1)) }
+            LAST_30_DAYS -> ReportRange(today.minusDays(29), today)
+            THIS_YEAR -> year(today.year)
+            LAST_YEAR -> year(today.year - 1)
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun year(year: Int) = ReportRange(LocalDate.of(year, 1, 1), LocalDate.of(year, 12, 31))
+
+    companion object {
+        /** The preset whose range is [range] on [today], if any. Port of matchingPreset. */
+        fun matching(range: ReportRange, today: LocalDate, firstDayOfWeek: DayOfWeek): RangePreset? =
+            entries.firstOrNull { it.range(today, firstDayOfWeek) == range }
+    }
+}
+
+/** The length of the chart's buckets. */
+enum class Resolution {
+    DAY,
+    WEEK,
+    MONTH,
+    ;
+
+    /** The first day of the bucket that holds [date]. */
+    fun bucketOf(date: LocalDate, firstDayOfWeek: DayOfWeek): LocalDate = when (this) {
+        DAY -> date
+        WEEK -> date.with(TemporalAdjusters.previousOrSame(firstDayOfWeek))
+        MONTH -> date.withDayOfMonth(1)
+    }
+
+    /** The first day of the bucket after the one starting on [start]. */
+    fun next(start: LocalDate): LocalDate = when (this) {
+        DAY -> start.plusDays(1)
+        WEEK -> start.plusDays(7)
+        MONTH -> start.plusMonths(1)
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports
+
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiError
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiResult
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.TogglApiClient
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.AccountSettings
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time.AccountTime
+import java.time.Clock
+import kotlin.coroutines.CoroutineContext
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
+import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
+import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
+
+/** A range and the preset it came from, null for a custom range or one stepped to. */
+data class RangeSelection(val range: ReportRange, val preset: RangePreset?)
+
+/** What the reports screen shows. No state carries numbers from another range or an earlier answer. */
+sealed interface ReportsState {
+
+    /** The screen is closed, and nothing is loaded or kept. */
+    data object Closed : ReportsState
+
+    /**
+     * The phone cannot turn the account's timezone into dates, so it cannot pick a range.
+     * [timeZone] is the account's zone name, or null when no account settings are stored.
+     */
+    data class NoAccountTime(val timeZone: String?) : ReportsState
+
+    data class Loading(val selection: RangeSelection) : ReportsState
+
+    data class Loaded(val selection: RangeSelection, val report: SummaryReport) : ReportsState
+
+    data class Failed(val selection: RangeSelection, val failure: ReportFailure) : ReportsState
+}
+
+/** The range this state is about, or null when there is none. */
+internal fun ReportsState.selection(): RangeSelection? = when (this) {
+    is ReportsState.Loading -> selection
+    is ReportsState.Loaded -> selection
+    is ReportsState.Failed -> selection
+    ReportsState.Closed, is ReportsState.NoAccountTime -> null
+}
+
+/** Why there is no report. */
+sealed interface ReportFailure {
+
+    /** No answer: the phone is offline, or the server or the proxy in front of it is down. */
+    data object Unreachable : ReportFailure
+
+    /** The server refused the stored API token, or the phone could not read it. */
+    data object AuthFailed : ReportFailure
+
+    /** The app sends nothing to the server until the user answers its trust prompt. */
+    data object TrustRefused : ReportFailure
+
+    /** Any other answer. [status] is the HTTP status, or null when the body was not a report. */
+    data class Unexpected(val status: Int?) : ReportFailure
+}
+
+/**
+ * The reports screen's state. Each time the screen opens and each time the range changes, it
+ * asks the configured server for the report, and it never asks on a timer. Reports live in
+ * memory while the screen is open; [close] drops them, and nothing is written to the phone.
+ * When a request fails, the screen shows why and no numbers.
+ *
+ * Call every function on the main thread.
+ *
+ * @param accountSettings the account's timezone, week start and duration format.
+ * @param client the signed-in account's API client, or null when signed out. Runs on [io].
+ * @param clock where today comes from, in the account's timezone.
+ */
+class ReportsModel(
+    val accountSettings: StateFlow<AccountSettings?>,
+    private val client: () -> TogglApiClient?,
+    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
+    private val io: CoroutineContext,
+    private val clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC(),
+) {
+
+    private val mutableState = MutableStateFlow<ReportsState>(ReportsState.Closed)
+    val state: StateFlow<ReportsState> = mutableState.asStateFlow()
+
+    /** The account's workspace and rounding, read once each time the screen opens. */
+    private var workspace: ReportWorkspace? = null
+
+    private var loading: Job? = null
+
+    /**
+     * Opens the screen on this week. Does nothing while the screen is open, so a configuration
+     * change keeps the report it shows instead of asking the server again.
+     */
+    fun open() {
+        if (state.value == ReportsState.Closed) select(RangePreset.THIS_WEEK)
+    }
+
+    /** Loads [preset]'s range around today in the account's timezone. */
+    fun select(preset: RangePreset) {
+        val time = accountTime() ?: return showNoAccountTime()
+        load(RangeSelection(preset.range(time.today(), time.firstDayOfWeek), preset))
+    }
+
+    /** Loads [range], which may span at most [MAX_RANGE_DAYS] days. */
+    fun selectCustom(range: ReportRange) {
+        require(range.days <= MAX_RANGE_DAYS) { "the Reports API accepts at most $MAX_RANGE_DAYS days" }
+        load(RangeSelection(range, preset = null))
+    }
+
+    /** Loads the range before the current one for a negative [direction], the one after for a positive one. */
+    fun shift(direction: Int) {
+        val current = selection() ?: return
+        load(RangeSelection(current.range.shift(direction), preset = null))
+    }
+
+    /** Asks for the current range again, or for this week when the account's timezone was unknown. */
+    fun retry() {
+        val current = selection()
+        if (current == null) select(RangePreset.THIS_WEEK) else load(current)
+    }
+
+    /** Closes the screen: stops waiting for an answer and forgets the report and the workspace. */
+    fun close() {
+        loading?.cancel()
+        loading = null
+        workspace = null
+        mutableState.value = ReportsState.Closed
+    }
+
+    /** Times in the account's timezone, or null without settings or when the phone cannot resolve the zone. */
+    fun accountTime(): AccountTime? = accountSettings.value?.time(clock)
+
+    private fun selection(): RangeSelection? = state.value.selection()
+
+    private fun showNoAccountTime() {
+        loading?.cancel()
+        mutableState.value = ReportsState.NoAccountTime(accountSettings.value?.timeZone)
+    }
+
+    private fun load(selection: RangeSelection) {
+        loading?.cancel()
+        mutableState.value = ReportsState.Loading(selection)
+        val known = workspace
+        loading = scope.launch {
+            // A cancelled load drops its answer: withContext throws once it returns.
+            val (shown, read) = withContext(io) { fetch(selection, known) }
+            workspace = read
+            mutableState.value = shown
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** What to show for [selection], and the workspace, [known] or read from the server when null. */
+    private fun fetch(selection: RangeSelection, known: ReportWorkspace?): Pair<ReportsState, ReportWorkspace?> {
+        val time = accountTime() ?: return ReportsState.NoAccountTime(accountSettings.value?.timeZone) to known
+        val api = client() ?: return ReportsState.Failed(selection, ReportFailure.AuthFailed) to known
+        val server = ReportsServer(api)
+        val workspace = known ?: when (val answer = server.workspace()) {
+            is ApiResult.Success -> answer.value
+            is ApiResult.Failure -> return ReportsState.Failed(selection, failureOf(answer.error)) to null
+        }
+        val shown = when (val answer = server.entries(workspace, selection.range, time.zone)) {
+            is ApiResult.Success -> ReportsState.Loaded(selection, SummaryReport.of(answer.value, selection.range, time.firstDayOfWeek))
+            is ApiResult.Failure -> ReportsState.Failed(selection, failureOf(answer.error))
+        }
+        return shown to workspace
+    }
+
+    private fun failureOf(error: ApiError): ReportFailure = when (error) {
+        is ApiError.Network -> ReportFailure.Unreachable
+        // A reverse proxy answers these while the Tokkl server behind it is down.
+        is ApiError.UnexpectedStatus -> if (error.status in PROXY_UNREACHABLE) ReportFailure.Unreachable else ReportFailure.Unexpected(error.status)
+        ApiError.AuthFailed -> ReportFailure.AuthFailed
+        is ApiError.TrustRefused -> ReportFailure.TrustRefused
+        is ApiError.RateLimited -> ReportFailure.Unexpected(error.status)
+        is ApiError.HostNotAllowed, is ApiError.InvalidResponse -> ReportFailure.Unexpected(null)
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        /** Bad gateway, service unavailable and gateway timeout. */
+        val PROXY_UNREACHABLE = setOf(502, 503, 504)
+    }
+}

+/**
+ * The reports screen (docs/PRD.md R1 and R5): a tab per report, so far the summary, under the
+ * range they share. Opening the screen asks the server for this week; leaving it forgets the
+ * report, while a configuration change keeps it. Offline, it says that reports need the server
+ * and shows no numbers. The detailed (TKA-0034) and weekly (TKA-0035) reports add their tabs here.
+ */
+@Composable
+fun ReportsScreen(model: ReportsModel, onBack: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
+    val activity = LocalActivity.current
+    DisposableEffect(model) {
+        model.open()
+        onDispose { if (activity?.isChangingConfigurations != true) model.close() }
+    }
+    val state by model.state.collectAsState()
+    val settings by model.accountSettings.collectAsState()
+    val time = remember(settings) { model.accountTime() }
+    ReportsContent(
+        state = state,
+        settings = settings,
+        time = time,
+        onBack = onBack,
+        onShift = model::shift,
+        onPreset = model::select,
+        onCustom = model::selectCustom,
+        onRetry = model::retry,
+        modifier = modifier,
+    )
+}
+
+/** [ReportsScreen] without its model. [time] is null when the phone cannot resolve the account's timezone. */
+@Composable
+internal fun ReportsContent(
+    state: ReportsState,
+    settings: AccountSettings?,
+    time: AccountTime?,
+    onBack: () -> Unit,
+    onShift: (Int) -> Unit,
+    onPreset: (RangePreset) -> Unit,
+    onCustom: (ReportRange) -> Unit,
+    onRetry: () -> Unit,
+    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
+) {
+    Scaffold(modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentWindowInsets = ScrollingScreenInsets) { innerPadding ->
+        Column(
+            modifier = Modifier
+                .fillMaxSize()
+                .padding(innerPadding)
+                .consumeWindowInsets(innerPadding)
+                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
+                .navigationBarsPadding()
+                .screenContent()
+                .padding(vertical = TokklTheme.spacing.space2),
+        ) {
+            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
+                IconButton(onClick = onBack) {
+                    Icon(painterResource(R.drawable.ic_arrow_back), contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.back))
+                }
+                Text(
+                    text = stringResource(R.string.reports),
+                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium,
+                    modifier = Modifier.semantics { heading() },
+                )
+            }
+            Spacer(Modifier.height(TokklTheme.spacing.underTopBar))
+            ReportTabs()
+            Spacer(Modifier.height(TokklTheme.spacing.space4))
+            RangeControl(
+                selection = state.selection(),
+                time = time,
+                onShift = onShift,
+                onPreset = onPreset,
+                onCustom = onCustom,
+            )
+            Spacer(Modifier.height(TokklTheme.spacing.betweenGroups))
+            Body(state, settings, onRetry)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** The report types. Only the summary exists so far. */
+@Composable
+private fun ReportTabs() {
+    PrimaryTabRow(selectedTabIndex = 0, containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background) {
+        Tab(selected = true, onClick = {}, text = { Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_tab_summary)) })
+    }
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun Body(state: ReportsState, settings: AccountSettings?, onRetry: () -> Unit) {
+    when (state) {
+        ReportsState.Closed, is ReportsState.Loading -> Loading()
+        is ReportsState.NoAccountTime -> Problem(
+            title = null,
+            message = if (state.timeZone == null) {
+                stringResource(R.string.reports_no_account_settings)
+            } else {
+                stringResource(R.string.reports_unknown_time_zone, state.timeZone)
+            },
+            onRetry = onRetry,
+        )
+        is ReportsState.Failed -> Failure(state.failure, onRetry)
+        is ReportsState.Loaded -> when {
+            settings == null -> Loading()
+            state.report.totals.entryCount == 0 -> Empty()
+            else -> SummaryContent(state.report, settings)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun Loading() {
+    Row(
+        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
+        modifier = Modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { liveRegion = LiveRegionMode.Polite },
+    ) {
+        CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(24.dp), strokeWidth = 3.dp)
+        Spacer(Modifier.width(TokklTheme.spacing.space3))
+        Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_loading), style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge)
+    }
+}
+
+/** Why there is no report, in words, with no number from any earlier answer. */
+@Composable
+private fun Failure(failure: ReportFailure, onRetry: () -> Unit) {
+    when (failure) {
+        ReportFailure.Unreachable -> Problem(
+            title = stringResource(R.string.reports_offline_title),
+            message = stringResource(R.string.reports_offline_message),
+            onRetry = onRetry,
+        )
+        ReportFailure.AuthFailed -> Problem(title = null, message = stringResource(R.string.reports_error_auth), onRetry = onRetry)
+        ReportFailure.TrustRefused -> Problem(title = null, message = stringResource(R.string.reports_error_trust), onRetry = onRetry)
+        is ReportFailure.Unexpected -> Problem(
+            title = null,
+            message = failure.status?.let { stringResource(R.string.reports_error_status, it) }
+                ?: stringResource(R.string.reports_error_unreadable),
+            onRetry = onRetry,
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun Problem(title: String?, message: String, onRetry: () -> Unit) {
+    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.inGroup)) {
+        Column(Modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { liveRegion = LiveRegionMode.Polite }) {
+            if (title != null) {
+                Text(title, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium, modifier = Modifier.semantics { heading() })
+            }
+            Text(message, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge)
+        }
+        OutlinedButton(onClick = onRetry) { Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_retry)) }
+    }
+}
+
+/** A range without time: what to do instead, as the web app's empty state. */
+@Composable
+private fun Empty() {
+    Column(
+        modifier = Modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {},
+        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.inGroup),
+    ) {
+        Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_empty_title), style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
+        Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_empty_message), style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge)
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports
+
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiError
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiResult
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.TogglApiClient
+import java.time.OffsetDateTime
+import java.time.ZoneId
+import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException
+import kotlinx.serialization.SerialName
+import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
+import kotlinx.serialization.builtins.ListSerializer
+import kotlinx.serialization.json.buildJsonObject
+import kotlinx.serialization.json.put
+
+/**
+ * The workspace a report covers: the account's default workspace, as in the web app, and its
+ * rounding setting. [rounding] is -1 down, 0 to the nearest and 1 up, each entry to
+ * [roundingMinutes], with 0 minutes for no rounding.
+ */
+data class ReportWorkspace(val id: Long, val rounding: Int, val roundingMinutes: Int)
+
+/**
+ * The Reports API v3 calls behind the summary, on the configured server. Every call blocks, so
+ * make them off the main thread. Nothing here stores what the server sends.
+ */
+class ReportsServer(private val api: TogglApiClient) {
+
+    /** The account's default workspace from `GET /api/v9/me`, with its rounding from `GET /api/v9/workspaces/{id}`. */
+    fun workspace(): ApiResult<ReportWorkspace> {
+        val user = when (val answer = api.get(api.server.api("me"), ReportUser.serializer())) {
+            is ApiResult.Success -> answer.value
+            is ApiResult.Failure -> return answer
+        }
+        val id = user.defaultWorkspaceId
+        return when (val answer = api.get(api.server.api("workspaces", id.toString()), WorkspaceRounding.serializer())) {
+            is ApiResult.Success -> ApiResult.Success(ReportWorkspace(id, answer.value.rounding, answer.value.roundingMinutes))
+            is ApiResult.Failure -> answer
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Every entry of [range] in [workspace], oldest first, from the detailed search, with each
+     * entry's day in [zone]. Pages follow the `X-Next-Row-Number` the server sends.
+     *
+     * The request asks for the workspace's rounding. Tokkl's web app always applies it, while the
+     * Reports API rounds only when asked, as in Toggl (Tokkl's docs/PRD.md R8).
+     */
+    fun entries(workspace: ReportWorkspace, range: ReportRange, zone: ZoneId): ApiResult<List<ReportEntry>> {
+        val url = api.server.reports("workspace", workspace.id.toString(), "search", "time_entries")
+        val entries = mutableListOf<ReportEntry>()
+        var firstRow = 1
+        repeat(MAX_PAGES) {
+            val body = buildJsonObject {
+                put("start_date", range.from.toString())
+                put("end_date", range.to.toString())
+                put("rounding", workspace.rounding)
+                put("rounding_minutes", workspace.roundingMinutes)
+                put("order_by", "date")
+                put("order_dir", "ASC")
+                put("page_size", PAGE_SIZE)
+                put("first_row_number", firstRow)
+            }
+            val page = when (val answer = api.sendWithHeaders("POST", url, body, ROWS)) {
+                is ApiResult.Success -> answer.value
+                is ApiResult.Failure -> return answer
+            }
+            for (row in page.value) {
+                try {
+                    entries += row.entries(zone)
+                } catch (e: DateTimeParseException) {
+                    return ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.InvalidResponse(IllegalArgumentException("a time entry's start is not a date and time", e)))
+                }
+            }
+            val next = page.headers[NEXT_ROW_HEADER]?.trim()?.toIntOrNull() ?: return ApiResult.Success(entries)
+            if (next <= firstRow) return ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.InvalidResponse(IllegalArgumentException("the next page does not move forward")))
+            firstRow = next
+        }
+        return ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.InvalidResponse(IllegalArgumentException("more than $MAX_PAGES pages")))
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        /** Tokkl's largest page. */
+        const val PAGE_SIZE = 1000
+
+        /** A year at a thousand entries a page needs far fewer; the cap stops a server that pages forever. */
+        const val MAX_PAGES = 100
+
+        const val NEXT_ROW_HEADER = "X-Next-Row-Number"
+
+        val ROWS = ListSerializer(DetailedRow.serializer())
+    }
+}
+
+@Serializable
+private class ReportUser(@SerialName("default_workspace_id") val defaultWorkspaceId: Long)
+
+/** Toggl and Tokkl send a workspace's rounding as whole numbers. */
+@Serializable
+private class WorkspaceRounding(
+    val rounding: Int = 0,
+    @SerialName("rounding_minutes") val roundingMinutes: Int = 0,
+)
+
+/**
+ * One row of a detailed search. Tokkl sends one time entry per row. A row that holds several, as
+ * Toggl's grouped rows do, counts its amount once, on its first entry.
+ */
+@Serializable
+private class DetailedRow(
+    val billable: Boolean = false,
+    @SerialName("project_id") val projectId: Long? = null,
+    @SerialName("project_name") val projectName: String? = null,
+    @SerialName("project_color") val projectColor: String? = null,
+    @SerialName("billable_amount_in_cents") val billableAmountInCents: Long? = null,
+    val currency: String? = null,
+    @SerialName("time_entries") val timeEntries: List<DetailedTimeEntry> = emptyList(),
+) {
+    /** Throws [DateTimeParseException] for a start that is not an RFC 3339 date and time. */
+    fun entries(zone: ZoneId): List<ReportEntry> = timeEntries.mapIndexed { index, entry ->
+        ReportEntry(
+            seconds = entry.seconds,
+            billable = billable,
+            amountCents = if (index == 0) billableAmountInCents else billableAmountInCents?.let { 0L },
+            currency = currency ?: "",
+            projectId = projectId,
+            projectName = projectName,
+            projectColor = projectColor,
+            date = OffsetDateTime.parse(entry.start).atZoneSameInstant(zone).toLocalDate(),
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+/** [start] is RFC 3339 with an offset, such as `2026-10-18T09:30:00+00:00`. [seconds] is after rounding. */
+@Serializable
+private class DetailedTimeEntry(val seconds: Long, val start: String)
