+/**
+ * The summary of a range with time in it, as the web app's SummaryTab: the totals, the time per
+ * day, week or month, and the time per project. Durations follow the account's format.
+ */
+@Composable
+internal fun SummaryContent(report: SummaryReport, settings: AccountSettings) {
+    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.betweenGroups)) {
+        Totals(report.totals, settings)
+        SeriesChart(report, settings)
+        ProjectBreakdown(report, settings)
+    }
+}
+
+/** A card in Tokkl's frame: surface, the large corners and a 2dp outline. */
+@Composable
+private fun ReportCard(content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit) {
+    Surface(
+        shape = MaterialTheme.shapes.medium,
+        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface,
+        border = BorderStroke(FrameWidth, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline),
+        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
+    ) {
+        Column(
+            modifier = Modifier.padding(TokklTheme.spacing.space4),
+            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space3),
+            content = content,
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun CardHeading(text: String) {
+    Text(text = text, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium, modifier = Modifier.semantics { heading() })
+}
+
+/**
+ * Total time, billable time with its share, the number of entries and the amount in each
+ * currency. Each total wraps onto its own line when the next one does not fit beside it.
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)
+@Composable
+private fun Totals(totals: ReportTotals, settings: AccountSettings) {
+    val resources = LocalResources.current
+    val locale = LocalConfiguration.current.locales[0]
+    ReportCard {
+        FlowRow(
+            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space6),
+            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space4),
+        ) {
+            Total(stringResource(R.string.reports_total_time)) {
+                DurationText(totals.seconds, settings, resources)
+            }
+            Total(stringResource(R.string.reports_billable_time)) {
+                DurationText(totals.billableSeconds, settings, resources)
+                if (totals.seconds > 0) {
+                    Text(
+                        text = stringResource(R.string.reports_share_of_total, sharePercent(totals.billableSeconds, totals.seconds)),
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
+                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
+                    )
+                }
+            }
+            Total(stringResource(R.string.reports_entries)) {
+                Text(NumberFormat.getIntegerInstance(locale).format(totals.entryCount), style = TokklTheme.totalTextStyle)
+            }
+            if (totals.amounts.isNotEmpty()) {
+                Total(stringResource(R.string.reports_amount)) {
+                    for (amount in totals.amounts) {
+                        Text(formatMoney(amount.cents, amount.currency, locale), style = TokklTheme.totalTextStyle)
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** One total, a small-caps label over its value, read by TalkBack as one item. */
+@Composable
+private fun Total(label: String, value: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit) {
+    Column(Modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {}) {
+        Text(text = label.uppercase(), style = TokklTheme.smallCapsLabelTextStyle)
+        value()
+    }
+}
+
+/** A duration in the account's format, which TalkBack reads in words. */
+@Composable
+private fun DurationText(seconds: Long, settings: AccountSettings, resources: Resources) {
+    val spoken = spokenDuration(resources, seconds)
+    Text(
+        text = settings.formatDuration(seconds),
+        style = TokklTheme.totalTextStyle,
+        modifier = Modifier.semantics { contentDescription = spoken },
+    )
+}
+
+/**
+ * The time per bucket as horizontal bars, one row each: the bucket and its total as text above
+ * a bar. Billable time sits at the start as a solid bar and the rest follows in stripes, as in
+ * the web app's chart, so the two differ by texture as well as colour. TalkBack reads the whole
+ * chart as one description that names every bucket's total.
+ */
+@Composable
+private fun SeriesChart(report: SummaryReport, settings: AccountSettings) {
+    val resources = LocalResources.current
+    val words = DateWords(resources)
+    val colours = TokklTheme.extraColors
+    val longest = report.series.maxOfOrNull { it.seconds }?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: 1L
+    val description = chartDescription(report, resources, words)
+    ReportCard {
+        CardHeading(stringResource(CHART_TITLES.getValue(report.resolution)))
+        Column(
+            modifier = Modifier.clearAndSetSemantics { contentDescription = description },
+            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space3),
+        ) {
+            ChartLegend()
+            for (point in report.series) {
+                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space1)) {
+                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom) {
+                        Text(
+                            text = words.bucket(point.start, report.resolution),
+                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
+                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
+                        )
+                        Spacer(Modifier.width(TokklTheme.spacing.space2))
+                        Text(
+                            text = settings.formatDuration(point.seconds),
+                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"),
+                            textAlign = TextAlign.End,
+                        )
+                    }
+                    Bar(point, longest, colours.chartBillable, colours.chartOther, colours.chartOtherWash)
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * "Bar chart of time per day, 2 hours in total." then each bucket and its total, in words. The
+ * total adds up the bars, as the web chart's description does.
+ */
+private fun chartDescription(report: SummaryReport, resources: Resources, words: DateWords): String {
+    val bars = report.series.joinToString(". ") { point ->
+        resources.getString(R.string.reports_chart_bar_description, words.bucket(point.start, report.resolution), spokenDuration(resources, point.seconds))
+    }
+    val total = spokenDuration(resources, report.series.sumOf { it.seconds })
+    return resources.getString(CHART_DESCRIPTIONS.getValue(report.resolution), total, bars)
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun ChartLegend() {
+    val colours = TokklTheme.extraColors
+    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space4)) {
+        LegendKey(stringResource(R.string.reports_billable)) { drawRect(colours.chartBillable) }
+        LegendKey(stringResource(R.string.reports_non_billable)) { stripes(colours.chartOther, colours.chartOtherWash) }
+    }
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun LegendKey(label: String, swatch: DrawScope.() -> Unit) {
+    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
+        Canvas(
+            Modifier
+                .size(12.dp)
+                .clip(MaterialTheme.shapes.extraSmall),
+            onDraw = swatch,
+        )
+        Spacer(Modifier.width(TokklTheme.spacing.space1))
+        Text(label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
+    }
+}
+
+/** A point's bar, as long as its share of the [longest] bucket, on a track as wide as the chart. */
+@Composable
+private fun Bar(point: SeriesPoint, longest: Long, billable: Color, other: Color, wash: Color) {
+    val track = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant
+    Canvas(
+        Modifier
+            .fillMaxWidth()
+            .height(BAR_HEIGHT)
+            .clip(MaterialTheme.shapes.extraSmall),
+    ) {
+        drawRect(track)
+        val billableWidth = size.width * point.billableSeconds / longest
+        val otherSeconds = point.seconds - point.billableSeconds
+        val gap = if (point.billableSeconds > 0 && otherSeconds > 0) SEGMENT_GAP.toPx() else 0f
+        val otherWidth = (size.width * otherSeconds / longest - gap).coerceAtLeast(0f)
+        if (billableWidth > 0) drawRect(billable, size = Size(billableWidth, size.height))
+        if (otherWidth > 0) {
+            val left = billableWidth + gap
+            clipRect(left = left, right = left + otherWidth) { stripes(other, wash) }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** Diagonal stripes of [line] on [wash], as the web chart's pattern for non-billable time. */
+private fun DrawScope.stripes(line: Color, wash: Color) {
+    drawRect(wash)
+    val step = STRIPE_STEP.toPx()
+    val width = STRIPE_WIDTH.toPx()
+    var x = -size.height
+    while (x < size.width) {
+        drawLine(line, Offset(x, size.height), Offset(x + size.height, 0f), strokeWidth = width)
+        x += step
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The time per project, most first: the project, its time, its share of the total and, when
+ * the report has one currency, its amount. Each row is one TalkBack item.
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)
+@Composable
+private fun ProjectBreakdown(report: SummaryReport, settings: AccountSettings) {
+    val resources = LocalResources.current
+    val locale = LocalConfiguration.current.locales[0]
+    val currency = report.currency
+    val colours = TokklTheme.extraColors
+    val muted = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant
+    ReportCard {
+        CardHeading(stringResource(R.string.reports_projects))
+        if (report.totals.amounts.size > 1) {
+            Text(stringResource(R.string.reports_mixed_currencies), style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = muted)
+        }
+        for (project in report.projects) {
+            val spoken = spokenDuration(resources, project.seconds)
+            Column(
+                modifier = Modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {},
+                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space1),
+            ) {
+                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
+                    val dot = projectMark(project.color, colours.projectMix, muted)
+                    Canvas(Modifier.size(DOT_SIZE)) { drawCircle(dot) }
+                    Spacer(Modifier.width(TokklTheme.spacing.space2))
+                    val unnamed = project.title.isBlank()
+                    Text(
+                        text = if (unnamed) stringResource(R.string.reports_without_project) else project.title,
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
+                        color = if (unnamed) muted else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
+                    )
+                }
+                FlowRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(TokklTheme.spacing.space4)) {
+                    Text(
+                        text = settings.formatDuration(project.seconds),
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"),
+                        modifier = Modifier.semantics { contentDescription = spoken },
+                    )
+                    Text(
+                        text = stringResource(R.string.reports_percent, sharePercent(project.seconds, report.totals.seconds)),
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"),
+                        color = muted,
+                    )
+                    if (currency != null && project.amountCents != 0L) {
+                        Text(
+                            text = formatMoney(project.amountCents, currency, locale),
+                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"),
+                        )
+                    }
+                }
+                ShareBar(project.seconds, report.totals.seconds)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/** The project's share as a bar, drawn only; the percentage beside it says the same in text. */
+@Composable
+private fun ShareBar(part: Long, whole: Long) {
+    val track = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant
+    val fill = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
+    Canvas(
+        Modifier
+            .fillMaxWidth()
+            .height(SHARE_HEIGHT)
+            .clip(CircleShape),
+    ) {
+        drawRect(track)
+        if (whole > 0) drawRect(fill, size = Size(size.width * (part.toFloat() / whole).coerceIn(0f, 1f), size.height))
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * A project's dot colour: its hex mixed 60% with [mix] in sRGB, as styles.css does, so it shows
+ * against the card. A project without a colour, or with one that is not `#rrggbb`, takes [fallback].
+ */
+internal fun projectMark(hex: String?, mix: Color, fallback: Color): Color {
+    val colour = hex?.takeIf { HEX.matches(it) }?.let { Color(0xFF000000 or it.substring(1).toLong(16)) } ?: fallback
+    return Color(
+        red = colour.red * 0.6f + mix.red * 0.4f,
+        green = colour.green * 0.6f + mix.green * 0.4f,
+        blue = colour.blue * 0.6f + mix.blue * 0.4f,
+    )
+}
+
+private val HEX = Regex("#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}")
+
+private val BAR_HEIGHT = 12.dp
+private val SEGMENT_GAP = 2.dp
+private val STRIPE_STEP = 6.dp
+private val STRIPE_WIDTH = 3.dp
+private val DOT_SIZE = 12.dp
+private val SHARE_HEIGHT = 6.dp
+
+private val CHART_TITLES = mapOf(
+    Resolution.DAY to R.string.reports_chart_title_day,
+    Resolution.WEEK to R.string.reports_chart_title_week,
+    Resolution.MONTH to R.string.reports_chart_title_month,

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports
+
+import java.time.DayOfWeek
+import java.time.LocalDate
+
+// The summary the web app shows, built from the entries of a Reports API v3 detailed search. The
+// sums port totalsOf, timeSeries and groupEntries in ../tokkl/packages/api/src/services/reports.ts,
+// which build the web app's POST /api/app/report answer from the same entries. Tokkl's v3 totals
+// and summary answers carry no billable time per group, no amounts per currency and no amounts
+// per group, so only the entries give every number the web app shows.
+
+/**
+ * One time entry of a report, after the server rounded it. [date] is the day it starts on in the
+ * account's timezone. [amountCents] is null when no rate applies, as for a non-billable entry.
+ */
+data class ReportEntry(
+    val seconds: Long,
+    val billable: Boolean,
+    val amountCents: Long?,
+    val currency: String,
+    val projectId: Long?,
+    val projectName: String?,
+    val projectColor: String?,
+    val date: LocalDate,
+)
+
+/** The money billed in one [currency], in cents. */
+data class Amount(val currency: String, val cents: Long)
+
+/** [amounts] has one row per currency, in the order the entries first use them. */
+data class ReportTotals(val seconds: Long, val billableSeconds: Long, val entryCount: Int, val amounts: List<Amount>)
+
+/** One bar of the chart: the bucket that starts on [start]. */
+data class SeriesPoint(val start: LocalDate, val seconds: Long, val billableSeconds: Long)
+
+/**
+ * One project's share of the range. [id] is null for the entries without a project, and
+ * [title] is empty when the server sent no name. [amountCents] adds up every currency, as the
+ * server's groups do, so show it only when the report has a single currency.
+ */
+data class ProjectShare(
+    val id: Long?,
+    val title: String,
+    val color: String?,
+    val seconds: Long,
+    val billableSeconds: Long,
+    val amountCents: Long,
+)
+
+/** What the summary tab shows for [range]. [projects] has the most time first. */
+data class SummaryReport(
+    val range: ReportRange,
+    val resolution: Resolution,
+    val totals: ReportTotals,
+    val series: List<SeriesPoint>,
+    val projects: List<ProjectShare>,
+) {
+
+    /** The one currency of the amounts, or null with none or several. Port of reportCurrency. */
+    val currency: String? get() = totals.amounts.singleOrNull()?.currency
+
+    companion object {
+        /**
+         * The summary of [entries], oldest first as the server sorts them, for [range] with weeks
+         * that start on [firstDayOfWeek].
+         */
+        fun of(entries: List<ReportEntry>, range: ReportRange, firstDayOfWeek: DayOfWeek): SummaryReport {
+            val resolution = range.resolution
+            return SummaryReport(
+                range = range,
+                resolution = resolution,
+                totals = totalsOf(entries),
+                series = series(entries, range, resolution, firstDayOfWeek),
+                projects = projects(entries),
+            )
+        }
+
+        private fun totalsOf(entries: List<ReportEntry>): ReportTotals {
+            // LinkedHashMap keeps the order currencies first appear in, as a JavaScript Map does.
+            val amounts = LinkedHashMap<String, Long>()
+            for (entry in entries) {
+                entry.amountCents?.let { amounts[entry.currency] = (amounts[entry.currency] ?: 0) + it }
+            }
+            return ReportTotals(
+                seconds = entries.sumOf { it.seconds },
+                billableSeconds = entries.filter { it.billable }.sumOf { it.seconds },
+                entryCount = entries.size,
+                amounts = amounts.map { (currency, cents) -> Amount(currency, cents) },
+            )
+        }
+
+        /**
+         * A point for every bucket from the one holding the range's first day up to its last day,
+         * empty ones included. An entry outside every bucket counts in none.
+         */
+        private fun series(entries: List<ReportEntry>, range: ReportRange, resolution: Resolution, firstDayOfWeek: DayOfWeek): List<SeriesPoint> {
+            val buckets = LinkedHashMap<LocalDate, LongArray>()
+            var start = resolution.bucketOf(range.from, firstDayOfWeek)
+            while (!start.isAfter(range.to)) {
+                buckets[start] = LongArray(2)
+                start = resolution.next(start)
+            }
+            for (entry in entries) {
+                val bucket = buckets[resolution.bucketOf(entry.date, firstDayOfWeek)] ?: continue
+                bucket[0] += entry.seconds
+                if (entry.billable) bucket[1] += entry.seconds
+            }
+            return buckets.map { (day, sums) -> SeriesPoint(day, seconds = sums[0], billableSeconds = sums[1]) }
+        }
+
+        /** One share per project, in the order of first use, then sorted by time with ties kept in that order. */
+        private fun projects(entries: List<ReportEntry>): List<ProjectShare> =
+            entries.groupBy { it.projectId }
+                .values
+                .map { members ->
+                    val first = members.first()
+                    val totals = totalsOf(members)
+                    ProjectShare(
+                        id = first.projectId,
+                        title = first.projectName ?: "",
+                        color = first.projectColor,
+                        seconds = totals.seconds,
+                        billableSeconds = totals.billableSeconds,
+                        amountCents = totals.amounts.sumOf { it.cents },
+                    )
+                }
+                .sortedByDescending { it.seconds }
+    }
+}

+/**
+ * One `GET /api/v9/me/preferences` answer. A refresh asks for it once and hands it to each
+ * reader: the account settings take [durationFormat], the cache the favorites. Each reader
+ * decodes only its own key from [body], so a malformed key fails that reader alone.
+ */
+class MePreferences(val body: JsonObject) {
+
+    /**
+     * The web app shows durations as improved when `duration_format` is missing or null, and
+     * when it names any format other than classic or decimal. A value that is not a string reads
+     * as improved too.
+     */
+    val durationFormat: DurationFormat
+        get() {
+            val name = (body["duration_format"] as? JsonPrimitive)?.takeIf { it.isString }?.content
+            return DurationFormat.fromTogglName(name)
+        }
+}

 /** Returns [seconds], already whole, as a Long, or null when it is not finite or too large. */
 private fun wholeSeconds(seconds: Double): Long? =
     if (seconds.isFinite() && seconds < LONG_LIMIT) seconds.toLong() else null
+
+/**
+ * The account's `duration_format` preference, which decides how [formatDuration] writes a
+ * duration. Toggl Track and Tokkl name it in `GET /api/v9/me/preferences`.
+ *
+ * @property togglName the name the server sends.
+ */
+enum class DurationFormat(val togglName: String) {
+    /** Hours, minutes and seconds, as `1:05:06` or `0:05:06`. */
+    IMPROVED("improved"),
+
+    /** `1:05:06` from an hour up, then `5:06 min`, then `6 sec`. */
+    CLASSIC("classic"),
+
+    /** Hours to two decimals, as `0.79 h`. */
+    DECIMAL("decimal"),
+    ;
+
+    companion object {
+        /**
+         * The format the server calls [name]. A missing name means improved, as in the web app,
+         * and so does any name other than classic or decimal, because the TypeScript
+         * formatDuration writes every other format the improved way.
+         */
+        fun fromTogglName(name: String?): DurationFormat = when (name) {
+            CLASSIC.togglName -> CLASSIC
+            DECIMAL.togglName -> DECIMAL
+            else -> IMPROVED
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Writes [totalSeconds] the way the web app shows a duration in [format]. A negative duration
+ * shows as zero.
+ *
+ * The arithmetic runs on doubles, as in JavaScript, so a duration from 2^53 seconds up is first
+ * rounded to the nearest double. The decimal format rounds the double's exact value with halves
+ * up, as `toFixed(2)` does, so 3618 seconds give `1.00 h`, and the point is a dot in every locale.
+ */
+fun formatDuration(totalSeconds: Long, format: DurationFormat = DurationFormat.IMPROVED): String {
+    // Math.floor in the TypeScript has nothing to do here, since a Long is already whole.
+    val seconds = if (totalSeconds < 0) 0.0 else totalSeconds.toDouble()
+    if (format == DurationFormat.DECIMAL) return "${jsToFixed(seconds / 3600, 2)} h"
+
+    val h = floor(seconds / 3600)
+    val m = floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)
+    val s = seconds % 60
+    if (format == DurationFormat.CLASSIC) {
+        if (h > 0) return "${whole(h)}:${pad(m)}:${pad(s)}"
+        if (m > 0) return "${whole(m)}:${pad(s)} min"
+        return "${whole(s)} sec"
+    }
+    return "${whole(h)}:${pad(m)}:${pad(s)}"
+}
+
+/**
+ * JavaScript's `String(n)` for a whole [n] below 2^53. The hours of a Long's seconds stay below
+ * that, and there both give the plain digits.
+ */
+private fun whole(n: Double): String = n.toLong().toString()
+
+private fun pad(n: Double): String = whole(n).padStart(2, '0')

 // Helpers that give the ports of Tokkl's TypeScript the JavaScript behaviour where Kotlin's differs.
@@ -26,3 +28,14 @@ internal fun jsRound(x: Double): Double {
     val whole = floor(x)
     return if (x - whole >= 0.5) whole + 1 else whole
 }
+
+/**
+ * JavaScript's `x.toFixed(digits)` for an [x] from 0 up to 1e21, where JavaScript switches to
+ * exponent notation. It rounds the double's exact value with halves up. 1.005 is stored as
+ * 1.00499999999999989..., so it gives `1.00`, where `String.format("%.2f")` gives `1.01`. The point
+ * is a dot in every locale.
+ */
+internal fun jsToFixed(x: Double, digits: Int): String {
+    require(x >= 0 && x < 1e21) { "jsToFixed takes 0 <= x < 1e21, not $x" }
+    return BigDecimal(x).setScale(digits, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).toPlainString()
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui

+import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes

+import androidx.compose.ui.res.painterResource
+import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource

+import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp

+/**
+ * The sizes of [Brand], Tokkl's sm, md and lg in Brand.tsx: the icon's size, the wordmark's
+ * width and the gap between them. Each has its own images, so none is drawn scaled down from a
+ * larger size. res/raw/brand_images_source.txt records where they come from.
+ */
+enum class BrandSize(
+    internal val iconSize: Dp,
+    internal val wordmarkWidth: Dp,
+    internal val gap: Dp,
+    @DrawableRes internal val icon: Int,
+    @DrawableRes internal val wordmark: Int,
+) {
+    /** A header strip. */
+    SMALL(24.dp, 84.dp, TokklSpacing.space2, R.drawable.tokkl_icon_sm, R.drawable.tokkl_wordmark_sm),
+
+    /** Beside a column of navigation. */
+    MEDIUM(30.dp, 130.dp, TokklSpacing.space3, R.drawable.tokkl_icon_md, R.drawable.tokkl_wordmark_md),
+
+    /** On its own above a screen's content, as on sign-in. */
+    LARGE(48.dp, 260.dp, TokklSpacing.space3, R.drawable.tokkl_icon_lg, R.drawable.tokkl_wordmark_lg),
+}
+
+/**
+ * Tokkl's brand, as Tokkl's Brand.tsx draws it: the app icon beside the owner's graffiti
+ * wordmark (docs/PRD.md, Visual direction, Wordmark). TalkBack reads the brand once, as "Tokkl",
+ * the wordmark's description; the icon is decorative. The brand is one semantics node, so a
+ * [modifier] that makes it a heading names it too.
+ *
+ * In dark mode a 1dp halo in `--wordmark-halo` traces the wordmark, because the artwork's navy
+ * outline matches the background there. Light mode draws none. In a row narrower than the
+ * brand, the wordmark shrinks to fit beside the icon.
+ */
+@Composable
+fun Brand(size: BrandSize, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
+    val wordmark = painterResource(size.wordmark)
+    val halo = TokklTheme.extraColors.wordmarkHalo
+    Row(
+        modifier = modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {},
+        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(size.gap),
+        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
+    ) {
+        Image(
+            painter = painterResource(size.icon),
+            contentDescription = null,
+            modifier = Modifier.size(size.iconSize),
+        )
+        Image(
+            painter = wordmark,
+            contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.app_name),
+            modifier = Modifier
+                .weight(1f, fill = false)
+                .width(size.wordmarkWidth)
+                .aspectRatio(WORDMARK_ASPECT_RATIO)
+                .drawBehind { drawHalo(wordmark, halo) },
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Draws [image]'s silhouette in [colour], moved [HALO_WIDTH] in each of the eight directions,
+ * for the image to draw over. Tokkl draws the halo as four chained 1px drop shadows, right,
+ * left, down and up. Each shadows the one before, so together they cover the same eight moves.
+ */
+private fun DrawScope.drawHalo(image: Painter, colour: Color) {
+    if (colour.alpha == 0f) return
+    val step = HALO_WIDTH.toPx()
+    val silhouette = ColorFilter.tint(colour)
+    for (dx in HALO_STEPS) {
+        for (dy in HALO_STEPS) {
+            if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
+            translate(dx * step, dy * step) {
+                with(image) { draw(size, colorFilter = silhouette) }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private val HALO_WIDTH = 1.dp
+private val HALO_STEPS = -1..1
+
+/** Width over height of the wordmark images: Tokkl's crop, 1645x709 px. */
+private const val WORDMARK_ASPECT_RATIO = 1645f / 709f

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui
+
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row

+import androidx.compose.material3.Checkbox
+import androidx.compose.material3.LocalMinimumInteractiveComponentSize
+import androidx.compose.material3.LocalTextStyle
+import androidx.compose.material3.RadioButton
+import androidx.compose.material3.Text
+import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
+import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
+import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
+import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.Role
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.TextStyle
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui.theme.TokklTheme
+
+// The rows of a choice: the control, a gap of the spacing scale's space3 and the label, in a row
+// that fills the width, is at least Material's minimum interactive size tall and takes a tap
+// anywhere across it. The control's own click handler stays null, so TalkBack reads the row as
+// one item with the control's role. The label takes [LocalTextStyle] unless given a style.
+
+/**
+ * One choice of several, with a radio button. Put the rows of one choice in a parent with
+ * `selectableGroup`. The selected button differs in shape, not only in colour.
+ */
+@Composable
+fun RadioOptionRow(
+    label: String,
+    selected: Boolean,
+    onSelect: () -> Unit,
+    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
+    textStyle: TextStyle = LocalTextStyle.current,
+) {
+    OptionRow(
+        label = label,
+        textStyle = textStyle,
+        modifier = modifier,
+        selection = Modifier.selectable(selected = selected, role = Role.RadioButton, onClick = onSelect),
+    ) {
+        RadioButton(selected = selected, onClick = null)
+    }
+}
+
+/** A choice that is on or off, with a checkbox. */
+@Composable
+fun CheckboxOptionRow(
+    label: String,
+    checked: Boolean,
+    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
+    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
+    textStyle: TextStyle = LocalTextStyle.current,
+) {
+    OptionRow(
+        label = label,
+        textStyle = textStyle,
+        modifier = modifier,
+        selection = Modifier.toggleable(value = checked, role = Role.Checkbox, onValueChange = onCheckedChange),
+    ) {
+        Checkbox(checked = checked, onCheckedChange = null)
+    }
+}
+
+/** [selection] makes the row selectable or toggleable, with its role. */
+@Composable
+private fun OptionRow(
+    label: String,
+    textStyle: TextStyle,
+    modifier: Modifier,
+    selection: Modifier,
+    control: @Composable () -> Unit,
+) {
+    Row(
+        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
+        modifier = modifier
+            .fillMaxWidth()
+            .heightIn(min = LocalMinimumInteractiveComponentSize.current)
+            .then(selection),
+    ) {
+        control()
+        Spacer(Modifier.width(TokklTheme.spacing.space3))
+        Text(text = label, style = textStyle)
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui.theme
+
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.RowScope
+import androidx.compose.material3.ColorScheme
+import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
+import androidx.compose.material3.NavigationBarItem
+import androidx.compose.material3.NavigationBarItemColors
+import androidx.compose.material3.Text
+import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
+import androidx.compose.runtime.ReadOnlyComposable
+import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
+
+/** Material 3's alpha for a disabled item. */
+private const val DisabledAlpha = 0.38f
+
+/**
+ * Tokkl's colours for a bottom bar item, replacing Material 3's defaults. The active indicator is
+ * a primary block behind the icon, with an on-primary icon. Material 3 draws the label below the
+ * indicator on the bar, where on-primary text would be unreadable, so the active label takes
+ * primary instead. Tokkl's web puts the label inside the block (`.nav-link.active` in
+ * styles.css). Inactive icons and labels take on-surface-variant.
+ *
+ * The scheme's own roles stay as they are: Material 3 would have painted the indicator in
+ * secondaryContainer, which Tokkl maps to --state-selected.
+ */
+internal fun navigationBarItemColors(scheme: ColorScheme) = NavigationBarItemColors(
+    selectedIconColor = scheme.onPrimary,
+    selectedTextColor = scheme.primary,
+    selectedIndicatorColor = scheme.primary,
+    unselectedIconColor = scheme.onSurfaceVariant,
+    unselectedTextColor = scheme.onSurfaceVariant,
+    disabledIconColor = scheme.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = DisabledAlpha),
+    disabledTextColor = scheme.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = DisabledAlpha),
+)
+
+/**
+ * The weight of an item's label. The active one is bold, 700, so that the weight and the
+ * indicator's shape mark it without colour. An inactive label keeps the weight it inherits.
+ */
+internal fun navigationBarLabelWeight(selected: Boolean): FontWeight? =
+    if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else null
+
+/** What a screen needs to build a bottom bar item from [NavigationBarItem] in Tokkl's colours. */
+object TokklNavigationBarDefaults {
+    /** The colours of [NavigationBarItem] in the current theme. */
+    @Composable
+    @ReadOnlyComposable
+    fun itemColors(): NavigationBarItemColors = navigationBarItemColors(MaterialTheme.colorScheme)
+}
+
+/**
+ * A [NavigationBarItem] in Tokkl's colours, with the active label in bold. Its label is always
+ * shown, so the icon alone never names a destination.
+ */
+@Composable
+fun RowScope.TokklNavigationBarItem(
+    selected: Boolean,
+    onClick: () -> Unit,
+    icon: @Composable () -> Unit,
+    label: String,
+    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
+    enabled: Boolean = true,
+) {
+    NavigationBarItem(
+        selected = selected,
+        onClick = onClick,
+        icon = icon,
+        modifier = modifier,
+        enabled = enabled,
+        label = { Text(label, fontWeight = navigationBarLabelWeight(selected)) },
+        alwaysShowLabel = true,
+        colors = TokklNavigationBarDefaults.itemColors(),
+    )
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui.theme
+
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.WindowInsets
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.WindowInsetsSides
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.only
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.systemBars
+import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
+
+/**
+ * The system bar insets a scrolling screen keeps outside its scroll, as its Scaffold's
+ * `contentWindowInsets`: the status bar and the sides. Text never scrolls under the status bar's
+ * icons. The screen consumes these and puts `navigationBarsPadding` inside the scroll, which then
+ * adds only the navigation bar's height at the bottom. Text scrolls under the transparent bar,
+ * and the last line can still scroll clear of it.
+ */
+val ScrollingScreenInsets: WindowInsets
+    @Composable get() = WindowInsets.systemBars.only(WindowInsetsSides.Top + WindowInsetsSides.Horizontal)

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui.theme
+
+import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
+import androidx.compose.ui.layout.layout
+import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Constraints
+import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
+import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
+
+/**
+ * Tokkl's spacing scale, `--space-1` to `--space-8` in styles.css (docs/PRD.md, Visual
+ * direction, Spacing and type scale), and the rhythm, margin and width built on it. Every gap,
+ * padding and margin on a screen is one of these.
+ */
+object TokklSpacing {
+    val space1: Dp = 4.dp
+    val space2: Dp = 8.dp
+    val space3: Dp = 12.dp
+    val space4: Dp = 16.dp
+    val space5: Dp = 20.dp
+    val space6: Dp = 24.dp
+    val space7: Dp = 32.dp
+    val space8: Dp = 48.dp
+
+    /** Between two groups of content, such as a dialog's message and its details. */
+    val betweenGroups: Dp = space6
+
+    /** Between the items of one group, such as a label and its value. */
+    val inGroup: Dp = space2
+
+    /** Between a screen's top bar and its first content. */
+    val underTopBar: Dp = space4
+
+    /** A screen's margin on both sides on compact widths, phones held upright. */
+    val compactMargin: Dp = space4
+
+    /** A screen's margin on both sides on medium and wider widths. */
+    val mediumMargin: Dp = space6
+
+    /** The widest a screen's single column gets. Wider windows centre it. */
+    val contentMaxWidth: Dp = 600.dp
+
+    /** The screen margin for a screen [width] wide. */
+    fun screenMargin(width: Dp): Dp = if (width < MEDIUM_WIDTH) compactMargin else mediumMargin
+
+    /** Where Material's window size classes start medium widths. */
+    private val MEDIUM_WIDTH = 600.dp
+}
+
+/**
+ * Lays out a screen's single column: [TokklSpacing.screenMargin] on both sides, at most
+ * [TokklSpacing.contentMaxWidth] wide and centred. The margin follows the width the screen
+ * gets. The column takes the whole content width, so a child that fills its width lines up
+ * with the margin. Multi-pane layouts are TKA-0056's.
+ */
+fun Modifier.screenContent(): Modifier = layout { measurable, constraints ->
+    check(constraints.hasBoundedWidth) { "screenContent needs a bounded width" }
+    val available = constraints.maxWidth
+    val margin = TokklSpacing.screenMargin(available.toDp()).roundToPx()
+    val width = (available - 2 * margin).coerceIn(0, TokklSpacing.contentMaxWidth.roundToPx())
+    val placeable = measurable.measure(
+        Constraints(
+            minWidth = width,
+            maxWidth = width,
+            minHeight = constraints.minHeight,
+            maxHeight = constraints.maxHeight,
+        ),
+    )
+    layout(available, placeable.height) {
+        placeable.placeRelative((available - width) / 2, 0)
+    }
+}

diff --git a/app/src/main/res/raw/brand_images_source.txt b/app/src/main/res/raw/brand_images_source.txt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0c82ba0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/src/main/res/raw/brand_images_source.txt
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+Where the brand images in res/drawable-*/tokkl_wordmark_*.png and
+res/drawable-*/tokkl_icon_*.png come from
+
+Sources: the owner's Tokkl artwork in ../tokkl/docs/brand (github.com/e0ipso/tokkl
+at 36bcba9), PNG only:
+  tokkl-wordmark-v3.png, 1774x887 RGBA, SHA-256
+  e3653f852d75bc2786ea5acbef7a3aebd396223c5188a24fde1eaf9195beb7b7
+  tokkl-icon-v4-1024.png, 1024x1024 RGBA, SHA-256
+  874f7ec9ccb91c4c60b426a4aa75827db1ed96833cdfbcd3b7e24edb3efe3aae
+The icon is the launcher icon's source too (res/mipmap-anydpi/ic_launcher.xml). The
+launcher layers carry padding for the adaptive icon mask, so the brand icon is cut
+from the source again.
+
+Crop: Tokkl's WORDMARK_CROP in ../tokkl/scripts/icons.ts, 1645x709+80+91, the
+letters and paint spots inside the source's transparent margin, plus 4 px.
+
+Sizes: ui/Brand.kt draws the icon at 24, 30 or 48 dp beside the wordmark at 84, 130
+or 260 dp wide (sm, md and lg, as in Tokkl's Brand.tsx). Each size has its own
+image in each density bucket, at 1x (mdpi), 1.5x (hdpi), 2x (xhdpi), 3x (xxhdpi)
+and 4x (xxxhdpi), so each size draws from an image made for its width near the
+screen's density, never from a larger size scaled down. Widths in px, wordmark
+then icon:
+  sm  84 126 168 252  336   24 36 48  72  96
+  md 130 195 260 390  520   30 45 60  90 120
+  lg 260 390 520 780 1040   48 72 96 144 192
+The widest, 1040 px, is under the crop's 1645 px, so nothing is upscaled.
+
+Built with ImageMagick 7.1.1-43 with Tokkl's own steps from scripts/icons.ts:
+resize, then reduce to a 256-colour palette with alpha, and strip metadata and
+timestamps. W is the wordmark width and I the icon size in px for one size and
+bucket:
+  magick tokkl-wordmark-v3.png -crop 1645x709+80+91 +repage -resize Wx
+    -colors 256 -strip -define png:exclude-chunks=date,time
+    png:drawable-BUCKET/tokkl_wordmark_SIZE.png
+  magick tokkl-icon-v4-1024.png -resize IxI
+    -colors 256 -strip -define png:exclude-chunks=date,time
+    png:drawable-BUCKET/tokkl_icon_SIZE.png
+The 260 and 520 px wordmarks and the 96 px icon come out byte for byte the same as
+Tokkl's packages/web/public/brand/wordmark-260.png, wordmark-520.png and
+icon-96.png.
+
+This note lives in res/raw because the build accepts only drawable files in
+res/drawable-*.

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.cache
+
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.AccountSettings
+import java.time.Clock
+import java.time.DayOfWeek
+import java.time.Instant
+import java.time.ZoneOffset
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** How a refresh replaces, prunes and keeps the cached rows. */
+class CacheContentsTest {
+
+    /**
+     * 10:30 UTC on 18 October is 23:30 the same day in Auckland (UTC+13 in October). The window
+     * starts at midnight on 5 October there, which is 11:00 UTC on 4 October.
+     */
+    @Test
+    fun historyStartsThirteenDaysBeforeTodayAtMidnightInTheAccountZone() {
+        val window = HistoryWindow.endingToday(time("Pacific/Auckland", NOW))
+
+        assertEquals(Instant.parse("2026-10-04T11:00:00Z"), window.start)
+        assertEquals(Instant.parse("2026-10-18T11:00:00Z"), window.end)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theSameMomentGivesAnotherWindowInAnotherZone() {
+        val window = HistoryWindow.endingToday(time("UTC", NOW))
+
+        assertEquals(Instant.parse("2026-10-05T00:00:00Z"), window.start)
+        assertEquals(Instant.parse("2026-10-19T00:00:00Z"), window.end)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun entriesBeforeTheWindowArePrunedAndTheFirstInstantIsKept() {
+        val window = HistoryWindow.endingToday(time("Pacific/Auckland", NOW))
+        val before = entry(1, start = "2026-10-04T10:59:59Z")
+        val first = entry(2, start = "2026-10-04T11:00:00Z")
+        val today = entry(3, start = "2026-10-18T09:00:00Z")
+
+        val refreshed = CacheContents().refreshedWith(
+            ServerSnapshot(history = window, timeEntries = listOf(before, first, today)),
+        )
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(first, today), refreshed.timeEntries)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun cachedEntriesArePrunedEvenWhenTheEntriesRequestFailed() {
+        val window = HistoryWindow.endingToday(time("Pacific/Auckland", NOW))
+        val old = entry(1, start = "2026-09-30T08:00:00Z")
+        val recent = entry(2, start = "2026-10-10T08:00:00Z")
+
+        val refreshed = CacheContents(timeEntries = listOf(old, recent))
+            .refreshedWith(ServerSnapshot(history = window, timeEntries = null))
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(recent), refreshed.timeEntries)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun withoutAKnownZoneNothingIsPruned() {
+        val old = entry(1, start = "2026-01-01T08:00:00Z")
+
+        val refreshed = CacheContents(timeEntries = listOf(old)).refreshedWith(ServerSnapshot(history = null))
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(old), refreshed.timeEntries)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aRefreshReplacesStaleRowsWithTheServersValues() {
+        val cached = CacheContents(
+            timeEntries = listOf(entry(1, description = "old text"), entry(2, description = "deleted on the server")),
+            projects = listOf(project(10, name = "Old name")),
+            clients = listOf(CachedClient(20, WORKSPACE, "Old client", archived = false), CachedClient(21, WORKSPACE, "Gone", false)),
+            tags = listOf(CachedTag(30, WORKSPACE, "old-tag")),
+            tasks = listOf(CachedTask(40, WORKSPACE, 10, "Old task", active = true)),
+            favorites = listOf(favorite(0, "Old favorite"), favorite(1, "Second")),
+        )
+        val server = ServerSnapshot(
+            history = WINDOW,
+            timeEntries = listOf(entry(1, description = "new text")),
+            projects = listOf(project(10, name = "New name")),
+            clients = listOf(CachedClient(20, WORKSPACE, "New client", archived = true)),
+            tags = listOf(CachedTag(30, WORKSPACE, "new-tag")),
+            tasks = listOf(CachedTask(40, WORKSPACE, 10, "New task", active = true)),
+            favorites = listOf(favorite(0, "New favorite")),
+        )
+
+        val refreshed = cached.refreshedWith(server)
+
+        assertEquals(
+            CacheContents(
+                timeEntries = server.timeEntries!!,
+                projects = server.projects!!,
+                clients = server.clients!!,
+                tags = server.tags!!,
+                tasks = server.tasks!!,
+                favorites = server.favorites!!,
+            ),
+            refreshed,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun listsTheRefreshDidNotReadStayAsTheyWere() {
+        val cached = CacheContents(
+            projects = listOf(project(10)),
+            clients = listOf(CachedClient(20, WORKSPACE, "Client", archived = false)),
+            tags = listOf(CachedTag(30, WORKSPACE, "tag")),
+            tasks = listOf(CachedTask(40, WORKSPACE, 10, "Task", active = true)),
+            favorites = listOf(favorite(0, "Favorite")),
+        )
+
+        assertEquals(cached, cached.refreshedWith(ServerSnapshot(history = WINDOW)))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun archivedProjectsStayWhileAnEntryOrFavoriteUsesThem() {
+        val usedByEntry = project(10, active = false, name = "Archived, on an entry")
+        val usedByFavorite = project(11, active = false, name = "Archived, in a favorite")
+        val unused = project(12, active = false, name = "Archived, unused")
+        val active = project(13)
+
+        val refreshed = CacheContents().refreshedWith(
+            ServerSnapshot(
+                history = WINDOW,
+                timeEntries = listOf(entry(1, projectId = 10)),
+                projects = listOf(usedByEntry, usedByFavorite, unused, active),
+                favorites = listOf(favorite(0, "Favorite", projectId = 11)),
+            ),
+        )
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(usedByEntry, usedByFavorite, active), refreshed.projects)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anArchivedProjectGoesOnceItsLastEntryIsPruned() {
+        val archived = project(10, active = false)
+        val cached = CacheContents(timeEntries = listOf(entry(1, start = "2026-09-01T08:00:00Z", projectId = 10)), projects = listOf(archived))
+
+        val refreshed = cached.refreshedWith(ServerSnapshot(history = WINDOW, timeEntries = emptyList(), projects = listOf(archived)))
+
+        assertEquals(emptyList<CachedProject>(), refreshed.projects)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun doneTasksStayWhileAnEntryUsesThem() {
+        val used = CachedTask(40, WORKSPACE, 10, "Done, on an entry", active = false)
+        val unused = CachedTask(41, WORKSPACE, 10, "Done, unused", active = false)
+
+        val refreshed = CacheContents().refreshedWith(
+            ServerSnapshot(history = WINDOW, timeEntries = listOf(entry(1, taskId = 40)), tasks = listOf(used, unused)),
+        )
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(used), refreshed.tasks)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowsTheServerNoLongerSendsStayWhileACachedEntryUsesThem() {
+        val usedTag = CachedTag(30, WORKSPACE, "used")
+        val unusedTag = CachedTag(31, WORKSPACE, "unused")
+        val usedProject = project(10, name = "Used")
+        val unusedProject = project(11, name = "Unused")
+        val cached = CacheContents(projects = listOf(usedProject, unusedProject), tags = listOf(usedTag, unusedTag))
+        val fresh = CachedTag(32, WORKSPACE, "fresh")
+
+        val refreshed = cached.refreshedWith(
+            ServerSnapshot(
+                history = WINDOW,
+                timeEntries = listOf(entry(1, projectId = 10, tagIds = listOf(30L))),
+                projects = emptyList(),
+                tags = listOf(fresh),
+            ),
+        )
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(usedProject), refreshed.projects)
+        assertEquals(listOf(fresh, usedTag), refreshed.tags)
+    }
+
+    private fun time(zone: String, now: Instant) =
+        AccountSettings(zone, DayOfWeek.MONDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = null).time(Clock.fixed(now, ZoneOffset.UTC))!!
+
+    private fun entry(
+        id: Long,
+        start: String = "2026-10-15T08:00:00Z",
+        description: String = "",
+        projectId: Long? = null,
+        taskId: Long? = null,
+        tagIds: List<Long> = emptyList(),
+    ) = CachedTimeEntry(
+        id = id,
+        workspaceId = WORKSPACE,
+        projectId = projectId,
+        taskId = taskId,
+        description = description,
+        start = Instant.parse(start),
+        stop = Instant.parse(start).plusSeconds(600),
+        billable = false,
+        tags = emptyList(),
+        tagIds = tagIds,
+    )
+
+    private fun project(id: Long, name: String = "Project $id", active: Boolean = true) =
+        CachedProject(id, WORKSPACE, clientId = null, name = name, color = "#0b83d9", active = active, billable = false)
+
+    private fun favorite(position: Int, description: String, projectId: Long? = null) =
+        CachedFavorite(position, description, projectId, taskId = null, tags = emptyList(), billable = false)
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val WORKSPACE = 1L
+        val NOW: Instant = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:30:00Z")
+        val WINDOW = HistoryWindow(Instant.parse("2026-10-05T00:00:00Z"), Instant.parse("2026-10-19T00:00:00Z"))
+    }
+}

+    @Test
+    fun entriesAreAskedForTheWindowInTheAccountZoneAndParsed() {
+        answers["/api/v9/me/time_entries"] = json(
+            """[{"id":1,"workspace_id":7,"project_id":3,"task_id":null,"description":"Stopped","start":"2026-10-17T09:00:00+00:00",
+               "stop":"2026-10-17T10:00:00+00:00","duration":3600,"billable":true,"tags":["a"],"tag_ids":[5]},
+              {"id":2,"workspace_id":7,"description":null,"start":"2026-10-18T10:00:00Z","stop":null,"duration":-1,"tags":null}]""",
+        )
+        val preferences = preferences("""{"date_format":"YYYY-MM-DD"}""")
+
+        val snapshot = readServerSnapshot(client, me("Pacific/Auckland"), preferences, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = true)
+
+        val request = recorded("/api/v9/me/time_entries")
+        assertEquals("2026-10-04T11:00:00Z", request.url.queryParameter("start_date"))
+        assertEquals("2026-10-18T11:00:00Z", request.url.queryParameter("end_date"))
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CachedTimeEntry(
+                    1, 7, 3, null, "Stopped", Instant.parse("2026-10-17T09:00:00Z"), Instant.parse("2026-10-17T10:00:00Z"),
+                    billable = true, tags = listOf("a"), tagIds = listOf(5L),
+                ),
+                CachedTimeEntry(
+                    2, 7, null, null, "", Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:00:00Z"), stop = null,
+                    billable = false, tags = emptyList(), tagIds = emptyList(),
+                ),
+            ),
+            snapshot.timeEntries,
+        )
+    }
+
+    /** Tokkl leaves `favorites` out of /me/preferences until the user saves one. */
+    @Test
+    fun preferencesWithoutFavoritesMeanNoFavorites() {
+        answers["/api/v9/me/time_entries"] = json("[]")
+        val preferences = preferences("""{"date_format":"YYYY-MM-DD"}""")
+
+        val snapshot = readServerSnapshot(client, me("UTC"), preferences, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = true)
+
+        assertEquals(emptyList<CachedFavorite>(), snapshot.favorites)
+    }
+
+    /** The favorites decode on their own, so a broken key leaves the duration format readable. */
+    @Test
+    fun favoritesThatDoNotDecodeAreUnreadAlone() {
+        answers["/api/v9/me/time_entries"] = json("[]")
+        val preferences = preferences("""{"duration_format":"classic","favorites":"broken"}""")
+
+        val snapshot = readServerSnapshot(client, me("UTC"), preferences, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = true)
+
+        assertNull(snapshot.favorites)
+        assertEquals(DurationFormat.CLASSIC, preferences.durationFormat)
+    }
+
+    /** Toggl Track has no favorites, so the snapshot keeps none, whatever the preferences hold. */
+    @Test
+    fun withoutFavoritesNoneAreKept() {
+        answers["/api/v9/me/time_entries"] = json("[]")
+        val preferences = preferences("""{"favorites":[{"description":"Standup"}]}""")
+
+        val snapshot = readServerSnapshot(client, me("UTC"), preferences, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = false)
+
+        assertEquals(emptyList<CachedFavorite>(), snapshot.favorites)
+        assertEquals(listOf("/api/v9/me/time_entries"), List(server.requestCount) { server.takeRequest().url.encodedPath })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun failedRequestsLeaveTheirListsUnread() {
+        val snapshot = readServerSnapshot(client, me("UTC"), preferences = null, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = true)
+
+        assertNull(snapshot.timeEntries)
+        assertNull(snapshot.favorites)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anUnknownZoneReadsNoEntries() {
+        val preferences = preferences("{}")
+
+        val snapshot = readServerSnapshot(client, me("Mars/Olympus_Mons"), preferences, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = true)
+
+        assertNull(snapshot.history)
+        assertNull(snapshot.timeEntries)
+        assertEquals("nothing was asked for", 0, server.requestCount)
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Wired as TokklApplication wires a refresh, minus the Room write and the trash probe. The
+     * settings take the duration format and the cache the favorites, from one request.
+     */
+    @Test
+    fun aTokklRefreshAsksForThePreferencesOnce() {
+        answers["/api/v9/me"] = json("""{"id":1,"timezone":"UTC","beginning_of_week":1,"role":"user"}""")
+        answers["/api/v9/me/time_entries"] = json("[]")
+        answers["/api/v9/me/preferences"] = json("""{"duration_format":"decimal","favorites":[{"description":"Standup","billable":true}]}""")
+        var snapshot: ServerSnapshot? = null
+        val settings = AccountSettingsSource(
+            initial = null,
+            save = {},
+            client = { client },
+            scope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Unconfined),
+            io = Dispatchers.Unconfined,
+            onRefresh = { api, me, preferences ->
+                snapshot = readServerSnapshot(api, me, preferences, Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC), favorites = true)
+            },
+        )
+
+        settings.refresh()
+
+        val paths = List(server.requestCount) { server.takeRequest().url.encodedPath }
+        assertEquals(paths.toString(), 1, paths.count { it == "/api/v9/me/preferences" })
+        assertEquals(DurationFormat.DECIMAL, settings.state.value?.durationFormat)
+        val standup = CachedFavorite(0, "Standup", projectId = null, taskId = null, tags = emptyList(), billable = true)
+        assertEquals(listOf(standup), snapshot?.favorites)
+    }
+
+    private fun recorded(path: String): RecordedRequest {
+        repeat(server.requestCount) {
+            val request = server.takeRequest()
+            if (request.url.encodedPath == path) return request
+        }
+        throw AssertionError("no request to $path")
+    }
+
+    private fun me(zone: String): Me =
+        Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }.decodeFromString(
+            Me.serializer(),
+            """{"id":1,"timezone":"$zone","beginning_of_week":1}""",
+        )
+
+    private fun preferences(body: String) = MePreferences(Json.parseToJsonElement(body).jsonObject)
+
+    private fun json(body: String) = MockResponse.Builder()
+        .code(200)
+        .addHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
+        .body(body)
+        .build()
+
+    private companion object {
+        val NOW: Instant = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:30:00Z")
+    }
+}

+        assertEquals("no favorites yet", emptyList<CachedFavorite>(), snapshot.favorites)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun allFlagsAreTrueOnTokkl() {
+        answers[TRASH] = json("[]")
+
+        val capabilities = source().refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME))
+
+        assertEquals(ALL, capabilities)
+        assertEquals("the flags are stored", listOf(ALL), saved)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun trashAndRestoreAreFalseWhenTheTrashAnswers404() {
+        val capabilities = source(initial = ALL).refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME))
+
+        assertFalse(capabilities.trash)
+        assertFalse(capabilities.restore)
+        assertEquals(listOf(ALL.copy(trash = false, restore = false)), saved)
+    }
+
+    /** Such as a proxy's HTML page in front of a server without the trash. */
+    @Test
+    fun aTrashAnswerThatIsNotAListHidesTrashAndRestore() {
+        answers[TRASH] = MockResponse.Builder().code(200).addHeader("Content-Type", "text/html").body("<html></html>").build()
+
+        val capabilities = source(initial = ALL).refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME))
+
+        assertFalse(capabilities.trash)
+        assertFalse(capabilities.restore)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aProbeWithoutAClearAnswerKeepsTheTrashFlags() {
+        answers[TRASH] = MockResponse.Builder().code(503).build()
+
+        val capabilities = source(initial = ALL).refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME))
+
+        assertEquals(ALL, capabilities)
+        assertEquals("nothing changed, so nothing was written", emptyList<Capabilities>(), saved)
+    }
+
+    /** The probe never makes up an entry id to try the restore endpoint. */
+    @Test
+    fun theOnlyProbeIsOneGetOfTheTrash() {
+        answers[TRASH] = json("[]")
+
+        source().refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME))
+
+        assertEquals(1, server.requestCount)
+        val request = server.takeRequest()
+        assertEquals("GET", request.method)
+        assertEquals(TRASH, request.url.encodedPath)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aFailedWriteKeepsTheNewFlagsAndTheNextRefreshWritesAgain() {
+        answers[TRASH] = json("[]")
+        var failWrites = true
+        val source = CapabilitiesSource(initial = null) {
+            if (failWrites) throw IOException("disk full")
+            saved += it
+        }
+
+        assertEquals(ALL, source.refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME)))
+        assertEquals(ALL, source.state.value)
+        failWrites = false
+        source.refresh(client, me(TOKKL_ME))
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(ALL), saved)
+    }
+
+    private fun source(initial: Capabilities? = null) = CapabilitiesSource(initial) { saved += it }
+
+    private fun me(body: String): Me = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }.decodeFromString(Me.serializer(), body)
+
+    private fun json(body: String) = MockResponse.Builder()
+        .code(200)
+        .addHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
+        .body(body)
+        .build()
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val TRASH = "/api/app/trash"
+        const val TOKKL_ME = """{"id":1,"timezone":"UTC","beginning_of_week":1,"role":"user","time_resolution_minutes":5}"""
+        const val TOGGL_ME = """{"id":1,"timezone":"UTC","beginning_of_week":1}"""
+        val ALL = Capabilities(trash = true, restore = true, favorites = true, timeResolution = true)
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.licences
+
+import java.io.File
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** [licenceBlocks] over the OFL the app ships, res/raw/anybody_ofl.txt. */
+class LicenceReflowTest {
+
+    /** Gradle runs unit tests in the app module's directory. */
+    private val shipped = File("src/main/res/raw/anybody_ofl.txt").readText()
+
+    private val blocks = licenceBlocks(shipped)
+
+    private val lines = blocks.flatMap { it.split('\n') }
+
+    @Test
+    fun keepsTheThreeBlocksBetweenTheRules() {
+        assertEquals(3, blocks.size)
+        assertEquals("SIL OPEN FONT LICENSE Version 1.1 - 26 February 2007", blocks[1])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun keepsEveryWordInOrder() {
+        val rule = Regex("""^-+$""")
+        val source = shipped.lines().filterNot { rule.matches(it) }.flatMap(::words)
+        assertEquals(source, blocks.flatMap(::words))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun keepsTheHeadingsOnTheirOwnLines() {
+        val headings = listOf("PREAMBLE", "DEFINITIONS", "PERMISSION & CONDITIONS", "TERMINATION", "DISCLAIMER")
+        for (heading in headings) {
+            val at = lines.indexOf(heading)
+            assertTrue("$heading is not a line of its own", at >= 0)
+            assertTrue("no text follows $heading on the next line", lines[at + 1].isNotBlank())
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun startsEachNumberedConditionOnItsOwnLine() {
+        val conditions = lines.filter { Regex("""^\d+\)""").containsMatchIn(it) }
+        assertEquals(listOf("1)", "2)", "3)", "4)", "5)"), conditions.map { it.substringBefore(' ') })
+        assertEquals(
+            "1) Neither the Font Software nor any of its individual components, " +
+                "in Original or Modified Versions, may be sold by itself.",
+            conditions.first(),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun joinsTheLinesOfEachParagraph() {
+        assertTrue(
+            lines.contains(
+                "The goals of the Open Font License (OFL) are to stimulate worldwide development of " +
+                    "collaborative font projects, to support the font creation efforts of academic and " +
+                    "linguistic communities, and to provide a free and open framework in which fonts " +
+                    "may be shared and improved in partnership with others.",
+            ),
+        )
+        // The disclaimer is in capitals, like a heading, but stays one paragraph under its heading.
+        val disclaimer = lines[lines.indexOf("DISCLAIMER") + 1]
+        assertTrue(disclaimer.startsWith("THE FONT SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED \"AS IS\", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS"))
+        assertTrue(disclaimer.endsWith("OTHER DEALINGS IN THE FONT SOFTWARE."))
+    }
+
+    /** A line ends before the next one only at a paragraph's end, after a heading or before a condition. */
+    @Test
+    fun breaksALineOnlyAtAParagraphEndAHeadingOrACondition() {
+        for (block in blocks) {
+            val blockLines = block.split('\n')
+            for ((previous, line) in blockLines.zipWithNext()) {
+                if (previous.isEmpty() || line.isEmpty()) continue
+                val afterHeading = previous.none(Char::isLowerCase) && previous.last().isLetterOrDigit()
+                val beforeCondition = Regex("""^\d+\)""").containsMatchIn(line)
+                assertTrue("\"$previous\" breaks before \"$line\"", afterHeading || beforeCondition)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun joinsWithOneSpaceAndKeepsNoTrailingSpace() {
+        for (line in lines) {
+            assertTrue("a double space in \"$line\"", "  " !in line)
+            assertEquals("a trailing space in \"$line\"", line.trimEnd(), line)
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun words(text: String): List<String> = text.split(Regex("""\s+""")).filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session
+
+import android.content.ContextWrapper
+import android.content.SharedPreferences
+import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time.DurationFormat
+import java.time.DayOfWeek
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** The store over an in-memory preferences file, since the JVM tests have no Android runtime. */
+class AccountSettingsStoreTest {
+
+    private val file = InMemoryPreferences()
+
+    // The unit test android.jar keeps constructors working, and the store asks the context for
+    // nothing but its preferences file.
+    private val store = AccountSettingsStore(
+        object : ContextWrapper(null) {
+            override fun getSharedPreferences(name: String, mode: Int): SharedPreferences {
+                assertEquals(AccountSettingsStore.FILE_NAME, name)
+                return file
+            }
+        },
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachDurationFormatReadsBackAsSaved() {
+        for (format in DurationFormat.entries) {
+            val settings = AccountSettings("Europe/Madrid", DayOfWeek.MONDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = 15.0, format)
+
+            store.save(settings)
+
+            assertEquals(format.togglName, file.all["duration_format"])
+            assertEquals(settings, store.load())
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun settingsStoredWithoutADurationFormatReadAsImproved() {
+        // What an app version from before the duration format left in the file.
+        file.edit().putString("timezone", "Europe/Madrid").putInt("beginning_of_week", 1).commit()
+
+        assertEquals(AccountSettings("Europe/Madrid", DayOfWeek.MONDAY, null), store.load())
+        assertEquals(DurationFormat.IMPROVED, store.load()!!.durationFormat)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearRemovesTheDurationFormat() {
+        store.save(AccountSettings("UTC", DayOfWeek.SUNDAY, null, DurationFormat.DECIMAL))
+
+        store.clear()
+
+        assertTrue("the file is empty", file.all.isEmpty())
+        assertNull(store.load())
+    }
+}
+
+/** A preferences file in memory. A commit clears first, then applies the edits, as Android does. */
+private class InMemoryPreferences : SharedPreferences {
+
+    private val values = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
+
+    override fun getAll(): Map<String, *> = values.toMap()
+
+    override fun getString(key: String, defValue: String?): String? = values[key] as String? ?: defValue
+
+    @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
+    override fun getStringSet(key: String, defValues: Set<String>?): Set<String>? =
+        values[key] as Set<String>? ?: defValues
+
+    override fun getInt(key: String, defValue: Int): Int = values[key] as Int? ?: defValue
+
+    override fun getLong(key: String, defValue: Long): Long = values[key] as Long? ?: defValue
+
+    override fun getFloat(key: String, defValue: Float): Float = values[key] as Float? ?: defValue
+
+    override fun getBoolean(key: String, defValue: Boolean): Boolean = values[key] as Boolean? ?: defValue
+
+    override fun contains(key: String): Boolean = key in values
+
+    override fun edit(): SharedPreferences.Editor = Editor()
+
+    override fun registerOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(
+        listener: SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener,
+    ) = Unit
+
+    override fun unregisterOnSharedPreferenceChangeListener(
+        listener: SharedPreferences.OnSharedPreferenceChangeListener,
+    ) = Unit
+
+    private inner class Editor : SharedPreferences.Editor {
+        private val changes = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>()
+        private var clearFirst = false
+
+        override fun putString(key: String, value: String?) = put(key, value)
+
+        override fun putStringSet(key: String, values: Set<String>?) = put(key, values?.toSet())
+
+        override fun putInt(key: String, value: Int) = put(key, value)
+
+        override fun putLong(key: String, value: Long) = put(key, value)
+
+        override fun putFloat(key: String, value: Float) = put(key, value)
+
+        override fun putBoolean(key: String, value: Boolean) = put(key, value)
+
+        override fun remove(key: String) = put(key, null)
+
+        override fun clear(): Editor {
+            clearFirst = true
+            return this
+        }
+
+        override fun commit(): Boolean {
+            if (clearFirst) values.clear()
+            for ((key, value) in changes) {
+                if (value == null) values.remove(key) else values[key] = value
+            }
+            return true
+        }
+
+        override fun apply() {
+            commit()
+        }
+
+        private fun put(key: String, value: Any?): Editor {
+            changes[key] = value
+            return this
+        }
+    }
+}

+
+    private fun assertFormats(format: DurationFormat, cases: List<Pair<Long, String>>) {
+        for ((seconds, expected) in cases) {
+            assertEquals("$seconds seconds, ${format.togglName}", expected, formatDuration(seconds, format))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `decimal rounds the double's exact value as toFixed does`() {
+        // String.format("%.2f") would give 1.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 1.00 and 2.01 for the first six,
+        // whose quotient is stored just below a half. The last four are stored just above one.
+        assertFormats(
+            DurationFormat.DECIMAL,
+            listOf(
+                3618L to "1.00 h",
+                54L to "0.01 h",
+                162L to "0.04 h",
+                270L to "0.07 h",
+                3582L to "0.99 h",
+                7218L to "2.00 h",
+                18L to "0.01 h",
+                126L to "0.04 h",
+                342L to "0.10 h",
+                36018L to "10.01 h",
+            ),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `decimal writes a dot in a locale that writes a comma`() {
+        val before = Locale.getDefault()
+        Locale.setDefault(Locale.GERMANY)
+        try {
+            assertFormats(DurationFormat.DECIMAL, listOf(2826L to "0.79 h", 3618L to "1.00 h"))
+        } finally {
+            Locale.setDefault(before)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `a format other than classic or decimal is improved`() {
+        // The TypeScript gives 0:05:06 for each of these names, and for null and the number 5.
+        for (name in listOf("foo", "Classic", "DECIMAL", "", null)) {
+            val format = DurationFormat.fromTogglName(name)
+            assertEquals("name $name", DurationFormat.IMPROVED, format)
+            assertEquals("name $name", "0:05:06", formatDuration(306, format))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `a duration from 2 to the power 53 up is rounded to a double first`() {
+        // Long.MAX_VALUE becomes 2^63, so the seconds end in 08 where Long arithmetic gives 07.
+        for (format in listOf(DurationFormat.IMPROVED, DurationFormat.CLASSIC)) {
+            assertFormats(
+                format,
+                listOf(
+                    Long.MAX_VALUE to "2562047788015215:30:08",
+                    9007199254740993L to "2501999792983:36:32",
+                    Long.MIN_VALUE to if (format == DurationFormat.CLASSIC) "0 sec" else "0:00:00",
+                ),
+            )
+        }
+        assertFormats(
+            DurationFormat.DECIMAL,
+            listOf(
+                Long.MAX_VALUE to "2562047788015215.50 h",
+                9007199254740993L to "2501999792983.61 h",
+                Long.MIN_VALUE to "0.00 h",
+            ),
+        )
+    }
 }

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui.theme
+
+import androidx.compose.material3.ColorScheme
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/** The bottom bar item colours Tokkl sets over Material 3's. ThemeContrastTest checks their contrast. */
+class NavigationBarColoursTest {
+
+    @Test
+    fun lightItemsFollowTheDecision() = assertDecision(TokklLightColorScheme)
+
+    @Test
+    fun darkItemsFollowTheDecision() = assertDecision(TokklDarkColorScheme)
+
+    @Test
+    fun onlyTheActiveLabelIsBold() {
+        assertEquals(FontWeight(700), navigationBarLabelWeight(selected = true))
+        assertNull(navigationBarLabelWeight(selected = false))
+    }
+
+    private fun assertDecision(s: ColorScheme) {
+        val item = navigationBarItemColors(s)
+        assertEquals(s.primary, item.selectedIndicatorColor)
+        assertEquals(s.onPrimary, item.selectedIconColor)
+        assertEquals(s.primary, item.selectedTextColor)
+        assertEquals(s.onSurfaceVariant, item.unselectedIconColor)
+        assertEquals(s.onSurfaceVariant, item.unselectedTextColor)
+        // Material 3's default indicator, which the colour scheme still maps to --state-selected.
+        assertNotEquals(s.secondaryContainer, item.selectedIndicatorColor)
+    }
+}

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.ui.theme
+
+import androidx.compose.material3.ColorScheme
+import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
+import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
+import kotlin.math.abs
+import kotlin.math.roundToInt
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/**
+ * The texture, scene and wordmark-halo colours against Tokkl's styles.css, which writes each
+ * token once as light-dark(light, dark) in its first :root block.
+ */
+class TextureTokensTest {
+
+    private class Token(
+        val name: String,
+        val light: Color,
+        val dark: Color,
+        val actual: (TokklExtraColors) -> Color,
+    )
+
+    /** Copied from `../tokkl/packages/web/src/styles.css`, tokens `--texture-*`, `--scene-*` and `--wordmark-halo`. */
+    private val stylesCss = listOf(
+        Token("--texture-glow", Color(0xFFF3DFD1), Color(0xFF352A38)) { it.textureGlow },
+        Token("--texture-swell", Color(0xFFE0EAE3), Color(0xFF17324B)) { it.textureSwell },
+        Token("--texture-stripe", Color(0xFFC7DCD7), Color(0xFF1A475D)) { it.textureStripe },
+        Token("--scene-glow", Color(0xFFDFB698), Color(0xFF4F332E)) { it.sceneGlow },
+        Token("--scene-swell", Color(0xFFB9CBBB), Color(0xFF134354)) { it.sceneSwell },
+        Token("--scene-stripe", Color(0xFFE9CEB3), Color(0xFF2D212A)) { it.sceneStripe },
+        Token("--wordmark-halo", Color.Transparent, Color(0xFFA9B0D6)) { it.wordmarkHalo },
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun lightValuesMatchStylesCss() =
+        assertValues(TokklLightExtraColors) { it.light }
+
+    @Test
+    fun darkValuesMatchStylesCss() =
+        assertValues(TokklDarkExtraColors) { it.dark }
+
+    @Test
+    fun onlyDarkDrawsTheWordmarkHalo() {
+        assertEquals(0f, TokklLightExtraColors.wordmarkHalo.alpha, 0f)
+        assertEquals(1f, TokklDarkExtraColors.wordmarkHalo.alpha, 0f)
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * styles.css computes the texture values as primary at 14% (glow) and secondary at 12% (swell)
+     * and 22% (stripe) over surface, and the scene values as primary at 28% (glow) and 14% (stripe)
+     * and secondary at 24% (swell) over bg, all mixed in sRGB. It says to recompute them when a
+     * colour changes, so a palette change that skips this fails here. A channel may differ by one,
+     * because the light --scene-stripe green mixes to 206.5 and styles.css stores 206.
+     */
+    @Test
+    fun lightMixesMatchTheirFormulas() = assertMixes(TokklLightColorScheme, TokklLightExtraColors)
+
+    @Test
+    fun darkMixesMatchTheirFormulas() = assertMixes(TokklDarkColorScheme, TokklDarkExtraColors)
+
+    private fun assertValues(extra: TokklExtraColors, expected: (Token) -> Color) {
+        val failures = stylesCss.mapNotNull { token -> mismatch(token.name, expected(token), token.actual(extra)) }
+        assertTrue("Values that differ from styles.css:\n" + failures.joinToString("\n"), failures.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    private fun assertMixes(s: ColorScheme, x: TokklExtraColors) {
+        val failures = listOfNotNull(
+            mismatch("--texture-glow", mix(s.primary, 0.14, s.surface), x.textureGlow, 1),
+            mismatch("--texture-swell", mix(s.secondary, 0.12, s.surface), x.textureSwell, 1),
+            mismatch("--texture-stripe", mix(s.secondary, 0.22, s.surface), x.textureStripe, 1),
+            mismatch("--scene-glow", mix(s.primary, 0.28, s.background), x.sceneGlow, 1),
+            mismatch("--scene-swell", mix(s.secondary, 0.24, s.background), x.sceneSwell, 1),
+            mismatch("--scene-stripe", mix(s.primary, 0.14, s.background), x.sceneStripe, 1),
+        )
+        assertTrue("Values that differ from their mix:\n" + failures.joinToString("\n"), failures.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    private fun mismatch(name: String, expected: Color, actual: Color, tolerance: Int = 0): String? {
+        val close = listOf(24, 16, 8, 0).all { shift ->
+            val e = (expected.toArgb() shr shift) and 0xFF
+            val a = (actual.toArgb() shr shift) and 0xFF
+            abs(e - a) <= tolerance
+        }
+        return if (close) null else "$name: expected ${hex(expected)}, got ${hex(actual)}"
+    }
+
+    private fun hex(colour: Color) = "#%08X".format(colour.toArgb())
+
+    /** CSS `color-mix(in srgb, top share, base)` for opaque colours, rounded per channel. */
+    private fun mix(top: Color, share: Double, base: Color): Color {
+        fun channel(shift: Int): Int {
+            val t = (top.toArgb() shr shift) and 0xFF
+            val b = (base.toArgb() shr shift) and 0xFF
+            return (t * share + b * (1 - share)).roundToInt()
+        }
+        return Color(red = channel(16), green = channel(8), blue = channel(0))
+    }
+}

             Pair("onTertiaryFixedVariant on tertiaryFixed", s.onTertiaryFixedVariant, s.tertiaryFixed, Kind.TEXT),
         )

+
+    /**
+     * The colours Tokkl gives a bottom bar item: the icon sits on the primary indicator, and the
+     * label sits below it, on the bar, which is surfaceContainer.
+     */
+    private fun navigationBarPairs(s: ColorScheme): List<Pair> {
+        val item = navigationBarItemColors(s)
+        val bar = s.surfaceContainer
+        return listOf(
+            Pair("active nav icon on indicator", item.selectedIconColor, item.selectedIndicatorColor, Kind.NON_TEXT),
+            Pair("active nav label on bar", item.selectedTextColor, bar, Kind.TEXT),
+            Pair("inactive nav icon on bar", item.unselectedIconColor, bar, Kind.NON_TEXT),
+            Pair("inactive nav label on bar", item.unselectedTextColor, bar, Kind.TEXT),
+        )
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * The pairs styles.css lists for the gradients behind the top bar strip and the sign-in
+     * scene: text and muted text on each texture colour, text on each scene colour, and the
+     * wordmark halo on the surfaces the wordmark sits on. Light draws no halo, so it has no halo
+     * pairs. The pairs where gradients overlap are a drawing ticket's check.
+     */
+    private fun texturePairs(s: ColorScheme, x: TokklExtraColors): List<Pair> {
+        val texture = listOf("textureGlow" to x.textureGlow, "textureSwell" to x.textureSwell, "textureStripe" to x.textureStripe)
+        val scene = listOf("sceneGlow" to x.sceneGlow, "sceneSwell" to x.sceneSwell, "sceneStripe" to x.sceneStripe)
+        val text = texture.flatMap { (name, colour) ->
+            listOf(
+                Pair("onSurface on $name", s.onSurface, colour, Kind.TEXT),
+                Pair("onSurfaceVariant on $name", s.onSurfaceVariant, colour, Kind.TEXT),
+            )
+        } + scene.map { (name, colour) -> Pair("onSurface on $name", s.onSurface, colour, Kind.TEXT) }
+        val haloDrawn = x.wordmarkHalo.alpha > 0f
+        val halo = if (haloDrawn) {
+            (listOf("background" to s.background, "surface" to s.surface) + texture + scene)
+                .map { (name, colour) -> Pair("wordmarkHalo on $name", x.wordmarkHalo, colour, Kind.NON_TEXT) }
+        } else {
+            emptyList()
+        }
+        return text + halo
     }
