---
type: practice
title: Port Tokkl's TypeScript with JavaScript semantics and verbatim test cases
description: >-
  The Kotlin time parsers copy JavaScript's whitespace, rounding and replace
  rules, return null for NaN, and reuse the .test.ts cases verbatim.
tags:
  - time
  - parsing
  - testing
  - tokkl
kk_schema_version: 3
kk_id: practice-port-tokkl-typescript-with-javascript-semantics
kk_derived_from: []
kk_relates_to:
  - practice-match-web-app-behavior-account-timezone
kk_depends_on: []
kk_confidence: high
---
The ports in `com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time` (`Duration.kt` for `parseDuration`, `TimeOfDay.kt` for `parseTimeOfDay`, `Snap.kt` for `snapSpan`, and `TimeZone.kt`, which accepts the timezone names Intl accepts) behave like the TypeScript they port, including where Kotlin differs from JavaScript. `JsSemantics.kt` has the helpers. JavaScript's `\s` and `String.prototype.trim` use the ECMAScript WhiteSpace and LineTerminator characters, while Java's `\s` matches only ASCII and Kotlin's `trim()` uses yet another set. `Math.round` rounds halves up and `kotlin.math.round` rounds them to even. JavaScript's `replace(',', '.')` changes only the first comma, so the port calls `replaceFirst`.

A Long cannot hold NaN, Infinity or integers from 2^63 up, so where the TypeScript returns one of those the port returns null. `parseDuration` returns NaN for the unit `constructor`, because its unit lookup finds that key on `Object.prototype`, and Infinity once a number of 300-odd digits overflows a double.

`DurationTest` and `TimeOfDayTest` hold the cases of the matching `.test.ts` file verbatim, and their header names the tokkl commit they were copied at. To follow an upstream change, copy the cases again and update that commit. `DurationJsSemanticsTest` pins the JavaScript traps in its own file, since they are not in `duration.test.ts` and a later diff against tokkl leaves them alone. Its expected values come from running the TypeScript file in Node 24, where `node file.mts` strips the types.

Where the TypeScript throws a RangeError, `snapSpan` throws `IllegalArgumentException`. Within hours of the end of a JavaScript Date's range, near the year 275760, the TypeScript can also return an invalid Date. An `Instant` has no such value, so the port throws there too, and `SnapJsDateRangeTest` pins that edge.

`parseTimeZone` was checked against Intl in Node 24.21 (ICU 78.3, tz 2026c), but its results still depend on the device. A name resolves only if the device's tz database lists it or `INTL_ONLY_NAMES` maps it, and that database can be older than the server's. Under tz 2026c America/Edmonton stays at -06:00 all year, while the JDK 21 on this machine ships 2026b and still gives -07:00 in winter.

type: map
title: 'Tokkl and Toggl API endpoints, auth and Tokkl-only extensions'
description: >-
  Tokkl speaks a subset of Toggl API v9 at /api/v9 and Reports v3 at
  /reports/api/v3 with Basic auth, plus Tokkl-only fields and endpoints.

  - api

kk_schema_version: 3

kk_derived_from:
  - docs/PRD.md
kk_relates_to: []

---
Tokkl implements a subset of the Toggl Track API v9 at `https://<server>/api/v9` and of the Reports API v3 at `https://<server>/reports/api/v3`. A client that accepts a base URL needs no other change to talk to it. The server URL may carry a path, such as `https://example.com/tokkl`. Toggl Track's API lives at `api.track.toggl.com`.

[credential-related line omitted]

Tokkl adds `role` and `time_resolution_minutes` to `GET /api/v9/me`, and the server snaps stopped entries to that resolution. Tokkl-only endpoints outside the Toggl API cover the trash (`GET/DELETE /api/app/trash`, `DELETE /api/app/trash/{id}`) and undoing a delete (`POST /api/app/time_entries/{id}/restore`). Favorites are a Tokkl key in `/me/preferences`, up to 9 of them.

The first version targets Tokkl only. Toggl Track comes later by pointing the app at Toggl's base URL, and that version hides the Tokkl-only features.

description: >-
  Sign-in stores timezone, week start and resolution from /me; onStart

---
`session/AccountSettings` holds the `GET /api/v9/me` fields that decide how the app shows and reads times: `timezone`, `beginning_of_week` and Tokkl's optional `time_resolution_minutes`. It holds no credential. Sign-in stores them from its own `/me`. A `/me` without `timezone` or `beginning_of_week`, or with a week start outside 0 to 6, fails sign-in as `SignInResult.NotTokkl`. Toggl Track and older Tokkl servers leave out `time_resolution_minutes` and do not snap, so it stays null for them.

`session/AccountSettingsSource` is the one place screens read the settings from. `TokklApplication.accountSettings` builds it over `AccountSettingsStore`, which keeps them in the private preferences file `account_settings`, so screens have them offline and after a restart. `MainActivity.onStart` calls `refresh()`, so a change made in the web app reaches the phone. A second `refresh()` while one runs gets the running job instead of sending another request. A failed request or write keeps what is stored, and a refresh drops its answer when sign-in stored newer settings while the request ran.

`AccountSettings.time()` gives a `time/AccountTime`: the account's zone, a clock in that zone, `now()`, `today()`, `local(instant)`, `startOfWeek(date)` and `snapUnsent(start, stop)`. It returns null when `parseTimeZone` cannot resolve the name, as when the server's Intl knows a zone that the phone's tz database lacks. There is no fallback to the device's zone or to UTC. A screen without an `AccountTime` must show no clock times or dates, take no time input and name the zone it does not know. Durations, the running clock, Start and Stop need no zone and keep working.

`NoDeviceZoneTest`, a JVM test, reads the constant pool of every compiled app class through `ClassReferences` and fails on `ZoneId.systemDefault`, `TimeZone.getDefault` (java.util and ICU), `Clock.systemDefaultZone`, a `now()` without arguments on the java.time date and time types, and a `Calendar` or `GregorianCalendar` built without a zone. Its `ALLOWED` set is empty, and a class added there needs a comment saying why the account's zone will not do. Take the zone, the clock and the dates from `AccountTime` instead.

  The shared client refuses unconfirmed http and unpinned self-signed
  certificates. SessionTrustModel prompts on every screen but sign-in.
tags:
  - security

[credential-related line omitted]

[credential-related line omitted]

`TogglApiClient` turns the refusals into `ApiError.CleartextNotConfirmed` and `ApiError.UntrustedCertificate`, both `ApiError.TrustRefused`, and reports them to its `TrustListener`. For the signed-in session that listener is `trust/SessionTrustModel`, and `TokklNavHost` shows its prompt from `trust/TrustPrompts.kt` above every screen except sign-in, which handles its own. Accepting stores the pin or the confirmation and runs the account settings refresh again. Cancelling keeps the requests refused and shows `TrustRefusedNotice`, whose "Review connection" button opens the prompt again. The prompt strings keep their `sign_in_*` names although every screen shows them.

Neither trust dialog closes on a tap outside or on Back, and the http warning needs a tick before its continue button works. `TrustActions` puts Cancel and then the risky action in one slot, stacked full width as `TextButton`s, so the risky action is never the filled call to action. At 200% font, `AlertDialog`'s own button row in material3 1.4.0 put Cancel over a Continue that wrapped to two lines, and the touch targets overlapped. `LargestFontScaleTest` opens both prompts at the largest font scale and checks with `assertDialogFits` that the dialog's scrolling area, title and buttons do not overlap.

The source of truth for the app's colours is the colour tokens in `../tokkl/packages/web/src/styles.css`.

The light theme has a sand background, ink text, a sunset orange primary, an aqua secondary, and hot pink and lime for states. The dark theme has a navy background with the neon counterparts.

The app bundles the Anybody font. Tokkl already ships it under the SIL Open Font License 1.1, with the license at `../tokkl/packages/web/public/fonts/OFL.txt`.

In the app, `ui/theme/Color.kt` defines the Material 3 roles and the running and billable state colours, each with a `styles.css:<line> --token` comment naming its source. `TokklTheme` has dynamic colour off by default. `Theme.Tokkl` in `res/values` and `res/values-night` sets `android:windowBackground` to the scheme's background (`@color/window_background`), so launch shows no flash of another colour before Compose draws. When a token changes, update `Color.kt` and `window_background` together. On Android 12 (API 31) and later, `syncApplicationNightMode` in `settings/ApplicationNightMode.kt` passes the stored theme mode to `UiModeManager.setApplicationNightMode` on every start and every change, off the main thread: light is `MODE_NIGHT_NO`, dark `MODE_NIGHT_YES` and system `MODE_NIGHT_AUTO`. Android picks `res/values` or `res/values-night` for the starting window by that mode, so a cold start shows the chosen theme's colours. `MainActivity` declares `android:configChanges="uiMode"`, so the sync does not recreate it. Below Android 12 the starting window still follows the system's dark mode, and the window theme never knows the wallpaper's colours. So `MainActivity` still repaints the window background and picks the system bar icon colours from the chosen theme each time the setting changes. Wallpaper colours are an opt-in switch on the settings screen, shown only on Android 12 (API 31) and later.

The font is upstream Anybody 1.114, the variable TTF with width and weight axes, at `res/font/anybody_wdth_wght.ttf`. `res/font` accepts only font files, so the OFL (`anybody_ofl.txt`) and the source note with commit, URL and SHA-256 (`anybody_font_source.txt`) live in `res/raw`. No code reads the source note, so `res/raw/keep.xml` marks it `tools:keep`. `ui/theme/Type.kt` defines `ClockTextStyle` and `TotalTextStyle` in Anybody.

The adaptive launcher icon is made from `../tokkl/docs/brand/tokkl-icon-v4-1024.png`. Its foreground and monochrome layers are 432 px PNGs in `res/drawable-xxxhdpi`, and `res/mipmap-anydpi/ic_launcher.xml` records the ImageMagick steps that built them.

---
type: practice
title: 'Use Tokkl''s palette, WCAG AA contrast and English string resources'
description: >-
  Always Tokkl's palette, with wallpaper dynamic colour only as an opt-in. WCAG
  AA contrast, colour never the only signal, text in resources.
tags:
  - ui
  - accessibility
  - i18n
kk_schema_version: 3
kk_id: practice-tokkl-palette-wcag-english-strings
kk_derived_from:
  - docs/PRD.md
kk_relates_to: []
kk_depends_on: []
kk_confidence: medium
---
Components come from Compose Material 3. Colour is always Tokkl's palette, never the wallpaper. Dynamic color is off and exists at most as an opt-in setting (G2). The theme is light, dark, or follows the system (G1).

Anybody, bundled with the app, sets the running clock, totals and headings. Everything else uses the system font.

Contrast meets WCAG AA as in Tokkl: 4.5:1 for text and 3:1 for non-text elements. Colour is never the only signal for a state. Every control has a TalkBack label, and layouts survive the largest font scale. Tokkl's sixth principle applies as written. Any style that costs a readable number, a reachable control or a passing test gets dropped.

The owner's design brief, "radical graffiti surfer", is Tokkl's principle 6, "Loud look, plain function" (surf-shop graffiti), together with the Visual direction subsection that follows the principles in section 4 of `../tokkl/docs/PRD.md`. The app's spacing scale, content width, type scale, shapes and wordmark values are drafted in the look-and-feel tickets TKA-0105 to TKA-0124. The code does not have them until those tickets land.

The app is English only. All text lives in string resources. No other language is planned, and contributors can add translations.

../tokkl/packages/api/src/routes/reports.ts
1 import express, { type Request, type Response, type Router } from 'express';
2 import type { Db } from '../db/index.ts';
3 import { requireAuth } from '../auth/middleware.ts';
4 import type { Deps } from '../http/context.ts';
5 import { readJson } from '../http/context.ts';
6 import { badRequest } from '../lib/errors.ts';
7 import { parseId } from '../lib/values.ts';
8 import { addDays } from '@tokkl/common/time';
9 import { memberWorkspace, type WorkspaceRow } from '../models/workspaces.ts';
10 import { filterFromBody, resolutionFor, type Dimension } from '../services/reports.ts';
11 import { checkOrderBy, v3WeeklyFilter, type JobResult, type ReportJob } from '../services/reportJobs.ts';

14 /** Toggl's Reports API v3 rejects ranges longer than a year. */
15 const TOGGL_MAX_DAYS = 366;
16 
17 /** The workspace in the URL, when the signed-in user is a member of it; 403 otherwise. */
18 function workspaceFor(db: Db, req: Request, res: Response): WorkspaceRow {
19   return memberWorkspace(db, res.locals.user.id, parseId(req.params.wid));
20 }
21 
22 const GROUPINGS: Record<string, Dimension> = {
23   projects: 'project', clients: 'client', tags: 'tag', tasks: 'task', time_entries: 'description', users: 'none',
24 };
25 
26 function dimension(value: unknown, fallback: string, field: string): [string, Dimension] {
27   const name = value === undefined || value === null || value === '' ? fallback : String(value);
28   const dim = GROUPINGS[name];
29   if (!dim) throw badRequest(`Invalid '${field}' value, allowed values are: '${Object.keys(GROUPINGS).join(', ')}'`);
30   return [name, dim];
31 }
32 
33 /** Builds the response off the main thread (see reportRunner.ts) and sends it. */
34 async function send(deps: Deps, res: Response, ws: WorkspaceRow, job: ReportJob): Promise<void> {
35   const result: JobResult = await reportRunner(deps.db).run(res.locals.user, ws, job);
36   res.set(result.headers);
37   if (result.type === 'csv') {
38     res.set('Content-Type', 'text/csv; charset=utf-8');
39     res.set('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${result.filename}"`);
40   } else {
41     res.type('json');
42   }
43   res.send(result.body);
44 }

46 /**
47  * The parts of Toggl's Reports API v3 that make sense for one person: detailed (with paging),
48  * totals, summary, weekly, and CSV exports.
49  */
50 export function reportsV3Routes(deps: Deps): Router {
51   const { db } = deps;
52   const api = express.Router();
53   api.use(requireAuth(deps));
54 
55   const v3Filter = (req: Request, res: Response) => {
56     const ws = workspaceFor(db, req, res);
57     const body = readJson(req);
58     return { ws, body, filter: filterFromBody(body, ws, TOGGL_MAX_DAYS) };
59   };
60 
61   api.post('/workspace/:wid/search/time_entries', async (req, res) => {
62     const { ws, body, filter } = v3Filter(req, res);
63     checkOrderBy(body.order_by);
64     const pageSize = Math.min(Math.max(Number(body.page_size ?? 50) || 50, 1), 1000);
65     const firstRow = Math.max(Number(body.first_row_number ?? 1) || 1, 1);
66     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'v3-search', filter, orderBy: body.order_by, orderDir: body.order_dir, pageSize, firstRow });
67   });
68 
69   api.post('/workspace/:wid/search/time_entries/totals', async (req, res) => {
70     const { ws, body, filter } = v3Filter(req, res);
71     const requestedResolution = body.granularity ?? body.resolution;
72     if (requestedResolution != null && requestedResolution !== 'day' && requestedResolution !== 'week' && requestedResolution !== 'month') {
73       throw badRequest("Invalid granularity or resolution: expected 'day', 'week' or 'month'");
74     }
75     const granularity = requestedResolution ?? resolutionFor(filter.startDate, filter.endDate);
76     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'v3-totals', filter, granularity, withGraph: Boolean(body.with_graph) });
77   });
78 
79   api.post('/workspace/:wid/search/time_entries.csv', async (req, res) => {
80     const { ws, body, filter } = v3Filter(req, res);
81     checkOrderBy(body.order_by);
82     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'v3-csv', filter, orderBy: body.order_by, orderDir: body.order_dir, durationFormat: body.duration_format });
83   });
84 
85   const summary = (req: Request, res: Response) => {
86     const { ws, body, filter } = v3Filter(req, res);
87     const [groupingName, grouping] = dimension(body.grouping, 'projects', 'grouping');
88     const [subName, sub] = dimension(body.sub_grouping, 'time_entries', 'sub_grouping');
89     if (grouping === sub) throw badRequest('grouping and sub_grouping must differ');
90     res.set('X-Range-Start', filter.startDate);
91     res.set('X-Range-End', filter.endDate);
92     res.set('X-Grouping', groupingName);
93     res.set('X-Sub-Grouping', subName);
94     return { ws, body, filter, grouping, sub };
95   };
96 
97   api.post('/workspace/:wid/summary/time_entries', async (req, res) => {
98     const { ws, body, filter, grouping, sub } = summary(req, res);
99     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'v3-summary', filter, grouping, sub, includeIds: body.include_time_entry_ids === true });
100   });
101 
102   api.post('/workspace/:wid/summary/time_entries.csv', async (req, res) => {
103     const { ws, body, filter, grouping, sub } = summary(req, res);
104     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'v3-summary-csv', filter, grouping, sub, durationFormat: body.duration_format });
105   });
106 
107   api.post('/workspace/:wid/weekly/time_entries', async (req, res) => {
108     const ws = workspaceFor(db, req, res);
109     const body = readJson(req);
110     const start = typeof body.start_date === 'string' ? body.start_date : '';
111     const range = filterFromBody({ ...body, end_date: body.end_date ?? (start ? addDays(start, 6) : undefined) }, ws, TOGGL_MAX_DAYS);
112     const { filter, weekStart } = v3WeeklyFilter(range);
113     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'v3-weekly', filter, weekStart });
114   });
115 
116   return api;
117 }
118 
119 /** The largest page the web app's detailed report may request. */
120 const APP_MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 500;
121 
122 function positiveInt(value: unknown, fallback: number, field: string): number {
123   if (value === undefined || value === null) return fallback;
124   if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 1) throw badRequest(`Invalid '${field}' value`);
125   return value;
126 }
127 
128 /** Report endpoint for the web app: totals, a time series and groupings in one response. */
129 export function appReportRoutes(deps: Deps): Router {
130   const { db } = deps;
131   const api = express.Router();
132 
133   /** The account's workspace, the request body and its filter, with the app's range allowance. */
134   const appFilter = (req: Request, res: Response) => {
135     const user = res.locals.user;
136     // PUT /me accepts only a workspace the user belongs to, but a membership can end after that.
137     const ws = memberWorkspace(db, user.id, user.default_workspace_id);
138     const body = readJson(req);
139     // The web app always applies the workspace rounding setting; the request can still override it.
140     const filter = filterFromBody({ rounding: ws.rounding, rounding_minutes: ws.rounding_minutes, ...body }, ws);
141     return { user, ws, body, filter };
142   };
143 
144   api.post('/report/weekly', requireAuth(deps), async (req, res) => {
145     const { ws, body, filter } = appFilter(req, res);
146     const rows = body.rows ?? 'projects';
147     if (rows !== 'projects' && rows !== 'clients') throw badRequest("Invalid 'rows' value, allowed values are: 'projects, clients'");
148     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'app-weekly', filter, rows });
149   });
150 
151   api.post('/report', requireAuth(deps), async (req, res) => {
152     const { ws, body, filter } = appFilter(req, res);
153     const [, grouping] = dimension(body.grouping, 'projects', 'grouping');
154     const [, sub] = dimension(body.sub_grouping, 'time_entries', 'sub_grouping');
155     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'app-report', filter, grouping, sub });
156   });
157 
158   /** The detailed report's sort order, checked before any entry is read. */
159   const detailedOrder = (body: Record<string, unknown>) => {
160     const sort = body.sort ?? 'date';
161     if (sort !== 'date' && sort !== 'duration' && sort !== 'description') {
162       throw badRequest("Invalid 'sort' value, allowed values are: 'date, duration, description'");
163     }
164     const dir = body.dir ?? 'desc';
165     if (dir !== 'asc' && dir !== 'desc') throw badRequest("Invalid 'dir' value, allowed values are: 'asc, desc'");
166     return { sort, dir } as const;
167   };
168 
169   api.post('/report/entries', requireAuth(deps), async (req, res) => {
170     const { ws, body, filter } = appFilter(req, res);
171     const { sort, dir } = detailedOrder(body);
172     const pageSize = positiveInt(body.page_size, 100, 'page_size');
173     if (pageSize > APP_MAX_PAGE_SIZE) throw badRequest(`'page_size' must be at most ${APP_MAX_PAGE_SIZE}`);
174     const page = positiveInt(body.page, 1, 'page');
175     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'app-entries', filter, sort, dir, pageSize, page });
176   });
177 
178   // CSV exports of the report the web app shows: its range allowance, filters and rounding.
179   api.post('/report/detailed.csv', requireAuth(deps), async (req, res) => {
180     const { ws, body, filter } = appFilter(req, res);
181     const { sort, dir } = detailedOrder(body);
182     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'app-detailed-csv', filter, sort, dir, durationFormat: body.duration_format });
183   });
184 
185   api.post('/report/summary.csv', requireAuth(deps), async (req, res) => {
186     const { ws, body, filter } = appFilter(req, res);
187     const [, grouping] = dimension(body.grouping, 'projects', 'grouping');
188     const [, sub] = dimension(body.sub_grouping, 'time_entries', 'sub_grouping');
189     await send(deps, res, ws, { kind: 'app-summary-csv', filter, grouping, sub, durationFormat: body.duration_format });
190   });
191 
192   return api;
193 }
../tokkl/packages/api/src/services/reportJobs.ts
1 import type { Db } from '../db/index.ts';

12   collectEntries, groupEntries, resolutionFor, timeSeries, totalsOf, weeklyGrid,

16 /**
17  * The part of each report request that reads the entries and builds the response body. The routes
18  * validate the request first and pass a job here, so a job only fails on a bug. Jobs read the
19  * database and nothing else, which lets them run in the report worker (see reportRunner.ts).
20  */
21 export type ReportJob =
22   | { kind: 'v3-search'; filter: ReportFilter; orderBy: unknown; orderDir: unknown; pageSize: number; firstRow: number }
23   | { kind: 'v3-totals'; filter: ReportFilter; granularity: Resolution; withGraph: boolean }
24   | { kind: 'v3-csv'; filter: ReportFilter; orderBy: unknown; orderDir: unknown; durationFormat: unknown }
25   | { kind: 'v3-summary'; filter: ReportFilter; grouping: Dimension; sub: Dimension; includeIds: boolean }
26   | { kind: 'v3-summary-csv'; filter: ReportFilter; grouping: Dimension; sub: Dimension; durationFormat: unknown }
27   | { kind: 'v3-weekly'; filter: ReportFilter; weekStart: string }
28   | { kind: 'app-weekly'; filter: ReportFilter; rows: 'projects' | 'clients' }
29   | { kind: 'app-report'; filter: ReportFilter; grouping: Dimension; sub: Dimension }
30   | { kind: 'app-entries'; filter: ReportFilter; sort: 'date' | 'duration' | 'description'; dir: 'asc' | 'desc'; pageSize: number; page: number }
31   | { kind: 'app-detailed-csv'; filter: ReportFilter; sort: 'date' | 'duration' | 'description'; dir: 'asc' | 'desc'; durationFormat: unknown }
32   | { kind: 'app-summary-csv'; filter: ReportFilter; grouping: Dimension; sub: Dimension; durationFormat: unknown };
33 
34 /** A finished response: a JSON or CSV body plus any extra headers. */
35 export interface JobResult {
36   type: 'json' | 'csv';
37   body: string;
38   filename?: string;
39   headers: Record<string, string>;
40 }
41 
42 function durationFormatOf(value: unknown): DurationFormat {
43   return value === 'classic' || value === 'decimal' ? value : 'improved';
44 }
45 
46 const ORDER_BY = ['date', 'user', 'duration', 'description', 'last_update'];
47 
48 /** Throws the Toggl error for an unknown order_by, before any entry is read. */
49 export function checkOrderBy(orderBy: unknown): void {
50   if (!(ORDER_BY as unknown[]).includes(orderBy ?? 'date')) {
51     throw badRequest("Invalid 'order_by' value, allowed values are: 'date, user, duration, description, last_update'");
52   }
53 }
54 
55 function sortDetailed(entries: ReportEntry[], orderBy: unknown, orderDir: unknown): ReportEntry[] {
56   checkOrderBy(orderBy);
57   const dir = String(orderDir ?? 'DESC').toUpperCase() === 'ASC' ? 1 : -1;
58   const sorted = [...entries];
59   switch (orderBy ?? 'date') {
60     case 'date':
61     case 'user':
62       sorted.sort((a, b) => dir * (a.start.localeCompare(b.start) || a.id - b.id));
63       break;
64     case 'duration':
65       sorted.sort((a, b) => dir * (a.seconds - b.seconds || a.id - b.id));
66       break;
67     case 'description':
68       sorted.sort((a, b) => dir * (a.description.localeCompare(b.description) || a.id - b.id));
69       break;
70     case 'last_update':
71       sorted.sort((a, b) => dir * (a.at.localeCompare(b.at) || a.id - b.id));
72       break;
73   }
74   return sorted;
75 }
76 
77 function detailedRow(entry: ReportEntry, rowNumber: number, user: { id: number; fullname: string; email: string }) {
78   return {
79     user_id: user.id,
80     username: user.fullname,
81     email: user.email,
82     project_id: entry.projectId,
83     project_name: entry.projectName,
84     project_color: entry.projectColor,
85     project_hex: entry.projectColor,
86     client_name: entry.clientName,
87     task_id: entry.taskId,
88     task_name: entry.taskName,
89     description: entry.description,
90     billable: entry.billable,
91     tag_ids: entry.tags.map((tag) => tag.id),
92     tag_names: entry.tags.map((tag) => tag.name),
93     hourly_rate_in_cents: entry.hourlyRateCents,
94     billable_amount_in_cents: entry.amountCents,
95     currency: entry.currency,
96     row_number: rowNumber,
97     time_entries: [{
98       id: entry.id,
99       seconds: entry.seconds,
100       start: togglTime(entry.start),
101       stop: togglTime(entry.stop),
102       at: togglTime(entry.at),
103     }],
104   };
105 }
106 
107 function summaryGroup(group: Group, groupingDimension: Dimension, includeIds: boolean) {
108   return {
109     id: groupingDimension === 'description' ? null : group.id,
110     ...(groupingDimension === 'project' ? { project_color: group.color, project_hex_color: group.color } : {}),
111     sub_groups: group.subGroups.map((sub) => ({
112       id: sub.id,
113       title: sub.title,
114       seconds: sub.seconds,
115       ...(includeIds ? { ids: sub.entryIds } : {}),
116     })),
117     ...(group.subGroups.length === 0 ? { seconds: group.seconds } : {}),
118   };
119 }
120 
121 /** Toggl's detailed CSV layout. */
122 function detailedCsv(entries: ReportEntry[], user: UserRow, durationFormat: unknown): string {
123   return toTogglCsv(entries.map((entry) => ({
124     ...entry, tags: entry.tags.map((tag) => tag.name),
125   })), user, durationFormatOf(durationFormat));
126 }
127 
128 /** One row per group, or per sub-group when the groups have them. */
129 function summaryCsv(groups: Group[], durationFormat: unknown): string {
130   const format = durationFormatOf(durationFormat);
131   const rows: Array<Array<string | number | null>> = [['Group', 'Sub group', 'Duration']];
132   for (const group of groups) {
133     if (group.subGroups.length === 0) rows.push([group.title || '(none)', '', formatDuration(group.seconds, format)]);
134     for (const subGroup of group.subGroups) {
135       rows.push([group.title || '(none)', subGroup.title || '(none)', formatDuration(subGroup.seconds, format)]);
136     }
137   }
138   return toCsv(rows);
139 }
140 
141 function appEntry(entry: ReportEntry) {
142   return {
143     id: entry.id, description: entry.description, start: entry.start, stop: entry.stop, seconds: entry.seconds,
144     billable: entry.billable, project_id: entry.projectId, project_name: entry.projectName, project_color: entry.projectColor,
145     client_name: entry.clientName, task_name: entry.taskName, tags: entry.tags.map((tag) => tag.name),
146     amount_cents: entry.amountCents, currency: entry.currency,
147   };
148 }
149 
150 function rangeHeaders(startDate: string, endDate: string): Record<string, string> {
151   return { 'X-Range-Start': startDate, 'X-Range-End': endDate };
152 }
153 
154 const json = (value: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): JobResult => ({ type: 'json', body: JSON.stringify(value), headers });
155 const csv = (body: string, filename: string, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): JobResult => ({ type: 'csv', body, filename, headers });
156 
157 function v3Weekly(entries: ReportEntry[], user: UserRow, weekStart: string) {
158   const rows = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof emptyWeeklyRow>>();
159   function emptyWeeklyRow(entry: ReportEntry) {
160     return {
161       user_id: user.id, user_name: user.fullname, project_id: entry.projectId, project_name: entry.projectName,
162       project_color: entry.projectColor, project_hex_color: entry.projectColor, client_name: entry.clientName,
163       planned_task_id: null, seconds: Array<number>(7).fill(0), billable_seconds: Array<number>(7).fill(0),
164       billable_amounts_in_cents: Array<number>(7).fill(0), hourly_rate_in_cents: entry.hourlyRateCents, currency: entry.currency,
165     };
166   }
167   for (const entry of entries) {
168     const key = String(entry.projectId ?? 'none');
169     let row = rows.get(key);
170     if (!row) rows.set(key, (row = emptyWeeklyRow(entry)));
171     const day = daysBetween(weekStart, entry.date);
172     if (day < 0 || day > 6) continue;
173     row.seconds[day]! += entry.seconds;
174     if (entry.billable) row.billable_seconds[day]! += entry.seconds;
175     row.billable_amounts_in_cents[day]! += entry.amountCents ?? 0;
176   }
177   return [...rows.values()];
178 }
179 
180 export function runJob(db: Db, user: UserRow, ws: WorkspaceRow, job: ReportJob): JobResult {
181   const { filter } = job;
182   const entries = () => collectEntries(db, user, ws, filter);
183   switch (job.kind) {
184     case 'v3-search': {
185       const sorted = sortDetailed(entries(), job.orderBy, job.orderDir);
186       const { firstRow, pageSize } = job;
187       const headers = rangeHeaders(filter.startDate, filter.endDate);
188       if (firstRow - 1 + pageSize < sorted.length) {
189         headers['X-Next-ID'] = String(sorted[firstRow - 1 + pageSize]!.id);
190         headers['X-Next-Row-Number'] = String(firstRow + pageSize);
191       }
192       const page = sorted.slice(firstRow - 1, firstRow - 1 + pageSize);
193       return json(page.map((entry, i) => detailedRow(entry, firstRow + i, user)), headers);
194     }
195     case 'v3-totals': {
196       const list = entries();
197       const totals = totalsOf(list);
198       const series = timeSeries(list, filter.startDate, filter.endDate, job.granularity, user.beginning_of_week);
199       return json({
200         seconds: totals.seconds,
201         tracked_days: new Set(list.map((e) => e.date)).size,
202         billable_amount_in_cents: totals.amounts.reduce((sum, a) => sum + a.cents, 0),
203         labour_cost_in_cents: 0,
204         resolution: job.granularity,
205         rates: [],
206         graph: job.withGraph ? series.map((point) => ({ seconds: point.seconds, billable_amount_in_cents: 0, labour_cost_in_cents: 0, by_rate: {} })) : [],
207       }, rangeHeaders(filter.startDate, filter.endDate));
208     }
209     case 'v3-csv':
210       return csv(detailedCsv(sortDetailed(entries(), job.orderBy, job.orderDir), user, job.durationFormat), 'Toggl_time_entries.csv');
211     case 'v3-summary': {
212       const groups = groupEntries(entries(), job.grouping, job.sub);
213       return json({ groups: groups.map((group) => summaryGroup(group, job.grouping, job.includeIds)) });
214     }
215     case 'v3-summary-csv':
216       return csv(summaryCsv(groupEntries(entries(), job.grouping, job.sub), job.durationFormat), 'Toggl_summary.csv');
217     case 'v3-weekly':
218       return json(v3Weekly(entries(), user, job.weekStart), rangeHeaders(job.weekStart, filter.endDate));
219     case 'app-weekly': {
220       const grid = weeklyGrid(entries(), filter.startDate, filter.endDate, job.rows, user.beginning_of_week);
221       return json({
222         start_date: filter.startDate,
223         end_date: filter.endDate,
224         mode: grid.mode,
225         columns: grid.columns,
226         rows: grid.rows,
227         column_totals: grid.columnTotals,
228         total: grid.total,
229       });
230     }
231     case 'app-report': {
232       const list = entries();
233       const resolution = resolutionFor(filter.startDate, filter.endDate);
234       const totals = totalsOf(list);
235       return json({
236         start_date: filter.startDate,
237         end_date: filter.endDate,
238         resolution,
239         week_start: startOfWeek(filter.startDate, user.beginning_of_week),
240         totals: {
241           seconds: totals.seconds,
242           billable_seconds: totals.billableSeconds,
243           entry_count: totals.entryCount,
244           amounts: totals.amounts,
245         },
246         series: timeSeries(list, filter.startDate, filter.endDate, resolution, user.beginning_of_week)
247           .map((point) => ({ start: point.start, seconds: point.seconds, billable_seconds: point.billableSeconds })),
248         groups: groupEntries(list, job.grouping, job.grouping === job.sub ? 'none' : job.sub).map(function toJson(group): unknown {
249           return {
250             id: group.id, title: group.title, color: group.color, seconds: group.seconds,
251             billable_seconds: group.billableSeconds, amount_cents: group.amountCents,
252             sub_groups: group.subGroups.map(toJson),
253           };
254         }),
255       });
256     }
257     case 'app-entries': {
258       const sorted = sortDetailed(entries(), job.sort, job.dir);
259       const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sorted.length / job.pageSize));
260       // A bookmarked page past the end shows the last page; the response says which page it is.
../tokkl/packages/api/src/services/reports.ts
1 import type { Db } from '../db/index.ts';
2 import { badRequest } from '../lib/errors.ts';
3 import { asBool } from '../lib/values.ts';
4 import { addDays, dateKey, daysBetween, parseDateKey, startOfDay, startOfWeek } from '@tokkl/common/time';
5 import type { UserRow } from '../models/users.ts';
6 import type { WorkspaceRow } from '../models/workspaces.ts';

8 export type IdFilter = Array<number | null>;
9 
10 export interface ReportFilter {
11   /** Inclusive, in the user's timezone. */
12   startDate: string;
13   /** Inclusive, in the user's timezone. */
14   endDate: string;
15   projectIds?: IdFilter;
16   clientIds?: IdFilter;
17   tagIds?: IdFilter;
18   taskIds?: IdFilter;
19   userIds?: IdFilter;
20   minDurationSeconds?: number;
21   maxDurationSeconds?: number;
22   /** Negated filters: drop entries matching any of these. `null` means "without". */
23   excludeProjectIds?: IdFilter;
24   excludeClientIds?: IdFilter;
25   excludeTagIds?: IdFilter;
26   timeEntryIds?: number[];
27   billable?: boolean;
28   description?: string;
29   /** -1 down, 0 nearest, 1 up. Applied per entry when `roundingMinutes` > 0. */
30   rounding?: number;
31   roundingMinutes?: number;
32 }

47   clientName: string | null;

56 interface EntryQueryRow {
57   id: number;
58   description: string;
59   start: string;
60   stop: string;
61   updated_at: string;
62   billable: number;
63   project_id: number | null;
64   project_name: string | null;
65   project_color: string | null;
66   project_rate: number | null;
67   project_currency: string | null;
68   client_id: number | null;
69   client_name: string | null;
70   task_id: number | null;
71   task_name: string | null;
72   tags_json: string;
73 }
74 
75 const MAX_RANGE_DAYS = 3660;
76 
77 /** Rounding steps Toggl accepts, in minutes. 0 turns rounding off. */
78 export const ROUNDING_MINUTES = [0, 1, 5, 6, 10, 12, 15, 30, 60, 240];
79 
80 export function validateRange(startDate: unknown, endDate: unknown, maxDays = MAX_RANGE_DAYS): { startDate: string; endDate: string } {
81   if (typeof startDate !== 'string' || !parseDateKey(startDate)) throw badRequest('Invalid start_date: expected YYYY-MM-DD');
82   if (typeof endDate !== 'string' || !parseDateKey(endDate)) throw badRequest('Invalid end_date: expected YYYY-MM-DD');
83   const days = daysBetween(startDate, endDate);
84   if (days < 0) throw badRequest('end_date must not be earlier than start_date');
85   if (days + 1 > maxDays) throw badRequest(`Maximum allowed date range is ${maxDays} days`);
86   return { startDate, endDate };
87 }
88 
89 function roundSeconds(seconds: number, mode: number, minutes: number): number {
90   if (minutes <= 0) return seconds;
91   const step = minutes * 60;
92   if (mode > 0) return Math.ceil(seconds / step) * step;
93   if (mode < 0) return Math.floor(seconds / step) * step;
94   return Math.round(seconds / step) * step;
95 }
96 
97 function idClause(column: string, ids: IdFilter | undefined, params: Array<string | number>): string | null {
98   if (!ids || ids.length === 0) return null;
99   const numeric = ids.filter((id): id is number => id !== null);
100   const parts: string[] = [];
101   if (numeric.length) {
102     parts.push(`${column} IN (${numeric.map(() => '?').join(',')})`);
103     params.push(...numeric);
104   }
105   if (ids.includes(null)) parts.push(`${column} IS NULL`);
106   return `(${parts.join(' OR ')})`;
107 }
108 
109 function tagClause(ids: IdFilter | undefined, params: Array<string | number>): string | null {
110   if (!ids || ids.length === 0) return null;
111   const numeric = ids.filter((id): id is number => id !== null);
112   const parts: string[] = [];
113   if (numeric.length) {
114     parts.push(`EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM time_entry_tags x WHERE x.time_entry_id = te.id AND x.tag_id IN (${numeric.map(() => '?').join(',')}))`);
115     params.push(...numeric);
116   }
117   if (ids.includes(null)) parts.push('NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM time_entry_tags x WHERE x.time_entry_id = te.id)');
118   return `(${parts.join(' OR ')})`;
119 }
120 
121 /** Returns stopped, non-deleted entries matching `filter`, oldest first. */
122 export function collectEntries(db: Db, user: UserRow, workspace: WorkspaceRow, filter: ReportFilter): ReportEntry[] {
123   const from = startOfDay(filter.startDate, user.timezone);
124   const to = startOfDay(addDays(filter.endDate, 1), user.timezone);
125   const params: Array<string | number> = [user.id, workspace.id, from.toISOString(), to.toISOString()];
126   const where = ['te.user_id = ?', 'te.workspace_id = ?', 'te.start >= ?', 'te.start < ?', 'te.stop IS NOT NULL', 'te.deleted_at IS NULL'];
127 
128   for (const clause of [
129     idClause('te.project_id', filter.projectIds, params),
130     idClause('p.client_id', filter.clientIds, params),
131     idClause('te.task_id', filter.taskIds, params),
132     idClause('te.user_id', filter.userIds, params),
133   ]) if (clause) where.push(clause);
134 
135   if (filter.minDurationSeconds !== undefined) {
136     where.push('unixepoch(te.stop) - unixepoch(te.start) >= ?');
137     params.push(filter.minDurationSeconds);
138   }
139   if (filter.maxDurationSeconds !== undefined) {
140     where.push('unixepoch(te.stop) - unixepoch(te.start) <= ?');
141     params.push(filter.maxDurationSeconds);
142   }
143 
144   const tags = tagClause(filter.tagIds, params);
145   if (tags) where.push(tags);
146 
147   // IFNULL keeps entries whose column is NULL from vanishing: NOT (NULL IN (...)) is NULL, not true.
148   for (const clause of [
149     idClause('te.project_id', filter.excludeProjectIds, params),
150     idClause('p.client_id', filter.excludeClientIds, params),
151     tagClause(filter.excludeTagIds, params),
152   ]) if (clause) where.push(`NOT IFNULL(${clause}, 0)`);
153   if (filter.timeEntryIds?.length) {
154     where.push(`te.id IN (${filter.timeEntryIds.map(() => '?').join(',')})`);
155     params.push(...filter.timeEntryIds);
156   }
157   if (filter.billable !== undefined) {
158     where.push('te.billable = ?');
159     params.push(Number(filter.billable));
160   }
161   if (filter.description) {
162     where.push('instr(lower(te.description), lower(?)) > 0');
163     params.push(filter.description);
164   }
165 
166   const rows = db.prepare(`
167     SELECT te.id, te.description, te.start, te.stop, te.updated_at, te.billable, te.project_id, te.task_id,
168       p.name AS project_name, p.color AS project_color, p.rate AS project_rate, p.currency AS project_currency,
169       c.id AS client_id, c.name AS client_name, t.name AS task_name,
170       (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('id', id, 'name', name)) FROM (
171          SELECT tg.id, tg.name FROM time_entry_tags tet JOIN tags tg ON tg.id = tet.tag_id
172          WHERE tet.time_entry_id = te.id ORDER BY tg.name COLLATE NOCASE)) AS tags_json
173     FROM time_entries te
174     LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.id = te.project_id
175     LEFT JOIN clients c ON c.id = p.client_id
176     LEFT JOIN tasks t ON t.id = te.task_id
177     WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}
178     ORDER BY te.start, te.id
179   `).all(...params) as unknown as EntryQueryRow[];
180 
181   const roundingMinutes = filter.roundingMinutes ?? 0;
182   return rows.map((row) => {
183     const raw = Math.floor((Date.parse(row.stop) - Date.parse(row.start)) / 1000);
184     const seconds = roundSeconds(raw, filter.rounding ?? 0, roundingMinutes);
185     const billable = asBool(row.billable);
186     // The workspace default rate is in the workspace currency, whatever currency the project names.
187     const rate = row.project_rate ?? workspace.default_hourly_rate;
188     const currency = row.project_rate !== null ? (row.project_currency ?? workspace.default_currency) : workspace.default_currency;
189     const hourlyRateCents = billable && rate !== null ? Math.round(rate * 100) : null;
190     return {
191       id: row.id,
192       description: row.description,
193       start: row.start,
194       stop: row.stop,
195       at: row.updated_at,
196       date: dateKey(new Date(row.start), user.timezone),
197       seconds,
198       billable,
199       projectId: row.project_id,
200       projectName: row.project_name,
201       projectColor: row.project_color,
202       clientId: row.client_id,
203       clientName: row.client_name,
204       taskId: row.task_id,
205       taskName: row.task_name,
206       tags: JSON.parse(row.tags_json) as Array<{ id: number; name: string }>,
207       hourlyRateCents,
208       currency,
209       amountCents: hourlyRateCents === null ? null : Math.round((seconds / 3600) * hourlyRateCents),
210     };
211   });
212 }
213 
214 export interface Totals {
215   seconds: number;
216   billableSeconds: number;
217   entryCount: number;
218   amounts: Array<{ currency: string; cents: number }>;
219 }
220 
221 export function totalsOf(entries: ReportEntry[]): Totals {
222   const amounts = new Map<string, number>();
223   let seconds = 0;
224   let billableSeconds = 0;
225   for (const entry of entries) {
226     seconds += entry.seconds;
227     if (entry.billable) billableSeconds += entry.seconds;
228     if (entry.amountCents !== null) amounts.set(entry.currency, (amounts.get(entry.currency) ?? 0) + entry.amountCents);
229   }
230   return {
231     seconds,
232     billableSeconds,
233     entryCount: entries.length,
234     amounts: [...amounts].map(([currency, cents]) => ({ currency, cents })),
235   };
236 }
237 
238 export type Resolution = 'day' | 'week' | 'month';
239 
240 export function resolutionFor(startDate: string, endDate: string): Resolution {
241   const days = daysBetween(startDate, endDate) + 1;
242   if (days <= 62) return 'day';
243   if (days <= 366) return 'week';
244   return 'month';
245 }
246 
247 export function timeSeries(
248   entries: ReportEntry[], startDate: string, endDate: string, resolution: Resolution, beginningOfWeek: number,
249 ): Array<{ start: string; seconds: number; billableSeconds: number }> {
250   const bucketOf = (key: string): string => {
251     if (resolution === 'day') return key;
252     if (resolution === 'week') return startOfWeek(key, beginningOfWeek);
253     return `${key.slice(0, 7)}-01`;
254   };
255   const buckets = new Map<string, { seconds: number; billableSeconds: number }>();
256   for (let key = bucketOf(startDate); key <= endDate;) {
257     buckets.set(key, { seconds: 0, billableSeconds: 0 });
258     if (resolution === 'day') key = addDays(key, 1);
259     else if (resolution === 'week') key = addDays(key, 7);
260     else {
261       const [y, m] = key.split('-').map(Number) as [number, number];
262       key = m === 12 ? `${y + 1}-01-01` : `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-01`;
263     }
264   }
265   for (const entry of entries) {
266     const bucket = buckets.get(bucketOf(entry.date));
267     if (!bucket) continue;
268     bucket.seconds += entry.seconds;
269     if (entry.billable) bucket.billableSeconds += entry.seconds;
270   }
271   return [...buckets].map(([start, value]) => ({ start, ...value }));
272 }
273 
274 export type WeeklyRows = 'projects' | 'clients';
275 
276 export interface WeeklyGrid {
277   /** One column per day for ranges up to a week, one per week for anything longer. */
278   mode: 'days' | 'weeks';
279   /** Each column's first and last day inside the range. */
280   columns: Array<{ start: string; end: string }>;
281   rows: Array<{ id: number | null; title: string; color: string | null; cells: number[]; total: number }>;
282   columnTotals: number[];
283   total: number;
284 }
285 
286 /**
287  * Totals per project or client and per day or week over every entry passed in. Week columns
288  * start on `beginningOfWeek`; the first and last are clipped to the range.
289  */
290 export function weeklyGrid(
291   entries: ReportEntry[], startDate: string, endDate: string, by: WeeklyRows, beginningOfWeek: number,
292 ): WeeklyGrid {
293   const mode = daysBetween(startDate, endDate) + 1 <= 7 ? 'days' : 'weeks';
294   const columnOf = (key: string): string => (mode === 'days' ? key : startOfWeek(key, beginningOfWeek));
295   const columns: WeeklyGrid['columns'] = [];
296   const index = new Map<string, number>();
297   for (let key = columnOf(startDate); key <= endDate; key = addDays(key, mode === 'days' ? 1 : 7)) {
298     const last = mode === 'days' ? key : addDays(key, 6);
299     index.set(key, columns.length);
300     columns.push({ start: key < startDate ? startDate : key, end: last > endDate ? endDate : last });
301   }
302   const rows = new Map<string, WeeklyGrid['rows'][number]>();
303   const columnTotals = columns.map(() => 0);
304   let total = 0;
305   for (const entry of entries) {
306     const column = index.get(columnOf(entry.date));
307     if (column === undefined) continue;
308     const id = by === 'projects' ? entry.projectId : entry.clientId;
309     let row = rows.get(String(id));
310     if (!row) {
311       row = by === 'projects'
312         ? { id, title: entry.projectName ?? '', color: entry.projectColor, cells: columns.map(() => 0), total: 0 }
313         : { id, title: entry.clientName ?? '', color: null, cells: columns.map(() => 0), total: 0 };
314       rows.set(String(id), row);
315     }
316     row.cells[column]! += entry.seconds;
317     row.total += entry.seconds;
318     columnTotals[column]! += entry.seconds;
319     total += entry.seconds;
320   }
321   // The "without project/client" row goes last; the rest by total, then title.
322   const sorted = [...rows.values()].sort((a, b) =>
323     Number(a.id === null) - Number(b.id === null) || b.total - a.total || a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
324   return { mode, columns, rows: sorted, columnTotals, total };
325 }
326 
327 export type Dimension = 'project' | 'client' | 'tag' | 'description' | 'task' | 'none';
328 
329 export interface GroupKey {
330   id: number | null;
331   title: string;
332   color: string | null;
333 }
334 
335 /** Keys for an entry along a dimension. Tags can yield several keys; an untagged entry yields one null key. */
336 export function keysFor(entry: ReportEntry, dimension: Dimension): GroupKey[] {
337   switch (dimension) {
338     case 'project':
339       return [{ id: entry.projectId, title: entry.projectName ?? '', color: entry.projectColor }];
340     case 'client':
341       return [{ id: entry.clientId, title: entry.clientName ?? '', color: null }];
342     case 'task':
343       return [{ id: entry.taskId, title: entry.taskName ?? '', color: null }];
344     case 'tag':
345       return entry.tags.length ? entry.tags.map((tag) => ({ id: tag.id, title: tag.name, color: null })) : [{ id: null, title: '', color: null }];
346     case 'description':
347       return [{ id: null, title: entry.description, color: null }];
348     case 'none':
349       return [{ id: null, title: '', color: null }];
350   }
351 }
352 
353 export interface Group extends GroupKey {
354   seconds: number;
355   billableSeconds: number;
356   amountCents: number;
357   entryIds: number[];
358   subGroups: Group[];
359 }
360 
361 function groupKeyString(key: GroupKey, dimension: Dimension): string {
362   return dimension === 'description' ? `d:${key.title}` : `i:${key.id ?? 'none'}`;
363 }
364 
365 export function groupEntries(entries: ReportEntry[], dimension: Dimension, subDimension: Dimension = 'none'): Group[] {
366   const groups = new Map<string, { key: GroupKey; entries: ReportEntry[] }>();
367   for (const entry of entries) {
368     for (const key of keysFor(entry, dimension)) {
369       const id = groupKeyString(key, dimension);
370       let group = groups.get(id);
371       if (!group) groups.set(id, (group = { key, entries: [] }));
372       group.entries.push(entry);
373     }
374   }
375   return [...groups.values()]
376     .map(({ key, entries: members }) => {
377       const totals = totalsOf(members);
378       return {
379         ...key,
380         seconds: totals.seconds,
381         billableSeconds: totals.billableSeconds,
382         amountCents: totals.amounts.reduce((sum, amount) => sum + amount.cents, 0),
383         entryIds: members.map((member) => member.id),
384         subGroups: subDimension === 'none' ? [] : groupEntries(members, subDimension),
385       };
386     })
387     .sort((a, b) => b.seconds - a.seconds);
388 }
389 
390 export function parseIdFilter(value: unknown, field: string): IdFilter | undefined {
391   if (value === undefined || value === null) return undefined;
392   if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw badRequest(`Invalid '${field}' value: expected an array`);
393   if (value.length > 1000) throw badRequest(`'${field}' accepts at most 1000 ids`);
394   return value.map((item) => {
395     if (item === null) return null;
396     if (typeof item === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(item) && item > 0) return item;
397     throw badRequest(`Invalid '${field}' value`);
398   });
399 }
400 
401 /** Reads the filter fields shared by Toggl's Reports API v3 bodies and the app's report endpoint. */
402 export function filterFromBody(body: Record<string, unknown>, workspace: WorkspaceRow, maxDays?: number): ReportFilter {
403   const range = validateRange(body.start_date, body.end_date ?? body.start_date, maxDays);
404   if (body.billable !== undefined && body.billable !== null && typeof body.billable !== 'boolean') {
405     throw badRequest("Invalid 'billable' value");
406   }
407   const timeEntryIds = parseIdFilter(body.time_entry_ids, 'time_entry_ids')?.filter((id): id is number => id !== null);
408   const durationBound = (field: string): number | undefined => {
409     const value = body[field];
410     if (value === undefined || value === null) return undefined;
411     if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) throw badRequest(`Invalid '${field}' value`);
412     return value;
413   };
414   const minDurationSeconds = durationBound('min_duration_seconds');
415   const maxDurationSeconds = durationBound('max_duration_seconds');
416   if (minDurationSeconds !== undefined && maxDurationSeconds !== undefined && minDurationSeconds >= maxDurationSeconds) {
417     throw badRequest('max_duration_seconds must be greater than min_duration_seconds');
418   }
419   const rounding = body.rounding === undefined ? undefined : Number(body.rounding);
420   const roundingMinutes = body.rounding_minutes === undefined ? undefined : Number(body.rounding_minutes);
421   if (roundingMinutes !== undefined && !ROUNDING_MINUTES.includes(roundingMinutes)) {
422     throw badRequest(`Invalid 'rounding_minutes' value, allowed values are: '${ROUNDING_MINUTES.join(', ')}'`);
423   }
424   // Toggl applies the workspace rounding only when the request asks for rounding.
425   const applyWorkspaceRounding = rounding !== undefined && roundingMinutes === undefined;
426   return {
427     ...range,
428     projectIds: parseIdFilter(body.project_ids, 'project_ids'),
429     clientIds: parseIdFilter(body.client_ids, 'client_ids'),
430     tagIds: parseIdFilter(body.tag_ids, 'tag_ids'),

48: /**
49:  * The sign-in form and its attempts. Each submit sends one request, and only
50:  * when the user asks: after a 429 the model refuses to submit to that server
51:  * until `Retry-After` has passed, and it never retries on its own. The
52:  * deadline persists in [cooldowns] by server origin, so a model in a new
53:  * process keeps the button disabled too. It goes once it passes or a sign-in
54:  * to that server succeeds.
55:  *
56:  * A plain `http://` server gets nothing until the user confirms the warning
[credential-related line omitted]
58:  * the request, the attempt ends with [SignInResult.CleartextNotConfirmed], and
59:  * [confirmCleartext] records the confirmation in [trust] and sends it again.
60:  * A certificate that no system or user CA trusts ends the attempt with
61:  * [SignInResult.UntrustedCertificate] before any credential is sent, and
62:  * [trustCertificate] pins the certificate the prompt showed and sends it again.
63:  *
64:  * @param trust where the user's answers go. The shared client checks it
65:  *   before every request and in every TLS handshake.
66:  * @param cooldowns where the 429 deadlines persist.
67:  * @param clock wall-clock milliseconds, such as `System.currentTimeMillis`,
68:  *   since a deadline has to outlast the process and a reboot.
69:  * @param scope runs the model's coroutines on one thread, the main thread in
70:  *   the app.
71:  * @param io where the blocking [SignIn.attempt] and the writes to [trust] and
72:  *   [cooldowns] run.
73:  */
74: class SignInModel(
75:     private val signIn: SignIn,
76:     private val trust: ServerTrust,
77:     private val cooldowns: SignInCooldowns,
78:     private val scope: CoroutineScope,
79:     private val io: CoroutineContext,
80:     private val clock: () -> Long,
81: ) {
82: 
83:     private val mutableState = MutableStateFlow(SignInState())
84:     val state: StateFlow<SignInState> = mutableState.asStateFlow()
85: 
86:     /** The 429 deadlines by server origin. Used on [scope]'s thread only. */
87:     private val deadlines = mutableMapOf<String, Long>()
88: 
89:     /** Keeps the writes to [cooldowns] in the order the model makes them. */
90:     private val cooldownWrites = Mutex()
91: 
92:     /** Reads the stored deadlines and drops the ones that have passed. Submit waits for it. */
93:     private val restoring: Job = scope.launch {
94:         val stored = withContext(io) { cooldowns.load() }
95:         val now = clock()
96:         for ((origin, deadline) in stored) {
97:             if (deadline > now) deadlines[origin] = deadline else forget(origin)
98:         }
99:         mutableState.update { it.copy(retryAt = deadlineFor(it.serverUrl)) }
100:     }
101: 
102:     fun onServerUrlChange(value: String) = edit(InputError.SERVER_URL) { copy(serverUrl = value, retryAt = deadlineFor(value)) }
103: 
104:     fun onMethodChange(value: SignInMethod) = mutableState.update { it.copy(method = value, inputErrors = emptySet()) }
105: 
[credential-related line omitted]
107: 
108:     fun onEmailChange(value: String) = edit(InputError.EMAIL, InputError.EMAIL_COLON) { copy(email = value) }
109: 
[credential-related line omitted]
111: 
112:     /** Whole seconds until sign-in opens again after a 429, or 0 when it is open. */
113:     fun secondsUntilRetry(): Long {
114:         val retryAt = mutableState.value.retryAt ?: return 0
115:         val left = retryAt - clock()
116:         return if (left <= 0) 0 else (left + MILLIS_PER_SECOND - 1) / MILLIS_PER_SECOND
117:     }
118: 
119:     /** Validates the form and, when it is complete and nothing blocks it, sends one sign-in request. */
120:     fun submit() {
121:         val current = mutableState.value
122:         if (current.submitting || current.signedIn || secondsUntilRetry() > 0) return
123:         val server = ServerUrl.parse(current.serverUrl.trim())
124:         val credentials = credentials(current)
125:         val errors = inputErrors(current, server)
126:         if (errors.isNotEmpty() || server == null || credentials == null) {
127:             mutableState.update { it.copy(inputErrors = errors) }
128:             return
129:         }
130:         mutableState.update { it.copy(inputErrors = emptySet(), submitting = true, failure = null, retryAt = null) }
131:         scope.launch {
132:             // A deadline from an earlier process may still be loading.
133:             restoring.join()
134:             val stored = deadlines[server.origin]
135:             if (stored != null && stored > clock()) {
136:                 mutableState.update { it.copy(submitting = false, retryAt = stored) }
137:                 return@launch
138:             }
139:             if (stored != null) forget(server.origin)
140:             val result = withContext(io) { signIn.attempt(server, credentials) }
141:             val retryAt = when (result) {
142:                 is SignInResult.RateLimited ->
143:                     (clock() + (result.retryAfterSeconds ?: DEFAULT_RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS) * MILLIS_PER_SECOND)
144:                         .also { remember(server.origin, it) }
145:                 SignInResult.SignedIn -> null.also { forget(server.origin) }
146:                 else -> null
147:             }
148:             mutableState.update { it.after(result, retryAt) }
149:         }
150:     }
151: 
152:     /**
153:      * The countdown reached zero. Forgets every deadline that has passed, on
154:      * the phone too.
155:      */
156:     fun cooldownEnded() {
157:         val now = clock()
158:         deadlines.filterValues { it <= now }.keys.forEach(::forget)
159:         mutableState.update { it.copy(retryAt = deadlineFor(it.serverUrl)) }
160:     }
161: 
162:     /**
163:      * The user confirmed the warning about a plain `http://` server. Records
164:      * the confirmation for that URL, then sends the attempt.
165:      */
166:     fun confirmCleartext() {
167:         val warning = mutableState.value.failure as? SignInResult.CleartextNotConfirmed ?: return
168:         storeAnswerThenSubmit { trust.confirmCleartext(warning.server) }
169:     }
170: 
171:     /**
172:      * The user trusts the certificate that the prompt showed. Pins exactly
173:      * that certificate, by its SHA-256, then sends the attempt. A server that
174:      * now presents another certificate fails the handshake and prompts again.
175:      */
176:     fun trustCertificate() {
177:         val prompt = mutableState.value.failure as? SignInResult.UntrustedCertificate ?: return
178:         storeAnswerThenSubmit { trust.pinCertificate(prompt.certificate) }
179:     }
180: 
181:     /** Closes a warning or prompt without sending or storing anything. */
182:     fun dismissPrompt() {
183:         mutableState.update { if (it.failure.isPrompt()) it.copy(failure = null) else it }
184:     }
185: 
186:     private fun storeAnswerThenSubmit(store: () -> Unit) {
187:         if (mutableState.value.submitting) return
188:         mutableState.update { it.copy(submitting = true, failure = null) }
189:         scope.launch {
190:             val stored = withContext(io) {
191:                 try {
192:                     store()
193:                     true
194:                 } catch (e: IOException) {
195:                     false
196:                 }
197:             }
198:             mutableState.update { it.copy(submitting = false, failure = SignInResult.AnswerNotStored.takeUnless { stored }) }
199:             if (stored) submit()
200:         }
201:     }
202: 
203:     private fun SignInResult?.isPrompt(): Boolean =
204:         this is SignInResult.CleartextNotConfirmed || this is SignInResult.UntrustedCertificate
205: 
206:     /** Clears the form after the app has moved on from a successful sign-in. */
207:     fun reset() {
208:         mutableState.value = SignInState()
209:     }
210: 
211:     private fun SignInState.after(result: SignInResult, retryAt: Long?): SignInState =
212:         when (result) {
213:             // Drops every secret the form held.
214:             SignInResult.SignedIn -> SignInState(signedIn = true)
215:             else -> copy(submitting = false, failure = result, retryAt = retryAt)
216:         }
217: 
218:     /** The deadline for the server in the form, or null when none blocks it. */
219:     private fun deadlineFor(serverUrl: String): Long? =
220:         ServerUrl.parse(serverUrl.trim())?.let { deadlines[it.origin] }
221: 
222:     private fun remember(origin: String, deadline: Long) {
223:         deadlines[origin] = deadline
224:         writeCooldowns { save(origin, deadline) }
225:     }
226: 
227:     private fun forget(origin: String) {
228:         deadlines.remove(origin)
229:         writeCooldowns { clear(origin) }
230:     }
231: 
232:     /**
233:      * Writes to [cooldowns] off the main thread, one at a time and in call
234:      * order: each call takes the lock before it first suspends. A failed
235:      * write leaves the deadline in memory, so this process still waits.
236:      */
237:     private fun writeCooldowns(write: SignInCooldowns.() -> Unit) {
238:         scope.launch(start = CoroutineStart.UNDISPATCHED) {
239:             cooldownWrites.withLock {
240:                 withContext(io) {
241:                     try {
242:                         cooldowns.write()
243:                     } catch (e: IOException) {
244:                         // Nothing to show: the deadline still holds until the process ends.
245:                     }
246:                 }
247:             }
248:         }
249:     }
250: 
251:     private fun edit(vararg clears: InputError, change: SignInState.() -> SignInState) =
252:         mutableState.update { it.change().copy(inputErrors = it.inputErrors - clears.toSet()) }
253: 
254:     private fun inputErrors(state: SignInState, server: ServerUrl?): Set<InputError> = buildSet {
255:         if (server == null) add(InputError.SERVER_URL)
256:         when (state.method) {

[credential-related line omitted]
259:                 val email = state.email.trim()
260:                 if (email.isEmpty()) add(InputError.EMAIL)
261:                 // Basic auth ends the user name at the first colon.
262:                 if (':' in email) add(InputError.EMAIL_COLON)

264:             }
265:         }
266:     }

11: /**
12:  * Keeps the sign-in form, and an attempt in flight, across configuration
13:  * changes. The secrets stay in this object's memory: nothing here goes into
14:  * saved instance state.
15:  */
16: class SignInViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
17: 
18:     val model = SignInModel(
19:         // Runs on the IO thread, since the stores touch the disk. The settings go first. If the
20:         // session write then fails, the app stays signed out and the next sign-in replaces them.
21:         signIn = SignIn(
22:             session = { server, token, settings ->
23:                 (application as TokklApplication).accountSettings.store(settings)
24:                 Session(application).save(server, token)
25:             },
26:         ),
27:         trust = ServerTrustStore(application),
28:         cooldowns = SignInCooldownStore(application),
29:         scope = viewModelScope,
30:         io = Dispatchers.IO,

8: import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
9: import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
10: import okhttp3.Request
11: import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
12: import okhttp3.Response

14: /**
15:  * Client for the Toggl Track API v9 and Reports API v3, as served by Tokkl or
16:  * by Toggl Track. Build endpoint URLs with [server], then call [get], [send],
17:  * [sendWithHeaders] or [sendNoContent]. Calls block the calling thread, so
18:  * make them off the main thread.
19:  */
20: class TogglApiClient(
21:     val server: ServerUrl,
22:     private val credentials: ApiCredentials,
23:     private val httpClient: OkHttpClient = SharedHttpClient.instance,
24:     private val trustListener: TrustListener? = null,
25: ) {
26: 
27:     fun <T> get(url: HttpUrl, deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>): ApiResult<T> =
28:         send("GET", url, body = null, deserializer)
29: 
30:     /**
31:      * Sends [body] as JSON, or no body when it is null, and decodes the 2xx
32:      * response body. An empty body does not decode, so use [sendNoContent]
33:      * for calls that answer without one. OkHttp refuses a null body for POST,
34:      * PUT and PATCH.
35:      */
36:     fun <T> send(
37:         method: String,
38:         url: HttpUrl,
39:         body: JsonElement?,
40:         deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>,
41:     ): ApiResult<T> = execute(method, url, body) { decode(it.body.string(), deserializer) }
42: 
43:     /**
44:      * Like [send], and keeps the response's headers too, for calls that put
45:      * values in them, such as the Reports API's `X-Next-Row-Number`, the row
46:      * the next page of a detailed report starts at.
47:      */
48:     fun <T> sendWithHeaders(
49:         method: String,
50:         url: HttpUrl,
51:         body: JsonElement?,
52:         deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>,
53:     ): ApiResult<WithHeaders<T>> = execute(method, url, body) { response ->
54:         when (val decoded = decode(response.body.string(), deserializer)) {
55:             is ApiResult.Success -> ApiResult.Success(WithHeaders(decoded.value, response.headers))
56:             is ApiResult.Failure -> decoded
57:         }
58:     }
59: 
60:     /**
61:      * Like [send], for calls whose response body the caller does not need,
62:      * such as Tokkl's DELETE of a time entry, task, client or tag, which
63:      * answer 200 with an empty body. Tokkl's project DELETE answers with the
64:      * project id as JSON, which this method ignores too. Any 2xx is a success.
65:      */
66:     fun sendNoContent(method: String, url: HttpUrl, body: JsonElement? = null): ApiResult<Unit> =
67:         execute(method, url, body) { ApiResult.Success(Unit) }
68: 
69:     private fun <T> execute(
70:         method: String,
71:         url: HttpUrl,
72:         body: JsonElement?,
73:         onSuccess: (Response) -> ApiResult<T>,
74:     ): ApiResult<T> {
75:         val request = Request.Builder()
76:             .url(url)
[credential-related line omitted]
78:             .method(method, body?.let { ApiJson.encodeToString(JsonElement.serializer(), it).toRequestBody(JSON) })
79:             .build()
80:         return try {
81:             httpClient.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
82:                 // The server answered, so it passed the trust checks.
83:                 trustListener?.answered()
84:                 if (response.isSuccessful) onSuccess(response) else ApiResult.Failure(httpError(response))
85:             }
86:         } catch (e: HostNotAllowedException) {
87:             ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.HostNotAllowed(e.origin))
88:         } catch (e: CleartextNotConfirmedException) {
89:             refused(ApiError.CleartextNotConfirmed(e.server))
90:         } catch (e: IOException) {
91:             val untrusted = e.untrustedCertificate()
92:             when {
93:                 untrusted != null -> refused(ApiError.UntrustedCertificate(untrusted.certificate, changed = untrusted.pinChanged))
94:                 e.certificateHostMismatch() -> refused(ApiError.CertificateHostMismatch(url.host))
95:                 else -> ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.Network(e))
96:             }
97:         }
98:     }
99: 
100:     private fun refused(refusal: ApiError.TrustRefused): ApiResult.Failure {
101:         trustListener?.refused(refusal)
102:         return ApiResult.Failure(refusal)
103:     }
104: 
105:     private fun httpError(response: Response): ApiError.Http =
106:         when (response.code) {
107:             403 -> ApiError.AuthFailed
108:             429 -> ApiError.RateLimited(retryAfterSeconds(response.header("Retry-After")))
109:             else -> ApiError.UnexpectedStatus(response.code)
110:         }
111: 
112:     private fun <T> decode(text: String, deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>): ApiResult<T> =
113:         try {
114:             ApiResult.Success(ApiJson.decodeFromString(deserializer, text))
115:         } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
116:             // kotlinx.serialization's SerializationException is an IllegalArgumentException.
117:             ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.InvalidResponse(e))
118:         }
119: 
120:     private companion object {
121:         val JSON = "application/json; charset=utf-8".toMediaType()
122: 
123:         /** Tokkl sends whole seconds. An HTTP date or a negative number counts as missing. */
124:         fun retryAfterSeconds(value: String?): Long? = value?.trim()?.toLongOrNull()?.takeIf { it >= 0 }
125:     }
126: }
127: 
128: /** A decoded response body and the headers it came with. */
129: class WithHeaders<out T>(val value: T, val headers: Headers)
130: 
131: /**
132:  * Hears how the signed-in session's requests fare with the trust checks, so
133:  * the app can show the matching prompt from any screen. Called on the thread
134:  * that made the request.
135:  */
136: interface TrustListener {
137: 
138:     /** A request was refused for [refusal] before anything was sent. */
139:     fun refused(refusal: ApiError.TrustRefused)
140: 
141:     /** The server answered a request, so it passes the trust checks again. */
142:     fun answered()
143: }
app/src/main/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/session/AccountSettingsSource.kt
1: package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session
2: 
3: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ApiResult
4: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.TogglApiClient
5: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time.DurationFormat
6: import java.io.IOException
7: import kotlin.coroutines.CoroutineContext
8: import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
9: import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineStart
10: import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
11: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
12: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
13: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
14: import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
15: import kotlinx.serialization.json.JsonObject
16: 
17: /**
18:  * The account settings for the whole process, and the one place screens read them from.
19:  * Sign-in stores the settings from its own `GET /api/v9/me`, and [refresh] reads them again, so
20:  * a change made in the web app reaches the phone. They persist, so screens have them offline and
21:  * after a restart. Screens take their zone, clock and week start from [AccountSettings.time],
22:  * never from the device, and write durations with [AccountSettings.formatDuration].
23:  *
24:  * @param initial the stored settings, or null when none are stored.
25:  * @param save writes the settings, throwing [IOException] when it cannot.
26:  * @param client the API client of the signed-in account, or null when signed out. Runs on [io].
27:  * @param scope outlives every screen, so leaving the app does not cancel a refresh.
28:  * @param onRefresh runs on [io] after each refresh whose `GET /api/v9/me` answered, inside the
29:  *   same job, with that client and answer, and the refresh's `GET /api/v9/me/preferences`, null
30:  *   when that request failed. The capabilities and the cache refresh there, so one `/me` and one
31:  *   `/me/preferences` serve them all, and a [refresh] call while it runs joins it. It does not
32:  *   run when sign-in stored newer settings while the requests ran.
33:  */
34: class AccountSettingsSource(
35:     initial: AccountSettings?,
36:     private val save: (AccountSettings) -> Unit,
37:     private val client: () -> TogglApiClient?,
38:     private val scope: CoroutineScope,
39:     private val io: CoroutineContext,
40:     private val onRefresh: (TogglApiClient, Me, MePreferences?) -> Unit = { _, _, _ -> },
41: ) {
42: 
43:     private val mutableState = MutableStateFlow(initial)
44: 
45:     /** The stored settings, or null when none are stored yet. */
46:     val state: StateFlow<AccountSettings?> = mutableState.asStateFlow()
47: 
48:     /** Guards [writes], [refreshing], and each write together with the state it sets. */
49:     private val lock = Any()
50: 
51:     /** Counts the writes, so a refresh drops an answer that a newer write has replaced. */
52:     private var writes = 0L
53: 
54:     private var refreshing: Job? = null
55: 
56:     /**
57:      * Writes [settings] and shows them in [state]. Sign-in calls this with the settings from its
58:      * own `GET /api/v9/me`. Blocks on disk I/O, so call it off the main thread.
59:      */
60:     @Throws(IOException::class)
61:     fun store(settings: AccountSettings) {
62:         synchronized(lock) { write(settings) }
63:     }
64: 
65:     /**
66:      * Reads `GET /api/v9/me` for the signed-in account, with its related data for the cache, and
67:      * `GET /api/v9/me/preferences` for the duration format, and keeps the settings. Does nothing
68:      * when signed out. A failed `/me` or write keeps what is stored, and failed preferences keep
69:      * the stored duration format. While one refresh runs, another call returns that one instead
70:      * of sending a second request.
71:      */
72:     fun refresh(): Job {
73:         val job = synchronized(lock) {
74:             refreshing?.takeUnless { it.isCompleted }
75:                 ?: scope.launch(io, start = CoroutineStart.LAZY) { refreshNow() }.also { refreshing = it }
76:         }
77:         job.start()
78:         return job
79:     }
80: 
81:     private fun refreshNow() {
82:         val writesBefore = synchronized(lock) { writes }
83:         val api = client() ?: return
84:         val url = api.server.api("me").newBuilder().addQueryParameter("with_related_data", "true").build()
85:         val me = (api.get(url, Me.serializer()) as? ApiResult.Success)?.value ?: return
86:         val fromMe = me.accountSettings() ?: return
87:         val preferences = readPreferences(api)
88:         synchronized(lock) {
89:             // Sign-in stored newer settings while the requests ran.
90:             if (writes != writesBefore) return
91:             val settings = fromMe.copy(
92:                 durationFormat = preferences?.durationFormat ?: mutableState.value?.durationFormat ?: DurationFormat.IMPROVED,
93:             )
94:             if (settings != mutableState.value) {
95:                 try {
96:                     write(settings)
97:                 } catch (e: IOException) {
98:                     // Keeps the stored settings until the next refresh.
99:                 }
100:             }
101:         }
102:         onRefresh(api, me, preferences)
103:     }
104: 
105:     /** The account's preferences, or null when the request fails. */
106:     private fun readPreferences(api: TogglApiClient): MePreferences? {
107:         val answer = api.get(api.server.api("me", "preferences"), JsonObject.serializer())
108:         return (answer as? ApiResult.Success)?.value?.let(::MePreferences)
109:     }
110: 
111:     /** Only while holding [lock]. */
112:     private fun write(settings: AccountSettings) {
113:         save(settings)
114:         writes++
115:         mutableState.value = settings
116:     }
117: }

../tokkl/packages/web/src/pages/reports/format.ts
import type { Grouping, Report } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { formatMoney } from '../../lib/settings.ts';

export const GROUPING_LABELS: Record<Grouping, string> = {
  projects: 'Project',
  clients: 'Client',
  tags: 'Tag',
  time_entries: 'Description',
  tasks: 'Task',
};

const EMPTY_TITLES: Record<Grouping, string> = {
  projects: 'Without project',
  clients: 'Without client',
  tags: 'Without tags',
  time_entries: 'Without description',
  tasks: 'Without task',
};

export function groupTitle(title: string, grouping: Grouping): string {
  return title.trim() === '' ? EMPTY_TITLES[grouping] : title;
}

/**
 * The single currency amounts can be shown in. Group amounts from the server are plain sums of
 * cents, so they only mean something when every amount in the report shares one currency.
 */
export function reportCurrency(report: Report): string | null {
  const [first, ...rest] = report.totals.amounts;
  return first && rest.length === 0 ? first.currency : null;
}

export function money(cents: number | null, currency: string | null): string {
  return cents === null || currency === null ? '' : formatMoney(cents, currency);
}

export function percent(part: number, whole: number): number {
  return whole > 0 ? (part / whole) * 100 : 0;
}

const percentFormat = new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 1, minimumFractionDigits: 1 });

export function formatPercent(value: number): string {
  return `${percentFormat.format(value)}%`;
}
