app/src/test/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/api/ServerUrlTest.kt
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class ServerUrlTest {

    @Test
    fun hostRootWithAndWithoutTrailingSlash() {
        for (base in listOf("https://example.com", "https://example.com/")) {
            val server = parse(base)
            assertEquals(base, "https://example.com/api/v9/me", server.api("me").toString())
            assertEquals(
                base,
                "https://example.com/reports/api/v3/workspace/1/search/time_entries",
                server.reports("workspace", "1", "search", "time_entries").toString(),
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun basePathWithAndWithoutTrailingSlash() {
        for (base in listOf("https://example.com/tokkl", "https://example.com/tokkl/", "https://example.com/tokkl//")) {
            val server = parse(base)
            assertEquals(base, "https://example.com/tokkl/api/v9/me", server.api("me").toString())
            assertEquals(
                base,
                "https://example.com/tokkl/reports/api/v3/workspace/1/search/time_entries",
                server.reports("workspace", "1", "search", "time_entries").toString(),
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun deeperPathPortAndPlainHttp() {
        val server = parse("http://100.64.0.1:8080/apps/tokkl/")
        assertEquals(
            "http://100.64.0.1:8080/apps/tokkl/api/v9/workspaces/7/time_entries",
            server.api("workspaces", "7", "time_entries").toString(),
        )
        assertEquals(
            "http://100.64.0.1:8080/apps/tokkl/reports/api/v3/workspace/7/summary/time_entries",
            server.reports("workspace", "7", "summary", "time_entries").toString(),
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun emptySegmentsInsideTheBasePathAreDropped() {
        assertEquals("https://example.com/a/b/api/v9/me", parse("https://example.com//a//b/").api("me").toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun encodedBasePathIsKept() {
        assertEquals(
            "https://example.com/my%20tokkl/api/v9/me",
            parse("https://example.com/my%20tokkl/").api("me").toString(),
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun endpointSegmentsCannotAddPathOrQuery() {
        val url = parse("https://example.com/tokkl").api("me/../../admin?x=1")
        assertEquals("https://example.com/tokkl/api/v9/me%2F..%2F..%2Fadmin%3Fx=1", url.toString())
        assertNull(url.query)
    }

    @Test
    fun noEndpointSegmentsGivesTheApiRoot() {
        assertEquals("https://example.com/tokkl/api/v9", parse("https://example.com/tokkl/").api().toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun sameServerWithOrWithoutTrailingSlash() {
        assertEquals(parse("https://example.com/tokkl"), parse("https://example.com/tokkl/"))
        assertEquals("https://example.com/tokkl", parse("https://example.com/tokkl/").toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsWhatIsNotAnHttpBaseUrl() {
        val bad = listOf(
            "", "example.com", "ftp://example.com", "https://",
            "https://example.com/tokkl?x=1", "https://example.com/#top",
[credential-pattern line omitted]
        )
        for (url in bad) {
            assertNull(url, ServerUrl.parse(url))
        }
    }

    private fun parse(url: String): ServerUrl = ServerUrl.parse(url) ?: error("$url did not parse")
}

app/src/test/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/api/TogglApiClientTest.kt
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

import java.net.SocketTimeoutException
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.json.buildJsonObject
import kotlinx.serialization.json.put
import mockwebserver3.MockResponse
import mockwebserver3.MockWebServer
import mockwebserver3.SocketEffect
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test

class TogglApiClientTest {

    @Serializable
    private data class Me(val id: Long, val email: String)

    private val server = MockWebServer()
[credential-related line omitted]
    private lateinit var client: TogglApiClient

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        server.start()
        val configured = serverUrl()
        // The shared client sends requests only to the configured server.
        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { configured }
        // The user has confirmed the MockWebServer's plain http URL.
        ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(InMemoryServerTrust(configured))
        client = TogglApiClient(configured, credentials)
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { null }
        ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(InMemoryServerTrust())
        server.close()
    }

    @Test
    fun getSendsBasicAuthToThePathBearingUrlAndDecodesJson() {
        server.enqueue(json(200, """{"id":42,"email":"ana@example.com","role":"admin","time_resolution_minutes":5}"""))

        val result = client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())

        assertEquals(ApiResult.Success(Me(42, "ana@example.com")), result)
        val request = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("GET", request.method)
        assertEquals("/tokkl/api/v9/me", request.url.encodedPath)
[credential-related line omitted]
    }

    @Test
    fun sendPostsJsonToReports() {
        server.enqueue(json(200, """{"id":1,"email":"ana@example.com"}"""))
        val body = buildJsonObject { put("start_date", "2026-10-01") }

        client.send("POST", client.server.reports("workspace", "1", "search", "time_entries"), body, Me.serializer())

        val request = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("POST", request.method)
        assertEquals("/tokkl/reports/api/v3/workspace/1/search/time_entries", request.url.encodedPath)
        assertEquals("application/json; charset=utf-8", request.headers["Content-Type"])
        assertEquals("""{"start_date":"2026-10-01"}""", request.body?.utf8())
    }

    @Test
    fun status403IsAnAuthFailure() {
[credential-related line omitted]

        val error = failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer()))

        assertEquals(ApiError.AuthFailed, error)
        assertEquals(403, (error as ApiError.Http).status)
    }

    @Test
    fun status429CarriesRetryAfterSeconds() {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(429).addHeader("Retry-After", "37").build())

        assertEquals(ApiError.RateLimited(37), failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())))
    }

    @Test
    fun status429WithoutUsableRetryAfterHasNoSeconds() {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(429).build())
        server.enqueue(
            MockResponse.Builder().code(429).addHeader("Retry-After", "Wed, 21 Oct 2026 07:28:00 GMT").build(),
        )

        repeat(2) {
            assertEquals(ApiError.RateLimited(null), failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())))
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun otherStatusesKeepTheirCode() {
        server.enqueue(json(500, "{}"))

        assertEquals(ApiError.UnexpectedStatus(500), failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())))
    }

    @Test
    fun malformedBodyIsAnInvalidResponse() {
        server.enqueue(json(200, """{"id":"not a number"}"""))

        assertTrue(failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())) is ApiError.InvalidResponse)
    }

    @Test
    fun emptyBodyOnADecodingCallIsAnInvalidResponse() {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(200).build())

        assertTrue(failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())) is ApiError.InvalidResponse)
    }

    @Test
    fun noContentCallSucceedsOn200WithAnEmptyBody() {
        // Tokkl's DELETE routes answer this way.
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(200).build())

        val result = client.sendNoContent("DELETE", client.server.api("workspaces", "1", "time_entries", "2"))

        assertEquals(ApiResult.Success(Unit), result)
        val request = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("DELETE", request.method)
        assertEquals("/tokkl/api/v9/workspaces/1/time_entries/2", request.url.encodedPath)
[credential-related line omitted]
    }

    @Test
    fun noContentCallSucceedsOn204() {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(204).build())

        assertEquals(ApiResult.Success(Unit), client.sendNoContent("DELETE", client.server.api("me")))
    }

    @Test
    fun noContentCallKeepsTheHttpErrorMappings() {
[credential-related line omitted]
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(429).addHeader("Retry-After", "5").build())
        server.enqueue(json(404, "\"Not found\""))
        val url = client.server.api("workspaces", "1", "time_entries", "2")

        assertEquals(ApiError.AuthFailed, failure(client.sendNoContent("DELETE", url)))
        assertEquals(ApiError.RateLimited(5), failure(client.sendNoContent("DELETE", url)))
        assertEquals(ApiError.UnexpectedStatus(404), failure(client.sendNoContent("DELETE", url)))
    }

    @Test
    fun noContentCallReportsANetworkFailure() {
        val url = serverUrl()
        server.close()

        val error = failure(TogglApiClient(url, credentials).sendNoContent("DELETE", url.api("me")))

        assertTrue(error.toString(), error is ApiError.Network)
    }

    @Test
    fun refusedConnectionIsANetworkError() {
        val url = serverUrl()
        server.close()

        val error = failure(TogglApiClient(url, credentials).get(url.api("me"), Me.serializer()))

        assertTrue(error.toString(), error is ApiError.Network)
        assertFalse(error is ApiError.Http)
    }

    @Test
    fun droppedConnectionIsANetworkError() {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().onRequestStart(SocketEffect.CloseSocket()).build())

        val error = failure(client.get(client.server.api("me"), Me.serializer()))

        assertTrue(error.toString(), error is ApiError.Network)
    }

    @Test
    fun timeoutIsANetworkError() {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().onResponseStart(SocketEffect.Stall).build())
        // Same settings as the shared client, with a read timeout short enough for a test.
        val impatient = SharedHttpClient.instance.newBuilder().readTimeout(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS).build()
        val url = serverUrl()

        val error = failure(TogglApiClient(url, credentials, impatient).get(url.api("me"), Me.serializer()))

        assertTrue(error.toString(), error is ApiError.Network && error.cause is SocketTimeoutException)
    }

    private fun serverUrl(): ServerUrl = ServerUrl.parse(server.url("/tokkl/").toString())!!

    private fun json(code: Int, body: String) = MockResponse.Builder()
        .code(code)
        .addHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
        .body(body)
        .build()

    private fun failure(result: ApiResult<*>): ApiError =
        (result as? ApiResult.Failure)?.error ?: error("expected a failure, got $result")
}

app/src/test/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/api/ConfiguredServerGuardTest.kt

/** The shared client sends requests, redirects included, only to the configured server. */
class ConfiguredServerGuardTest {

    @Serializable
    private data class Me(val id: Long)

    private val configured = MockWebServer()
    private val other = MockWebServer()
[credential-related line omitted]
    private lateinit var server: ServerUrl
    private lateinit var trust: InMemoryServerTrust
    private lateinit var client: TogglApiClient

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        configured.start()
        other.start()
        server = ServerUrl.parse(configured.url("/tokkl/").toString())!!
        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { server }
        // The user has confirmed the MockWebServer's plain http URL.
        trust = InMemoryServerTrust(server)
        ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(trust)
        client = TogglApiClient(server, credentials)
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { null }
        ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(InMemoryServerTrust())
        configured.close()
        other.close()
    }

    @Test
    fun requestToTheConfiguredServerPasses() {
        configured.enqueue(me())

        assertEquals(ApiResult.Success(Me(7)), client.get(server.api("me"), Me.serializer()))
        assertEquals(1, configured.requestCount)
    }

    @Test
    fun requestToAnotherPortFailsWithTheOriginAndSendsNothing() {
        val url = other.url("/api/v9/me")

        val result = client.get(url, Me.serializer())

        assertEquals(ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.HostNotAllowed(origin(url))), result)
        assertEquals(0, other.requestCount)
    }

    @Test
    fun requestToAnotherHostFailsWithTheHostName() {
        val otherHost = if (configured.hostName == "127.0.0.1") "localhost" else "127.0.0.1"
        val url = server.api("me").newBuilder().host(otherHost).build()

        val error = failure(client.get(url, Me.serializer()))

        assertEquals(ApiError.HostNotAllowed("http://$otherHost:${configured.port}"), error)
        assertEquals(0, configured.requestCount)
    }

    @Test
    fun requestWithAnotherSchemeFails() {
        val url = server.api("me").newBuilder().scheme("https").port(configured.port).build()

        assertEquals(ApiError.HostNotAllowed(origin(url)), failure(client.get(url, Me.serializer())))
        assertEquals(0, configured.requestCount)
    }

    @Test

        configured.enqueue(me())
        configured.enqueue(MockResponse.Builder().code(307).addHeader("Location", "/tokkl/again").build())
        configured.enqueue(me())

        client.send("POST", server.api("tags"), body, Me.serializer())
        client.send("POST", server.api("tags"), body, Me.serializer())

        val requests = List(4) { configured.takeRequest() }
        assertEquals(listOf("POST", "GET", "POST", "POST"), requests.map { it.method })
        assertEquals(0L, requests[1].bodySize)
        assertEquals("""{"name":"x"}""", requests[3].body?.utf8())
    }

    @Test

        assertEquals(0, configured.requestCount)
    }

    @Test

        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { null }

        val after = client.get(server.api("me"), Me.serializer())

        assertEquals(ApiResult.Success(Me(7)), during)
        assertEquals(ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.HostNotAllowed(origin(server.api("me")))), after)

    }

app/src/test/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/api/InMemoryServerTrust.kt
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

/** The user's trust decisions in memory, for tests. Starts with [confirmed] already confirmed. */
class InMemoryServerTrust(vararg confirmed: ServerUrl) : ServerTrust {

    val cleartextConfirmed: MutableSet<ServerUrl> = confirmed.toMutableSet()

    /** Pinned SHA-256 fingerprints by `host:port`. */
    val pins: MutableMap<String, String> = mutableMapOf()

    override fun isCleartextConfirmed(server: ServerUrl): Boolean = server in cleartextConfirmed

    override fun confirmCleartext(server: ServerUrl) {
        cleartextConfirmed += server
    }

    override fun pinnedSha256(host: String, port: Int): String? = pins["$host:$port"]

    override fun pinCertificate(certificate: ServerCertificate) {
        pins["${certificate.host}:${certificate.port}"] = certificate.sha256
    }
}

18: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.trust.SessionTrustModel
19: import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
20: import kotlinx.coroutines.MainScope
21: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
22: 
23: class TokklApplication : Application() {
24: 
25:     /**
26:      * Loaded on first use, which is MainActivity's onCreate, on the main thread. The first frame
27:      * has to know the theme, or launch would show the other mode's colours first. Android learns
28:      * the chosen mode too, for the starting window of the next launch.
29:      */
30:     val themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel by lazy {
31:         val store = ThemeSettingsStore(this)
32:         val scope = MainScope()
33:         ThemeSettingsModel(initial = store.load(), save = store::save, scope = scope, io = Dispatchers.IO).also { model ->
34:             syncApplicationNightMode(this, model.state.map { it.mode }, scope, Dispatchers.IO)
35:         }
36:     }
37: 
38:     /**
39:      * Loaded on first use, which reads the stored settings from disk. That is usually
40:      * MainActivity's onStart on the main thread, and the file holds four short values. Each
41:      * refresh refreshes the [capabilities] and the [cache] too, with the same `GET /api/v9/me`,
42:      * and the cache takes the favorites from the settings' `GET /api/v9/me/preferences`.
43:      */
44:     val accountSettings: AccountSettingsSource by lazy {
45:         val store = AccountSettingsStore(this)
46:         AccountSettingsSource(
47:             initial = store.load(),
48:             save = store::save,
49:             client = Session(this)::client,
50:             scope = MainScope(),
51:             io = Dispatchers.IO,
52:             onRefresh = { api, me, preferences -> cache.refresh(api, me, preferences, capabilities.refresh(api, me)) },
53:         )
54:     }
55: 
56:     /** What the server offers. Loaded on first use from a file of four flags. */
57:     val capabilities: CapabilitiesSource by lazy {
58:         val store = CapabilitiesStore(this)
59:         CapabilitiesSource(initial = store.load(), save = store::save)
60:     }
61: 
62:     /** The offline cache. Its database file opens on the first query, which Room keeps off the main thread. */
63:     val cache: AccountCache by lazy { AccountCache(CacheDatabase.open(this)) }
64: 
65:     /**
66:      * The trust refusals of the signed-in session's requests, which every
67:      * [Session] reports and the navigation host prompts about. Accepting one
68:      * refreshes the account settings, the request that ran into it.
69:      */
70:     val sessionTrust: SessionTrustModel by lazy {
71:         SessionTrustModel(
72:             trust = ServerTrustStore(this),
73:             retry = { accountSettings.refresh() },
74:             scope = MainScope(),
75:             io = Dispatchers.IO,
76:         )
77:     }
78: 
79:     override fun onCreate() {
80:         super.onCreate()
81:         // Every process, whatever starts it, sends requests only to the stored server.
82:         ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom(ServerConfigStore(this)::load)
83:         // A plain http:// server gets requests only once the user has confirmed it.
84:         ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(ServerTrustStore(this))
85:     }
86: }

27: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.trust.TrustPrompt

29: import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
30: import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
31: 
32: /** The app's screens. */
33: enum class Destination { SIGN_IN, HOME, SETTINGS, LICENCES, REPORTS }
34: 
35: /**
36:  * The app's navigation host: a back stack of [Destination]s that survives
37:  * configuration changes and process death. It starts at sign-in until a
38:  * server and its token are stored, and at the home screen after that.
39:  * System back pops the stack and closes the app from its first screen.
40:  *
41:  * Above any screen of a signed-in session it shows the prompt for a request
42:  * that [sessionTrust] saw refused, and after a cancel a notice that opens the
43:  * prompt again. Sign-in shows its own prompts.
44:  */
45: @Composable
46: fun TokklNavHost(
47:     signInModel: SignInModel,
48:     themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel,
49:     sessionTrust: SessionTrustModel,
50:     reports: ReportsModel,
51: ) {
52:     val appContext = LocalContext.current.applicationContext
53:     val backStack = rememberSaveable(saver = BackStackSaver) { SnapshotStateList<Destination>() }
54:     if (backStack.isEmpty()) {
55:         // Shows the window background, which matches the theme, while the
56:         // stores are read off the main thread.
57:         LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
58:             val signedIn = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { Session(appContext).isSignedIn() }
59:             backStack.add(if (signedIn) Destination.HOME else Destination.SIGN_IN)
60:         }
61:         return
62:     }
63:     BackHandler(enabled = backStack.size > 1) { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) }
64:     val destination = backStack.last()
65:     Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
66:         Screen(destination, backStack, signInModel, themeSettings, reports)
67:         if (destination != Destination.SIGN_IN) SessionTrustPrompts(sessionTrust, modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter))
68:     }
69: }
70: 
71: @Composable
72: private fun Screen(
73:     destination: Destination,
74:     backStack: SnapshotStateList<Destination>,
75:     signInModel: SignInModel,
76:     themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel,
77:     reports: ReportsModel,
78: ) {
79:     when (destination) {
80:         Destination.SIGN_IN -> SignInScreen(
81:             model = signInModel,
82:             onSignedIn = {
83:                 signInModel.reset()
84:                 backStack.clear()
85:                 backStack.add(Destination.HOME)
86:             },
87:         )
88:         Destination.HOME -> LaunchScreen(
89:             onOpenReports = { backStack.add(Destination.REPORTS) },
90:             onOpenSettings = { backStack.add(Destination.SETTINGS) },
91:             onOpenLicences = { backStack.add(Destination.LICENCES) },
92:         )
93:         Destination.SETTINGS -> SettingsScreen(
94:             model = themeSettings,
95:             onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) },
96:             onOpenLicences = { backStack.add(Destination.LICENCES) },
97:         )
98:         Destination.LICENCES -> LicencesScreen(onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) })
99:         Destination.REPORTS -> ReportsScreen(model = reports, onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) })
100:     }
101: }
102: 
103: /** The prompt for a refused request, or after a cancel the notice that reopens it. [modifier] places the notice. */
104: @Composable
105: private fun SessionTrustPrompts(sessionTrust: SessionTrustModel, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
106:     val state by sessionTrust.state.collectAsState()
107:     val refusal = state.refusal ?: return
108:     if (state.promptOpen && refusal is ApiError.TrustQuestion) {
109:         TrustPrompt(refusal, onAccept = sessionTrust::accept, onCancel = sessionTrust::cancel)
110:     } else {
111:         TrustRefusedNotice(refusal, onReview = sessionTrust::reopen, modifier = modifier)
112:     }
113: }
114: 
115: private val BackStackSaver = listSaver<SnapshotStateList<Destination>, String>(
116:     save = { stack -> stack.map { it.name } },
117:     restore = { names -> names.map(Destination::valueOf).toMutableStateList() },
118: )

export function serializeClient(row: ClientRow) {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    wid: row.workspace_id,
    archived: asBool(row.archived),
    name: row.name,
    at: togglTime(row.updated_at),
    notes: row.notes,
    creator_id: null,
    integration_provider: null,
    permissions: null,
  };
}

    project_id: row.project_id,

    server_deleted_at: null,

    project_is_private: asBool(row.project_is_private),

47: function boundary(value: string, field: string, user: UserRow): Date {
48:   const parsed = parseDateOrInstant(value, field);
49:   return 'instant' in parsed ? parsed.instant : startOfDay(parsed.date, user.timezone);
50: }
51: 
52: function changedSince(req: Request): string | undefined {
53:   const since = query(req, 'since');
54:   if (since === undefined) return undefined;
55:   const date = new Date(Number(since) * 1000);
56:   if (!/^\d+$/.test(since) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
57:     throw badRequest('Since is expected to be an unix timestamp, integer value');
58:   }
59:   return date.toISOString();
60: }
61: 
62: function modifiedSince<T extends { updated_at: string }>(rows: T[], since: string | undefined): T[] {
63:   return since === undefined ? rows : rows.filter((row) => row.updated_at >= since);
64: }
65: 
66: function timeEntryFilter(req: Request, user: UserRow): TimeEntryFilter {
67:   const since = changedSince(req);
68:   const before = query(req, 'before');
69:   const startDate = query(req, 'start_date');
70:   const endDate = query(req, 'end_date');
71:   const filter: TimeEntryFilter = {};
72: 
73:   if (since !== undefined) filter.changedSince = new Date(since);
74:   if ((startDate === undefined) !== (endDate === undefined)) {
75:     throw badRequest('start_date and end_date must be used together');
76:   }
77:   if (startDate !== undefined && endDate !== undefined) {
78:     filter.from = boundary(startDate, 'start_date', user);
79:     filter.to = boundary(endDate, 'end_date', user);
80:     if (filter.to < filter.from) throw badRequest('end_date must not be earlier than start_date');
81:   }
82:   if (before !== undefined) filter.to = boundary(before, 'before', user);
83:   if (!filter.changedSince && !filter.from && !filter.to) {
84:     filter.from = new Date(Date.now() - DEFAULT_LIST_DAYS * 86_400_000);
85:   }
86:   return filter;
87: }
88: 
89: function projectFilter(req: Request): ProjectFilter {
90:   const active = query(req, 'active');
91:   const billable = query(req, 'billable');
92:   const clientIds = query(req, 'client_ids');
93:   return {
94:     active: active === 'both' ? 'both' : active === 'false' ? false : active === 'true' ? true : 'both',
95:     billable: billable === undefined ? undefined : billable === 'true',
96:     name: query(req, 'name') || query(req, 'search') || undefined,
97:     clientIds: clientIds ? parseIdList(clientIds, 1000) : undefined,
98:     sinceIso: changedSince(req),
99:   };
100: }

102: /**
103:  * Applies Toggl's page/per_page parameters when a client sends them. Without them the whole list is
104:  * returned; Toggl pages by default, but one person's projects and tags fit in a single response.
105:  */
106: function paginate<T>(req: Request, items: T[], defaultPerPage: number): T[] {
107:   const page = query(req, 'page');
108:   const perPageParam = query(req, 'per_page');
109:   if (page === undefined && perPageParam === undefined) return items;
110:   const perPage = Math.min(Math.max(Number(perPageParam ?? defaultPerPage) || defaultPerPage, 1), 200);
111:   const pageNumber = Math.max(Number(page ?? 1) || 1, 1);
112:   return items.slice((pageNumber - 1) * perPage, pageNumber * perPage);
113: }

139: function readTagIds(db: Db, wid: number, body: Record<string, unknown>): number[] | undefined {
140:   let ids: number[] | undefined;
141:   if (body.tag_ids !== undefined && body.tag_ids !== null) {
142:     if (!Array.isArray(body.tag_ids)) throw badRequest('Invalid tag_ids: expected an array');
143:     ids = body.tag_ids.map((value) => {
144:       const id = optionalId(value, 'tag_ids');
145:       if (!id) throw badRequest('Invalid tag_ids');
146:       return getTag(db, wid, id).id;
147:     });
148:   }
149:   if (body.tags !== undefined && body.tags !== null) {
150:     if (!Array.isArray(body.tags) || body.tags.some((tag) => typeof tag !== 'string')) {
151:       throw badRequest('Invalid tags: expected an array of strings');
152:     }
153:     ids = [...(ids ?? []), ...resolveTagNames(db, wid, body.tags as string[])];
154:   }
155:   return ids === undefined ? undefined : [...new Set(ids)];
156: }
157: 
158: function applyTagAction(current: number[], incoming: number[] | undefined, action: unknown): number[] {
159:   if (incoming === undefined) return current;
160:   if (action === 'add') return [...new Set([...current, ...incoming])];
161:   if (action === 'delete' || action === 'remove') return current.filter((id) => !incoming.includes(id));
162:   return incoming;
163: }
164: 
165: function readDuration(value: unknown): number | undefined {
166:   if (value === undefined || value === null) return undefined;
167:   if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw badRequest('Invalid duration: expected an integer');
168:   return Math.trunc(value);
169: }
170: 
171: function readStart(body: Record<string, unknown>, fallback: Date): Date {
172:   const value = body.start ?? fallback.toISOString();
173:   const start = parseInstant(value, 'start');
174:   if (body.start_date === undefined || body.start_date === null) return start;
175:   if (typeof body.start_date !== 'string' || !parseDateKey(body.start_date)) {
176:     throw badRequest('Invalid start_date: expected YYYY-MM-DD');
177:   }
178:   return parseInstant(`${body.start_date}${(value as string).slice(10)}`, 'start');
179: }
180: 
181: function assertConsistentDuration(start: Date, stop: Date | null, duration: number | undefined, body: Record<string, unknown>): void {
182:   if (body.stop != null && stop && duration !== undefined && seconds(stop) - seconds(start) !== duration) {
183:     throw badRequest('Stop and duration must be consistent with start');
184:   }
185: }
186: 
187: function resolveProjectAndTask(
188:   db: Db, wid: number, body: Record<string, unknown>, current: { projectId: number | null; taskId: number | null },
189: ): { projectId: number | null; taskId: number | null; project: ProjectRef | null; projectChanged: boolean } {
190:   const projectInput = optionalId(body.project_id !== undefined ? body.project_id : body.pid, 'project_id');
191:   const taskInput = optionalId(body.task_id !== undefined ? body.task_id : body.tid, 'task_id');
192:   let projectId = projectInput === undefined ? current.projectId : projectInput;
193:   let taskId = taskInput === undefined ? current.taskId : taskInput;
194:   if (projectInput !== undefined && taskInput === undefined && projectInput !== current.projectId) taskId = null;
195: 
196:   if (taskId !== null) {
197:     const task = getTaskRef(db, wid, taskId);
198:     if (projectId === null) projectId = task.project_id;
199:     else if (task.project_id !== projectId) throw badRequest('Task does not belong to the project');
200:   }
201:   const project = projectId === null ? null : getProjectRef(db, wid, projectId);
202:   return { projectId, taskId, project, projectChanged: projectInput !== undefined && projectInput !== current.projectId };
203: }
204: 
205: function assertWorkspace(body: Record<string, unknown>, wid: number): void {
206:   const bodyWid = optionalId(body.workspace_id ?? body.wid, 'workspace_id');
207:   if (bodyWid && bodyWid !== wid) throw badRequest('Workspace ID in the body does not match the URL');
208: }

18: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.licences.LicencesScreen
19: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports.ReportsModel
20: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.reports.ReportsScreen
21: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.Session
22: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings.SettingsScreen
23: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings.ThemeSettingsModel
24: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.signin.SignInModel
25: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.signin.SignInScreen
26: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.trust.SessionTrustModel
27: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.trust.TrustPrompt
28: import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.trust.TrustRefusedNotice
29: import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
30: import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
31: 
32: /** The app's screens. */
33: enum class Destination { SIGN_IN, HOME, SETTINGS, LICENCES, REPORTS }
34: 
35: /**
36:  * The app's navigation host: a back stack of [Destination]s that survives
37:  * configuration changes and process death. It starts at sign-in until a
38:  * server and its token are stored, and at the home screen after that.
39:  * System back pops the stack and closes the app from its first screen.
40:  *
41:  * Above any screen of a signed-in session it shows the prompt for a request
42:  * that [sessionTrust] saw refused, and after a cancel a notice that opens the
43:  * prompt again. Sign-in shows its own prompts.
44:  */
45: @Composable
46: fun TokklNavHost(
47:     signInModel: SignInModel,
48:     themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel,
49:     sessionTrust: SessionTrustModel,
50:     reports: ReportsModel,
51: ) {
52:     val appContext = LocalContext.current.applicationContext
53:     val backStack = rememberSaveable(saver = BackStackSaver) { SnapshotStateList<Destination>() }
54:     if (backStack.isEmpty()) {
55:         // Shows the window background, which matches the theme, while the
56:         // stores are read off the main thread.
57:         LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
58:             val signedIn = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { Session(appContext).isSignedIn() }
59:             backStack.add(if (signedIn) Destination.HOME else Destination.SIGN_IN)
60:         }
61:         return
62:     }
63:     BackHandler(enabled = backStack.size > 1) { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) }
64:     val destination = backStack.last()
65:     Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
66:         Screen(destination, backStack, signInModel, themeSettings, reports)
67:         if (destination != Destination.SIGN_IN) SessionTrustPrompts(sessionTrust, modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomCenter))
68:     }
69: }
70: 
71: @Composable
72: private fun Screen(
73:     destination: Destination,
74:     backStack: SnapshotStateList<Destination>,
75:     signInModel: SignInModel,
76:     themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel,
77:     reports: ReportsModel,
78: ) {
79:     when (destination) {
80:         Destination.SIGN_IN -> SignInScreen(
81:             model = signInModel,
82:             onSignedIn = {
83:                 signInModel.reset()
84:                 backStack.clear()
85:                 backStack.add(Destination.HOME)
86:             },
87:         )
88:         Destination.HOME -> LaunchScreen(
89:             onOpenReports = { backStack.add(Destination.REPORTS) },
90:             onOpenSettings = { backStack.add(Destination.SETTINGS) },
91:             onOpenLicences = { backStack.add(Destination.LICENCES) },
92:         )
93:         Destination.SETTINGS -> SettingsScreen(
94:             model = themeSettings,
95:             onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) },
96:             onOpenLicences = { backStack.add(Destination.LICENCES) },
97:         )
98:         Destination.LICENCES -> LicencesScreen(onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) })
99:         Destination.REPORTS -> ReportsScreen(model = reports, onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) })
100:     }
101: }
102: 
103: /** The prompt for a refused request, or after a cancel the notice that reopens it. [modifier] places the notice. */
104: @Composable
105: private fun SessionTrustPrompts(sessionTrust: SessionTrustModel, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
106:     val state by sessionTrust.state.collectAsState()
107:     val refusal = state.refusal ?: return
108:     if (state.promptOpen && refusal is ApiError.TrustQuestion) {
109:         TrustPrompt(refusal, onAccept = sessionTrust::accept, onCancel = sessionTrust::cancel)
110:     } else {
111:         TrustRefusedNotice(refusal, onReview = sessionTrust::reopen, modifier = modifier)
112:     }
113: }
114: 
115: private val BackStackSaver = listSaver<SnapshotStateList<Destination>, String>(
116:     save = { stack -> stack.map { it.name } },
117:     restore = { names -> names.map(Destination::valueOf).toMutableStateList() },
118: )

23: class TokklApplication : Application() {
24: 
25:     /**
26:      * Loaded on first use, which is MainActivity's onCreate, on the main thread. The first frame
27:      * has to know the theme, or launch would show the other mode's colours first. Android learns
28:      * the chosen mode too, for the starting window of the next launch.
29:      */
30:     val themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel by lazy {
31:         val store = ThemeSettingsStore(this)
32:         val scope = MainScope()
33:         ThemeSettingsModel(initial = store.load(), save = store::save, scope = scope, io = Dispatchers.IO).also { model ->
34:             syncApplicationNightMode(this, model.state.map { it.mode }, scope, Dispatchers.IO)
35:         }
36:     }
37: 
38:     /**
39:      * Loaded on first use, which reads the stored settings from disk. That is usually
40:      * MainActivity's onStart on the main thread, and the file holds four short values. Each
41:      * refresh refreshes the [capabilities] and the [cache] too, with the same `GET /api/v9/me`,
42:      * and the cache takes the favorites from the settings' `GET /api/v9/me/preferences`.
43:      */
44:     val accountSettings: AccountSettingsSource by lazy {
45:         val store = AccountSettingsStore(this)
46:         AccountSettingsSource(
47:             initial = store.load(),
48:             save = store::save,
49:             client = Session(this)::client,
50:             scope = MainScope(),
51:             io = Dispatchers.IO,
52:             onRefresh = { api, me, preferences -> cache.refresh(api, me, preferences, capabilities.refresh(api, me)) },
53:         )
54:     }
55: 
56:     /** What the server offers. Loaded on first use from a file of four flags. */
57:     val capabilities: CapabilitiesSource by lazy {
58:         val store = CapabilitiesStore(this)
59:         CapabilitiesSource(initial = store.load(), save = store::save)
60:     }
61: 
62:     /** The offline cache. Its database file opens on the first query, which Room keeps off the main thread. */
63:     val cache: AccountCache by lazy { AccountCache(CacheDatabase.open(this)) }
64: 
65:     /**
66:      * The trust refusals of the signed-in session's requests, which every
67:      * [Session] reports and the navigation host prompts about. Accepting one
68:      * refreshes the account settings, the request that ran into it.
69:      */
70:     val sessionTrust: SessionTrustModel by lazy {
71:         SessionTrustModel(
72:             trust = ServerTrustStore(this),
73:             retry = { accountSettings.refresh() },
74:             scope = MainScope(),
75:             io = Dispatchers.IO,
76:         )
77:     }
78: 
79:     override fun onCreate() {
80:         super.onCreate()
81:         // Every process, whatever starts it, sends requests only to the stored server.
82:         ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom(ServerConfigStore(this)::load)
83:         // A plain http:// server gets requests only once the user has confirmed it.
84:         ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(ServerTrustStore(this))
85:     }
86: }

94:      * change keeps the report it shows instead of asking the server again.

104:     }

110:     }
111:

113:     fun shift(direction: Int) {
114:         val current = selection() ?: return
115:         load(RangeSelection(current.range.shift(direction), preset = null))
116:     }
117:

120:         val current = selection()
121:         if (current == null) select(RangePreset.THIS_WEEK) else load(current)
122:     }

127:         loading = null

130:     }

143:         loading?.cancel()

146:         loading = scope.launch {

 succeeded in 0ms:
---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

app/src/main/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/settings/ApplicationNightMode.kt
28:  * The setting stays the source of truth, so this runs on every start too and repairs a night mode
29:  * that went out of step. Each call is a binder call, so it runs on [io]. MainActivity handles the
30:  * configuration change itself, so a change keeps the activity and the screen it shows.
31:  *
32:  * Android added the app's night mode in 12 (API 31). Below that the starting window follows the
33:  * system's mode and this does nothing.
34:  */
35: fun syncApplicationNightMode(context: Context, modes: Flow<ThemeMode>, scope: CoroutineScope, io: CoroutineContext) {
36:     if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.S) return
37:     val uiModeManager = context.getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java)
38:     modes.distinctUntilChanged()
39:         .onEach { uiModeManager.setApplicationNightMode(it.applicationNightMode) }

app/src/test/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/ClassReferences.kt
19:     data class Member(val owner: String, val name: String, val descriptor: String) {
20:         override fun toString(): String = "$owner.$name$descriptor"
21:     }
22: 
23:     /** The classes and members that one class file refers to. */
24:     class References(val classes: Set<String>, val members: Set<Member>) {
29: 
30:     /** Every compiled app class, by name such as `com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/MainActivity`. */
31:     val app: Map<String, References> by lazy {
32:         read(SharedHttpClient::class.java).filterKeys { it.startsWith(APP_PACKAGE) }
33:     }
34: 
35:     /** Every class in the directory or jar that [type] was loaded from. */
36:     fun read(type: Class<*>): Map<String, References> {
37:         val location = File(type.protectionDomain!!.codeSource.location.toURI())
38:         val classFiles: Map<String, ByteArray> = if (location.isDirectory) {
39:             location.walkTopDown()
40:                 .filter { it.isFile && it.name.endsWith(".class") }
51: 
52:     private fun references(bytes: ByteArray): References {
53:         val input = DataInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes))
54:         check(input.readInt() == CLASS_MAGIC) { "not a class file" }
55:         input.readUnsignedShort()
56:         input.readUnsignedShort()
app/src/test/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/api/SingleHttpClientTest.kt
16:     @Test
17:     fun noAppClassButSharedHttpClientBuildsAnHttpClient() {
18:         assertTrue("the scan found the app's classes", "com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/MainActivity" in classes)
19: 
20:         val offenders = classes
21:             .filterKeys { !isSharedHttpClient(it) }
22:             .flatMap { (name, references) -> references.filter(::isForbidden).map { "$name uses $it" } }
28:     @Test
29:     fun scanSeesTheSharedClientBeingBuilt() {
30:         val shared = classes.filterKeys(::isSharedHttpClient).values.flatten()
31: 
32:         assertTrue(shared.any { it == "okhttp3/OkHttpClient\$Builder.<init>" && isForbidden(it) })
33:     }
34: 
35:     private fun isSharedHttpClient(className: String): Boolean =
36:         className == SHARED || className.startsWith("$SHARED\$")
37: 
38:     private fun isForbidden(reference: String): Boolean =
39:         FORBIDDEN.any { prefix ->
40:             reference == prefix ||
41:                 (prefix.endsWith("/") && reference.startsWith(prefix)) ||
42:                 reference.startsWith("$prefix.")
app/src/androidTest/java/com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/licences/LicencesScreenTest.kt
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.licences

import android.view.KeyEvent
import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.SemanticsProperties
import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsDisplayed
import androidx.compose.ui.test.hasText
import androidx.compose.ui.test.isHeading
import androidx.compose.ui.test.junit4.v2.createAndroidComposeRule
import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithContentDescription
import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithText
import androidx.compose.ui.test.performClick
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.MainActivity
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.R
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.SessionRule
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.awaitText
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test

/** The Licences screen, reached from the launch screen of the real activity. */
class LicencesScreenTest {
    @get:Rule(order = 0)
    val session = SessionRule()

    @get:Rule(order = 1)
    val composeRule = createAndroidComposeRule<MainActivity>()

    @Test
    fun launchScreenEntryOpensTheFullOflText() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.licence_anybody_title)).assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.licence_anybody_licence)).assertIsDisplayed()
        val shown = composeRule.onNode(hasText(OFL_TITLE, substring = true))
            .assertIsDisplayed()
            .fetchSemanticsNode().config[SemanticsProperties.Text]
            .joinToString("") { it.text }
        val shipped = composeRule.activity.resources.openRawResource(R.raw.anybody_ofl)
            .bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        // lines() splits at CRLF and LF alike, so this compares content, not line ends.
        assertEquals(shipped.lines(), shown.lines())
    }

    @Test
    fun backButtonReturnsToTheLaunchScreen() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.onNodeWithContentDescription(string(R.string.back)).performClick()

        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.launch_message)).assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    /** System back goes to the launch screen instead of closing the app. */
    @Test
    fun systemBackReturnsToTheLaunchScreen() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.waitUntil("the app window has input focus", FOCUS_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            var focused = false
            composeRule.activityRule.scenario.onActivity { focused = it.hasWindowFocus() }
            focused
        }
        InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().sendKeyDownUpSync(KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BACK)

        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.launch_message)).assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    /** A rotation, theme or font change recreates the activity. */
    @Test
    fun licencesStayOpenWhenTheActivityIsRecreated() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.activityRule.scenario.recreate()

        licencesHeading().assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    private fun openLicences() {
        composeRule.awaitText(string(R.string.licences))
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.licences)).performClick()
        licencesHeading().assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    private fun licencesHeading() = composeRule.onNode(hasText(string(R.string.licences)) and isHeading())

    private fun string(@StringRes id: Int): String = composeRule.activity.getString(id)

    private companion object {
        const val OFL_TITLE = "SIL OPEN FONT LICENSE Version 1.1"
        const val FOCUS_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000L
    }
}


exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 -B - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
